### [1] [Uncovering the Blind Spots of OKS-mAP: A Unified Protocol for Occlusion, Joint-Angle, and Temporal Evaluation of 2D Pose Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12911)
*Yifan Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了评估2D人体姿态估计的新指标，改进了OKS-mAP存在的盲点，包括地面真值可见性分层、关节角度误差和帧间位移等模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的OKS-mAP指标存在三个不足，研究为了更准确地评估各模型的性能，提出了一项补充协议，该协议包含三个模块：按可见性分层的OKS-mAP、关节角度误差及其一致性、以及帧间位移。

Method: 研究者在四个数据集上对四种模型进行了评估，每种模型来自两种架构族（YOLO11s-pose 和 ViTPose-B/L），采用无检测器的公开协议进行测试。

Result: 结果发现集合mAP高估了遮挡场景的表现，关节角度误差在遮挡层被放大，角度评估指标能捕捉mAP无法定位的差异。在PoseTrack18测试集上，两个YOLO模型的原始抖动一致，像素域OneEuro平滑降低了9-10%的跳跃率仅轻微影响匹配目标精度，Transformer模型的跳跃率显著较低。分层mAP与全mAP的相关性良好，而角度差分向量则部分发散，表明具有独立信息。

Conclusion: 该研究通过开放的协议和工具提供了更全面的性能评估方法，补充而非替代现有的OKS-mAP指标。

Abstract: The dominant metric for 2D human pose estimation, OKS-mAP, has three blind spots: it does not distinguish visible from occluded keypoints, does not measure joint-angle error, and is silent on the temporal stability of tracking-free per-frame inference. We propose a supplementary protocol with three modules: (M1) OKS-mAP stratified by ground-truth visibility, with Delta_occ = mAP(v=1) - mAP(full), quantifying how much the aggregate metric masks occlusion-specific differences; (M2) joint-angle error (RMSE/MAE/P95) plus knee-valgus detection consistency; (M3) frame-to-frame displacement, median-filter residual jitter, and jump rate, before and after OneEuro smoothing. We ran it on four datasets (COCO val2017, CrowdPose, OCHuman, PoseTrack18) and four models from two architecture families (YOLO11s-pose, an occlusion-domain fine-tuned variant, ViTPose-B/L), under a common detector-free oracle protocol and an open-source harness. Delta_occ is negative in every combination (-0.19 to -0.47): aggregate mAP overestimates occluded-scene performance in every tested combination. Joint-angle error is amplified in occluded layers; angle metrics reveal differences mAP cannot localize, e.g., the smaller occlusion-trained model significantly outperforming a larger transformer in occluded elbows. On PoseTrack18 val, raw jitter matched between the two YOLO models; pixel-domain OneEuro smoothing cut jump rates 9-10% at no matched-scope AP cost; residual jitter persists after detector removal; the transformer pair's jump rate is significantly lower; smoothing benefits replicate across architectures. Stratified mAP tracks full mAP closely (sign-consistency 1.0, 12/12), serving magnitude diagnosis rather than re-ranking; angle-metric difference vectors (especially knee) partially diverge, suggesting independent information. The protocol and harness are open-sourced, complementing rather than replacing OKS-mAP.

</details>


### [2] [URL-Sim: Unified and Regulated LiDAR Simulation for High-Fidelity Reconstruction of Dynamic Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12932)
*Kefei Qian,Xipeng Feng,Jie Li,Long Cao,Yuhan Chen,Xiangfei Huang,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: URL-Sim 提出了一种新的用于自主驾驶系统安全验证的动态 LiDAR 模拟框架，能够高效且准确地重建异构动态物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有基于神经辐射场 (NeRF) 的 LiDAR 模拟器渲染速度慢和基于 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) 的方法在处理动态物体时存在的局限性，提出了 URL-Sim 以提高模拟效率和准确度。

Method: URL-Sim 构建了一个带有多维度重要性评分的动态高斯场景图，细化策略将高斯函数分配到最关键区域，同时维持固定的原始对象数量。

Result: 在 Waymo 和 KITTI-360 数据集上的实验表明，URL-Sim 在与现有基线相当的原始预算下实现了更高的定量准确性和视觉质量，并准确地恢复了多种动态交通参与者的几何形状和姿态。

Conclusion: URL-Sim 代表了一种新的动态 LiDAR 模拟方法，适用于复杂的真实交通场景，并可能促进成本效益的安全验证。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity LiDAR simulation scenes from real-world driving logs is essential for cost-effective safety validation of autonomous driving systems. While simulators based on Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) achieve realistic scene reconstruction, their large-scale adoption is limited by slow rendering speeds. Recent 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) approaches have improved rendering efficiency through explicit primitive representation and differentiable rasterization. However, existing 3DGS-based methods remain largely restricted to rigid vehicles and produce redundant primitives through unconstrained densification. To address these limitations, we propose URL-Sim, a unified and regulated dynamic LiDAR simulation framework. URL-Sim constructs a dynamic Gaussian scene graph that decouples the modeling of traffic participants, including vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists, enabling accurate reconstruction of heterogeneous dynamic objects within a single framework. It further introduces a regulated Gaussian densification strategy that ranks primitives with multi-dimensional importance scores and allocates Gaussians to the most critical regions while keeping the total primitive count bounded. Experiments on the Waymo and KITTI-360 datasets demonstrate that URL-Sim achieves higher quantitative accuracy and visual quality than existing baselines under comparable primitive budgets, and accurately recovers the geometry and poses of diverse dynamic traffic participants.

</details>


### [3] [Evaluation protocols for historical script classification: the cost of manuscript-level leakage and the role of soft labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12969)
*Daniil I. Naryzhnyi,Roman A. Popkov,Daniil A. Bakalin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究者测试了一个脚本类型分类器在未知手稿中的表现，发现模型在处理整个手稿和多个页面时表现出明显的准确性下降，特别是在未见过的手稿上，模型过于自信，ECE指标显示模型信心与准确性不符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估脚本类型分类器在未知手稿中的普适性和准确性，研究者对HEbrew手稿进行了测试。

Method: 研究者使用了一个脚本类型分类器，分别在VM-L-HP-ext数据集的不同分割方式（按页面分割和按手稿分割）进行了测试，并调整了模型的平滑度、贴现标签和温度缩放来试图提高其准确性。

Result: 当按照页面分割时，模型的准确性从97%下降到54%，而在按手稿分割时则下降到59%。在新未知手稿上，模型表现进一步下降，准确率为39%。此外，模型对自己的预测过于自信，尤其是在页面级别的ECE指标上表现明显，这表明模型预测和实际不符。通过调整模型参数，如引入贴现标签和平滑度，可以稍微改进精度，但没有明显提升。

Conclusion: 研究发现脚本类型分类器在新未见过的手稿上表现较差，尽管一些干预措施（如贴现标签和平滑度）能小幅改进准确度，但在新手稿上的过拟合问题难以完全解决。

Abstract: A paleographer applies a script type classifier to unseen manuscripts. We measure how much testing on other pages of training manuscripts inflates accuracy. On VML-HP-ext (715 pages, 171 Hebrew manuscripts, 14 script classes) we compare page-level and manuscript-level splits. Page-level accuracy drops from 97% to 54% (59% at manuscript level), and is 39% on the official blind test set. The gap of 40 to 47 percentage points (pp) at patch, page and manuscript level is statistically significant and reproduces with ResNet18; on binarized patches (ink and background only) it is 34 to 42 pp, also significant. On unseen manuscripts the model is overconfident: mean confidence 70% against 54% correct pages; patch-level expected calibration error (ECE, mean gap between confidence and accuracy) rises from 0.065 to 0.444. Binarization leaves patch-level overconfidence but cuts it markedly at page level. Confidence still helps triage: in the more confident half of pages 67% are correct, 41% in the rest. Training on expert soft labels showed no evidence of an accuracy difference, though a small page-level loss cannot be ruled out. The predicted distributions are closer to the expert's assessment. ECE is lower at patch level (0.21 to 0.31) and in most runs at page level, but not significantly at manuscript level. Label smoothing (blurring the label, no expert input) also lowers ECE but comes no closer to the expert. Temperature scaling (post-training confidence correction) helps significantly only at patch level. On 97 pages of 16 manuscripts with mixed expert assessment, soft labels cut divergence from the expert by 22 to 30% (significant in four of five runs); the point estimate of accuracy is 16.5 to 23.7 pp below hard labels, but with 16 manuscripts the loss can neither be confirmed nor ruled out. We propose recommendations for the evaluation protocol; code will be released with the paper

</details>


### [4] [Attribute-Evidence Learning with Multimodal Prompt for Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12975)
*Wei Zhang,Zhu Teng,Zhixiang Chi,Junnan Zou,Wenxin Luo,Linfeng Ye,Jianping Fan,Konstantions N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AptM-GCD提出了一种新的多模态框架，通过属性证据学习和多模态提示推理，利用局部属性证据并将其与全局视觉理解相结合，以发现未标记的数据中的新类别，表现出优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖视觉线索，容易忽略人类感知的多模态性质，导致全局标签和局部属性之间的错位，影响了模型的准确性和泛化能力。AptM-GCD通过利用属性作为本地视觉证据来解决这一问题。

Method: AptM-GCD框架包含两种机制：1. 属性证据学习（AEL）：根据属性嵌入与视觉补丁级别的对应关系，辨识可靠属性相关区域并提取区分性局部证据，减少噪声或无关属性提示的影响；2. 多模态提示学习（MPL）：将细粒度的文本证据转换为视觉提示，不强制要求模态间的直接全球对齐，通过上下文推理增强类别区分。

Result: 在六组数据集上进行的实验表明，AptM-GCD在细粒度和通用基准测试中均实现了最先进的性能，展示了强大的泛化能力，在单模态和类别不平衡场景下也持续收益。

Conclusion: AptM-GCD通过创新的方法提高了发现未标记数据中新类别的能力，具有广泛的适用性和优越的性能表现。

Abstract: Generalized category discovery (GCD) aims to discover novel categories from unlabeled data by leveraging limited labeled samples from old classes. Existing methods that rely solely on visual cues often neglect the multimodal nature of human perception, prompting recent approaches to incorporate textual information such as category tags and attributes. However, these approaches typically entangle global tags and local attributes within a shared global image representation, rendering attributes unreliable and potentially providing misleading cues that can drive the model toward incorrect predictions. To this end, we propose AptM-GCD, a novel multimodal framework that exploits apt textual guidance through attribute evidence learning and multimodal prompt reasoning. Specifically, we design an Attribute-Evidence Learning (AEL) mechanism that treats attributes as localized visual evidence rather than global category descriptors. By interacting attribute embeddings with patch-level visual tokens, AEL identifies reliable attribute-related regions and extracts discriminative local evidence, thereby reducing the impact of noisy or irrelevant attribute cues. Building upon the extracted evidence, we further develop a Multimodal Prompt Learning (MPL) mechanism to bridge local attribute semantics and global visual understanding. MPL converts fine-grained textual evidence into visual prompts and injects them into a multimodal prompt transformer, enabling attribute information to enhance category discrimination through contextual reasoning without enforcing direct global alignment between modalities. Extensive experiments on six datasets demonstrate that AptM-GCD consistently achieves SoTA performance across both fine-grained and generic benchmarks. Moreover, the proposed framework exhibits strong generalization ability, delivering consistent gains under unimodal and class-imbalanced GCD settings.

</details>


### [5] [Partition, Refine, and Detect: Preserving Novel Category Capacity in Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12976)
*Wei Zhang,Zhu Teng,Zhixiang Chi,Junnan Zou,Yi Liu,Jianping Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PR-OVD 通过分区和细化框架增强了新型类别的表示能力，提升了开放词汇目标检测的性能，特别是在新型类别上取得了显著的6.5%平均提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在识别新型类别时容易出现语义坍缩和置信度校准问题，PR-OVD 建立分区和细化框架，为新型类别保留代表能力，并加强类别区分，以解决此问题。

Method: PR-OVD 使用粗粒度分区和细化机制，分别构建视觉和文本模式下的全局分区，减少无关嵌入泄漏，并通过类别级细化调整决策边界。此外，还设计了认知启发式的 Structure Prompt (SP)，整合了自我中心属性和客观上下文，以稳定地进行分区和细化学习。

Result: 在两个开放词汇目标检测基准上的实验表明，PR-OVD 相对于最先进的方法在新型类别上实现了平均6.5%的性能提升。此外，PR-OVD 还能够提升其他开放词汇任务，如分割和分类。

Conclusion: PR-OVD 框架有效地解决了开放词汇目标检测中的挑战，提高了新型类别的识别精度，并展示了其在多个开放词汇任务中的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) expects the model trained on base classes to recognize both base and novel classes at inference. Typical methods rely on CLIP-style vision-language embeddings, yet the learned space is often biased toward base categories, with limited effective capacity preserved for novel concepts. Such a base-dominated representation can compress novel semantics, leading to semantic collapse and mis-calibrated confidence at inference. To address this issue, we present PR-OVD, a partition-and-refine framework that explicitly reserves capacity for novel categories and strengthens category separation, while being seamlessly integrated into different detector architectures. Specifically, PR-OVD first performs coarse partitioning to build global divisions for base, novel, and an explicit background partition in both visual and textual modalities, preventing object-irrelevant embeddings from leaking into category-specific representations. It then applies category-level refinement within each partition to sharpen decision boundaries and alleviate the lack of fine-grained region-level pretraining in CLIP. To further provide reliable visual anchors for novel categories, we design a cognitively inspired Structure Prompt (SP) that encodes both egocentric attributes and allocentric context, facilitating stable partition-and-refine learning. Extensive experiments on two OVD benchmarks show that PR-OVD delivers an average 6.5\% proportional improvement on novel categories over the state-of-the-art methods. Notably, PR-OVD also benefits other open-vocabulary tasks, including segmentation and classification, further confirming its effectiveness and generality.

</details>


### [6] [Selective Surgical Action-Center Localization:Retaining Localization Gains through Supervised Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12977)
*Yanchong Xue,Wanting Zhang,Lingtao Yu,Guochao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种选择性定位管道，该管道结合了热图监督和坐标监督，并使用逻辑回归评分器来区分可报告中心和定位误差在指定容差范围内的预测。通过不同基线模型的比较，该方法在特定阈值下显著提高了正确输出的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前在对手术中的操作中心进行定位时，仍然存在准确参考点和是否报告的决策问题。特别是在不同来源的图像混合时，提高定位精度的效果取决于哪些正确的候选者能够幸存下来。

Method: 本文通过结合热图监督和坐标监督，结合逻辑回归评分器来识别可报告中心和具有特定误差范围的预测。并提出了不同的评价指标来评估和比较不同模型的效果。

Result: 在特定的阈值下（80%的接受率以及0.10倍于图像对角线的误差容差），本文提出的方法能够保留每100个输入中46.08个正确输出，而 baseline 方法则只能保留44.20个，表明该方法有1.88的增益（95%置信区间为0.94到2.79）。

Conclusion: 该研究指出，在选择性定位中，基于热图和坐标监督的方法可以更有效地提高识别正确中心的准确性，并减少正确的预测被误判的情况。

Abstract: Surgical action-center localization requires both an accurate reference point and a decision about whether to report it. When frames with and without a reportable center are mixed, the benefit of improved localization depends on which correct candidates survive selection. We present a selective localization pipeline combining heatmap and coordinate supervision with a logistic-regression scorer. The scorer learns to identify predictions with a reportable center and a localization error within a specified tolerance. The internal evaluation uses 5,000 frames from 50 surgical sources, five source-grouped folds and two random seeds. Matched locator and scoring comparisons track correct candidates and their rejection. At an 80% acceptance quota per source and a tolerance of 0.10 times the image diagonal, our method retains 46.08 correct outputs per 100 inputs. The baseline locator with the same scoring approach retains 44.20. The gain is 1.88 (descriptive 95% source-paired bootstrap interval, 0.94 to 2.79). Under entropy-based selection, increased rejection of correct predictions offsets the higher supply of correct predictions on average. For the coordinate-supervised locator, supervised scoring reduces these rejections from 6.15 to 2.99 per 100 inputs. Evaluating retained correct outputs reveals whether a selection rule preserves or erodes an improvement in localization.

</details>


### [7] [Trajectory-Regularized Distribution Distillation for One-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12978)
*An Zhao,Yixiang Zhou,Zejian Li,Yi Guo,Pei Chen,Lingyun Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了结合分布级和轨迹级监督的轨迹正则化分布蒸馏(TRD)方法，该方法在SANA-1.6B和Stable Diffusion 3.5上表现出优于代表性的一致性基线和分布匹配基线的生成质量和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的方法在小采样预算下性能不佳，而直接优化生成分布的方法需要一个额外的网络来跟踪学生的更新得分。TRD通过单一的学生前向传递中的动量混合策略结合了分布级和轨迹级监督，解决了上述问题。

Method: TRD通过在一次学生前向传递过程中预测一个动量混合策略来同时作为单步生成器、学生边际速度估计器以及与教师ODE轨迹时间依赖速度场的监督器。

Result: TRD在SANA-1.6B和Stable Diffusion 3.5上的实验结果表明，它在生成质量和训练效率方面优于一致性基线和分布匹配基线，但样本多样性有所降低。

Conclusion: TRD提供了一种新的方式来整合分布级和轨迹级监督，为流匹配模型的一步生成提供了改进的方法。

Abstract: Distilling flow matching models for one-step generation generally follows two paradigms. Trajectory alignment supervises the student with the teacher's probability flow ODE, but its performance degrades under very small sampling budgets. Distribution matching directly optimizes the generated distribution, but estimating its reverse Kullback-Leibler gradient typically requires a separate network to track the evolving student score. We propose trajectory-regularized distribution distillation (TRD), which combines distribution-level and trajectory-level supervision through a momentum-mixture policy predicted in a single student forward pass. This policy serves simultaneously as a one-step generator, an estimator of the student's marginal velocity that removes the need for a second trainable network in distribution matching, and a closed-form time-dependent velocity field supervised along the teacher ODE trajectory. Experiments on SANA-1.6B and Stable Diffusion 3.5 show that TRD outperforms representative consistency and distribution matching baselines in generation quality and training efficiency for both one-step and few-step generation, with a trade-off in sample diversity.

</details>


### [8] [Evidence-Grounded Iterative Reasoning for the Diagnosis of Diffuse Cystic Lung Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12981)
*Haoqing Li,Jun Shi,Tou Li,Xianmeng Chen,Rui Wang,Yating Peng,Qiwei Jia,Li Gao,Qianyi Chen,Hong An,Ruoyun Ouyang,Xiaowen Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DCLDs-EIR 提出了一种集诊断假设修订与多模态证据收集验证于一体的证据导向迭代推理框架，通过多视图肺和囊肿分割渲染及与临床信息的结合进行诊断假设的初始鉴别，利用竞争假设指导轴向CT检查识别局部发现，并与特定疾病知识图谱检索关联，通过逐步评估患者观察和知识来修订诊断状态和形成反馈循环，多中心评估表明 DCLDs-EIR 在提高诊断准确性和实现可追溯差诊断方面优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的影像学表现重叠使得 DCLDs 的鉴别诊断复杂化，而有限的标注数据限制了监督模型的开发。尽管多模态大型语言模型能够整合影像和临床信息，但其诊断推理仍受限于不完整的三维感知及缺乏疾病特定证据。

Method: DCLDs-EIR 采用了证据导向的迭代推理框架，通过多视图肺和囊肿分割渲染识别全局囊肿分布，初始化鉴别诊断。采用竞争性假设指导轴向CT检查，识别局部发现，结合临床背景检索特定疾病知识图谱，评估患者观察和知识以修订诊断状态。

Result: 多中心评估表明，DCLDs-EIR 在诊断准确性上优于基准方法，且能够通过明确诊断假设、患者观察和特定疾病知识之间的连接实现可追溯的鉴别诊断。

Conclusion: DCLDs-EIR 提高了 DCLDs 的诊断准确性，并通过结合影像和临床信息实现了透明和可追溯的鉴别诊断过程。

Abstract: Overlapping imaging manifestations complicate the differential diagnosis of Diffuse Cystic Lung Diseases (DCLDs), while limited annotated data constrain supervised model development. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can integrate imaging and clinical information, but their diagnostic reasoning remains limited by incomplete volumetric perception and insufficient grounding in disease-specific evidence. We propose DCLDs-EIR, an evidence-grounded iterative reasoning framework coupling diagnostic hypothesis refinement with multimodal evidence acquisition and verification. Multiview renderings of predicted lung and cyst segmentations characterize global cyst distributions and initialize the differential diagnosis. Competing hypotheses guide axial CT inspection to identify local findings that, together with clinical context, inform retrieval from a disease-specific knowledge graph. Accumulated patient observations and retrieved knowledge are jointly assessed for diagnostic support and refutation to revise the diagnostic state. Updated hypotheses and unresolved distinctions guide subsequent inspection, forming a feedback loop between evidence acquisition and hypothesis verification. Multicenter evaluation demonstrates that DCLDs-EIR consistently improves diagnostic accuracy over baselines and enables traceable differential diagnosis through explicit links between diagnostic hypotheses, patient observations, and disease-specific knowledge.

</details>


### [9] [TIB: Sample-wise Tempered Information Bottleneck for Multimodal Attribution beyond Alignment Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12997)
*Qiming Huang,Peixi Liu,Jianbo Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为TIB的自适应调节跨模态可靠性来调整归因强度的样本级Tempered信息瓶颈框架，从而解决了现有信息瓶颈方法在部分语义不一致情况下的解释过度收缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨了现有基于信息瓶颈的多模态归因方法在存在部分语义不一致性和不可靠跨模态对应关系的现实数据集中的局限性，并提出了TIB框架以提高多模态模型的解释能力。

Method: TIB框架通过在不同样本上自适应地调整信息瓶颈中的归因强度，来减少对所有代理监督信号的可靠性假设，适用于跨模态不一致的情况。

Result: 通过对控制基准和大规模数据集的广泛实验，证明了TIB框架的有效性，并验证了现有的基于信息瓶颈的归因方法存在失效模式。

Conclusion: 本文提出了TIB框架，该框架能够更好地适应多模态数据中的语义不一致性和跨模态对应关系的可靠性问题，从而提高了多模态模型的解释性和准确性。

Abstract: Multimodal (e.g. vision-language, in this work) attribution aims to interpret the models without access to ground-truth explanatory supervision. Existing multimodal attribution methods typically rely on paired modalities as proxy supervision, implicitly assuming that image-text pairs are semantically aligned. Information bottleneck (IB)-based attribution methods follow this general paradigm and instantiate it through a global sufficiency-compression objective. In realistic multimodal datasets, however, this assumption is frequently violated due to partial semantic misalignment and unreliable cross-modal correspondences. Through theoretical analysis, we show that, under such misalignment, information-bottleneck-based attribution objectives exhibit a structural limitation: by assuming equal reliability across all proxy supervision signals, they induce posterior over-contraction and forced explanations. To address this issue, we propose TIB, a sample-wise Tempered Information Bottleneck framework that adaptively modulates attribution strength according to cross-modal reliability, without assuming semantic alignment. Extensive experiments on controlled benchmarks and large-scale datasets show the effectiveness of the proposed approach, while also validating the identified failure mode.

</details>


### [10] [PhysMoE: Fast Spatial Grounding with Single-Token Coordinate Representation in Modality-Decoupled VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13000)
*Ruimeng Liu,Denan Liang,Ruofei Bai,Fuming Zhang,Kuan Xu,Shenghai Yuan,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysMoE 是一种新的物理模态基于的Vision-Language模型，通过共享底层、引入物理优先级和可微的Signed Distance Field（SDF）监督，解决了现有视觉-语言模型在连续性与解码延迟、多接触及不规则形状处理上的缺陷，提升了在9个基于坐标的任务中的性能和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的视觉-语言模型在执行坐标预测任务时，存在连续性丢失和解码延迟、难以处理多接触及不规则形状的问题。PhysMoE 提出了一种新的物理模态架构来解决这些问题，通过共享底层和引入物理优先级，提高了任务性能和效率。

Method: PhysMoE 引入了三层核心贡献：1）通过共享底层并利用物理模态路由上层，消除梯度干扰；2）将每个坐标编码为单个令牌，利用视觉编码器学习的绝对位置嵌入，注入2D空间先验，实现坐标与视觉特征的对齐；3）采用可微的Signed Distance Field（SDF）监督，适用于指向和不规则几何形状，无需昂贵的掩码解码器。

Result: PhysMoE 模型在9个坐标预测基准测试中显示出15.7%的平均性能提升和8.31倍的计算效率提升，同时保持了对11个基于文本的机器人理解任务的多模态理解和推理能力。

Conclusion: PhysMoE 提出的新架构在解决视觉-语言模型的现有瓶颈方面取得了显著进展，展示了在机器人规划与操作任务中实际应用的潜力。

Abstract: Embodied Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are backbones for robotic planning and manipulation. However, for coordinate prediction in grounding tasks, existing architectures suffer from two distinct bottlenecks: discrete tokenization disrupts spatial continuity with high decoding latency, while standard point regression fails on multi-contact affordances and irregularly shaped targets. To resolve these challenges, we propose PhysMoE, introducing three core contributions: (1) sharing lower layers for multimodal context while routing upper layers to specialized spatial (pixel) and linguistic (text) experts by physical modality, eliminating gradient interference; (2) encoding each coordinate as a single token by reusing the visual encoder's learned positional embeddings, injecting 2D spatial priors to natively align coordinates with visual features and ease training; and (3) differentiable Signed Distance Field (SDF) supervision on pointing, accommodating affordances and irregular geometries without costly mask decoders. Compared to the base model, PhysMoE achieves a 15.7% average gain and an 8.31x throughput speedup across 9 coordinate-based grounding benchmarks (trajectory, affordance, and localization), while preserving multimodal understanding and reasoning across 11 text-based embodied benchmarks.

</details>


### [11] [G-STAR: Group-Scaled Token Capacity for Deeply Compressed Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13004)
*Xu Huang,Ye Huang,Xiaotong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Jialv Zou,Minhao Jing,Haodong Li,Wenhao Yan,Yaxin Luo,Duomin Wang,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种空间-组群共缩放框架(GSTAR)，通过产品量化技术和高效的解码器设计，在保持高质量重建和生成性能的同时，实现了图像生成中高延迟的大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散自回归模型虽然能提供良好的图像生成框架，但在生成速度和信息容量之间存在固有的权衡。为了突破这一限制，研究者基于产品量化提出了GSTAR框架，以更高效地压缩模型并保持模型性能。

Method: GSTAR框架基于产品量化策略，考虑了固定信息预算下的组大小与轴向空间压缩因子的关系，并提出了一种半自回归解码器，结合并行特征计算和轻量级因果建模，以高效处理组内依赖。

Result: GSTAR展示了在保持高质量重建和生成性能的同时，能够在多个基准测试中实现高达64倍的空间压缩，且具有竞争力的模型性能。在1024x1024分辨率下，GSTAR实现了使用NVIDIA H200生成一张图像2.5秒的低延迟。

Conclusion: GSTAR框架证明了高度压缩的离散表示可以支持在低延迟条件下进行高质量的自回归图像生成。

Abstract: Discrete autoregressive models offer a scalable framework for image generation. However, they face a fundamental trade-off: long token sequences preserve representation quality but slow generation, while aggressive spatial compression reduces representation capacity. Based on product quantization, we show that, under a fixed nominal information budget, group size should scale quadratically with the per-axis spatial compression factor. Building on this principle, we propose GSTAR, a spatial--group co-scaling framework that enables \(32\times\) and even effective \(64\times\) compression while preserving strong reconstruction, understanding, and generation performance. Since larger groups introduce intra-group dependencies that must be modeled efficiently, we analyze their impact on inference latency and generation quality and introduce a semi-autoregressive decoder that combines parallel feature computation with lightweight causal modeling. With these designs, our highly compressed models perform competitively with leading models across five representative text-to-image benchmarks (GenEval, DPG-Bench, PRISM, LongText-Bench, and Qwen-Image-Bench). At \(1024\times1024\) resolution, our model delivers a \(20\times\) speedup over prior discrete autoregressive models, generating each image in 2.5 seconds on a single NVIDIA H200. Together, these results show that highly compressed discrete representations can enable high-quality autoregressive generation at low latency.

</details>


### [12] [Instance-preserving graded semantic reporting for surgical action triplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13009)
*Yanchong Xue,Wanting Zhang,Lingtao Yu,Guochao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了实例保留的分级语义报告方法，通过保留每个选定实例的预测掩码和标签，并使用层次正确性评分来指导仪器（I）、仪器动词（IV）或完整仪器动词目标（IVT）的报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决完全或拒绝报告手术动作三元组时可能会错误舍弃正确识别的器械和动作的问题。

Method: 引入了一种实例保留的分级语义报告方法，将实例选择与语义详细选择分离，使用层次正确性评分指导报告。应用实用增量规则（DELTA）权衡更详细报告的预期增益与错误成本。

Result: 在八段手术视频的5,641个测试帧中，与匹配的完全或拒绝基准相比，DELTA方法保留正确部分报告的比例为80.0%，增加IV能提升总体正确报告效益19.13到23.13每100帧，同时权重报告风险变化为-0.77到+1.01个百分点。

Conclusion: 这种分级报告方法提供了部分手术预测作为局部描述，明确了报告的实用性与完整性的权衡关系。

Abstract: Complete-or-reject reporting of surgical action triplets can discard a correctly identified instrument and action when the target is uncertain. We introduce instance-preserving graded semantic reporting, which separates instance selection from the choice of semantic detail. Each selected instance retains its predicted mask and labels while hierarchical correctness scores guide instrument (I), instrument-verb (IV), or complete instrument-verb-target (IVT) reporting. The utility-increment rule DELTA weighs the expected gain from finer reporting against its error cost. We compare three decision rules on 5,641 test frames from eight surgical videos using fixed predictions and scores. At the 10% development calibration target, DELTA retains correct partial reports for 80.0% of candidates rejected by a matched complete-or-reject baseline (5,329/6,662). Adding IV increases source-normalized weighted correct-report yield by 19.13 to 23.13 per 100 frames across all three rules relative to separately recalibrated I/IVT controls. Weighted reporting risk changes by -0.77 to +1.01 percentage points, with complete correct-triplet counts unchanged or reduced. Graded reporting makes partial surgical predictions available as localized descriptions, with an explicit trade-off between report utility and completeness.

</details>


### [13] [Preserving Cultural Records: A Large-Scale Benchmark and Visual-Prior Routing Network for Ancient Book Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13010)
*Shumin Fan,Tianyu Song,Hongming Chen,Xiaodong Zhou,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AncBench 提供了 10 万对对齐的干净-降级图像，模拟了复杂的退化模式，AncRestore 是一种退化感知的视觉先验路由网络，能够根据不同退化特征选择和整合合适的视觉先验，从而提高古书图像的恢复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的古籍图像修复基准规模有限且依赖于简化退化模拟，难以涵盖真实世界的退化模式，限制了修复模型的泛化能力。AncBench 和 AncRestore 的提出旨在解决这些问题， AncBench 通过综合退化管道提供了一种大规模的合成配对基准， AncRestore 则提出了一种基于视觉先验的动态路由机制，以更好地应对不同类型的退化。

Method: AncBench 构建了一个包含 100K 个配对的合成清洁-退化图像集。它利用一个综合降解管道，考虑了材料老化、纸张纹理变化、模糊、污渍、水引起的几何扭曲和局部遮挡等因素来模拟现实的综合退化模式。而 AncRestore 是一种降解感知的视觉先验路由网络，通过学习退化图像中的退化表示，并根据不同的退化特征动态选择和整合合适的视觉先验。

Result: 实验证明 AncRestore 能够有效地抑制复合退化，同时保留文本结构，提高修复质量。相对于其他方法， AncRestore 在古籍图像恢复方面显示出了应用潜力。

Conclusion: AncBench 和 AncRestore 的提出标志着古籍图像修复领域的进步，提供了一种更真实、更综合的退化模拟方案，并且通过有效的视觉先验路由策略提高了图像恢复的质量。

Abstract: Ancient books preserve invaluable cultural heritage, yet their images often suffer from complex degradations caused by long-term ageing, environmental exposure, and physical damage. Existing ancient book image restoration benchmarks are limited in scale and rely on simplified degradation simulations, which cannot fully represent real-world degradation patterns and restrict the generalization ability of restoration models. To address these challenges, we introduce AncBench, a large-scale synthetic paired benchmark containing 100K aligned clean-degraded image pairs. AncBench models realistic composite degradations through a comprehensive degradation pipeline that considers material ageing, paper texture variation, blur, staining, water-induced geometric distortion, and local occlusion. Furthermore, we propose AncRestore, a degradation-aware visual-prior routing network for ancient book image restoration. AncRestore first learns degradation representations from degraded images and then dynamically selects and integrates suitable visual priors according to different degradation characteristics. Guided by these degradation cues, a dynamic routing mechanism combines expert-wise and region-wise routing strategies adaptively adjusts the contribution of these priors across different regions, enabling the model to recover both global appearance and local text details. Extensive experiments show that AncRestore effectively suppresses composite degradations while preserving text structures and enhancing restoration quality against other methods, highlighting its application potential for intelligent ancient book image restoration in cultural heritage preservation.

</details>


### [14] [GTAGym: Real AAA Game Tasks Environment for Evaluation and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13028)
*Jiajun Chen,Shicheng Fang,Jiahao Lu,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GTAGym 是一个用于评估和强化学习（RL）在《GTA V》任务中的平台，提供了丰富的评估基准和自动效果检查，用于测试代理的感知、推理和控制。研究发现 Qwen3.8-27B 在特定任务上的表现有了显著提升，尤其是室内导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于评估代理在 AAA 游戏中完成任务的挑战性，研究需要开发一个详细的环境和框架来自动评估和进行强化学习。

Method: 开发了 GTAGym，该平台包括基于《GTA V》主线任务的评估基准 GTAVBench，以及一个可自动生成任务参数的平台。通过使用这些环境和基准，研究评估了前沿大语言模型（LLM）和 Qwen3.8-27B 在多种任务中的表现。

Result: 研究发现，最佳 LLM 配置在 GTAVBench-AGI 的整体准确率为 70.9%，并在特定任务上取得了显著进步。基于生成导航任务的 LoRA RL 提高了在领域内的性能，并且在未见过的任务类别中也有很好的迁移。

Conclusion: GTAGym 提供了一个强大的评估和强化学习环境，有助于推进代理在 AAA 游戏场景中的应用，尤其是在感知、推理和控制方面。该平台为未来的研发提供了广泛的测试基础和方法。

Abstract: AAA games offer a rich testbed for evaluating agent perception, reasoning, and control. However, evaluating whether agents can complete tasks in AAA games is challenging and often human-annotated because task outcomes are often difficult to verify automatically, let alone applying it for Reinforcement Learning (RL). We introduce GTAGym, an environment and framework for evaluation and RL on GTA V tasks. Its evaluation benchmark, GTAVBench, derived from GTA V main-story missions, contains 103 online tasks reconstructed across nine task families, alongside 47 image-based shooting and sniping tasks using GTA V imagery. A web server provides access to the online environment and evaluation interface, supporting parallel testing through screenshots and keyboard-mouse actions with automatic engine-state outcome checks. We evaluate six frontier LLMs on GTAVBench with textual task instructions and on its GTAVBench-AGI branch, where instructions appear only in the first screenshot and a harness automatically compresses interaction history. The best configuration achieves 70.9% overall accuracy in GTAVBench-AGI, yet aerial navigation, evasion and interception remain difficult. GTAGym also provides a scalable generator that samples task parameters within user-specified ranges and option sets under fixed templates, creating batches of 3D and image-based shooting environments for RL with automatic reward and termination signals. LoRA RL of Qwen3.8-27B on generated on-foot navigation tasks improves the in-domain performance and transfers to out-of-domain task categories, and native GTA V tasks from 15% to 23%.

</details>


### [15] [MICRO: A Surgery-Phase Retinal Image Rigid Registration Benchmark and One-Step Frequency-Structure Similarity Registration Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13045)
*Mingdao Gong,Yan Hu,Mingzhen Yuan,Jialu Zhang,Junming Feng,Yiming Jiang,Jing Ma,Jinghua Liu,Huazhu Fu,Hai Lu,Xiaoqing Zhang,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了 MICRO 数据集和 OFS2Reg 方法，旨在促进精确多模态眼底图像刚性注册的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏涵盖不同眼科手术阶段的多模态眼底图像刚性注册数据集，导致现有深度注册方法依赖两步分割-然后检测框架，但分割质量限制了其性能。研究者提出构建数据集和方法以改进此问题。

Method: 研究者提出了一种新颖的一步频率-结构相似度注册 (OFS2Reg) 学习框架，使用共享检测网络下的交叉模态血管结构相似性来构建关键点拓扑约束。具体而言，OFS2Reg 使用一个精心设计的频率导向结构保持描述符以突出显著的血管结构，构建粗粒度的关键点拓扑约束，并开发了一种新的跨模态血管结构相似度学习方法来细化跨模态关键点拓扑约束。

Result: 通过使用 MICRO 数据集和 OFS2Reg 框架，研究者能够更精确地对齐像素级别的跨模态血管结构。

Conclusion: 此研究为多模态眼底图像刚性注册提供了一个重要的数据集和先进方法，有助于提高眼科手术的精确度。

Abstract: Precise multimodal retinal image rigid registration (MRIRG) is essential for aligning cross-modal vessel structures, offering critical guidance for ophthalmic surgery. Despite its significance, the advancement of MRIRG is hindered by the lack of publicly available multimodal retinal image rigid registration datasets spanning various ophthalmic surgery phases (e.g., preoperative, intraoperative, and postoperative). To address this issue, we introduce the MICRO dataset, a large-scale MRIRG benchmark, comprising 1,365 cross-modal retinal image pairs with standardized annotations across all ophthalmic surgery phases. Moreover, existing deep registration methods achieve competitive MRIRG results via a two-step segmentation-then-detection framework, but segmentation quality heavily constrains their performance. We argue that this dependency stems from an implicit need for keypoint topology constraints, which have not been exploited for MRIRG through a one-step registration framework. Inspired by the above analysis, we propose a novel One-step Frequency-Structure Similarity Registration (OFS2Reg) learning framework for MRIRG that leverages the potential of cross-modal vessel structure similarities to construct keypoint topology constraints under a shared detection network. To be specific, OFS2Reg applies a well-designed frequency-guided structure preservation descriptor to construct coarse keypoint topology constraints in descriptors by highlighting significant vessel structures. Additionally, we develop a new cross-modal vessel structure similarity learning method that refines cross-modal keypoint topology constraints to precisely align pixel-level cross-modal vessel structures by leveraging cross-modal vessel structure similarity priors.

</details>


### [16] [SpatialSR: Spatial Resolution-Centric Pipeline for Arbitrary-Scale Remote Sensing Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13046)
*Yan Zhang,Runfeng Luo,Liangke Gao,Lin Yuan,Bin Xiao,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialSR 是一种针对遥感图像 (RSI) 的任意尺度超分辨率方法，它利用 RSISR 中的空间分辨率 (SRe) 作为超分辨率重建的重要先验，通过设计空间分辨率估计模块 (SREM) 动态确定所需的任意缩放因子，结合预训练的 SRe 到图像扩散模型提取 SRe 指定的纹理风格代码，并通过轨迹一致性损失 (TC) 优化，实现了高效且高质量的 SRe 意识下的纹理一致性。实验表明，SpatialSR 达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RSISR 方法主要依赖于相对尺度因子进行图像恢复，未能充分考虑图像的绝对空间分辨率作为重建先验，导致了空间相关的失真和下游任务性能的下降。SpatialSR 旨在解决这些问题，通过 SRe 作为先验，实现了更高质量的恢复。

Method: SpatialSR 方法包括：1) 空间分辨率估计模块 (SREM) 估计低分辨率 (LR) 图像的空间分辨率；2) 使用预训练的 SRe 到图像扩散模型来提取指定空间分辨率下的纹理风格代码；3) 设计了分辨率感知的风格一致性模块 (RSCM) 来确保重建图像中的 SRe 意识下的纹理一致性；4) 使用轨迹一致性 (TC) 损失来优化生成模型，实现高效的单步高质量重建。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，SpatialSR 在各种常用的 RSISR 和下游任务数据集上展现了最先进的性能，特别是在图像分类和分割任务中。

Conclusion: SpatialSR 通过引入空间分辨率作为先验，并结合预训练的扩散模型和轨迹一致性损失，显著提高了遥感图像的超分辨率重建质量，展示了其在现有技术中的优越性能。

Abstract: Although remote sensing image arbitrary-scale super-resolution (RSI-ASSR) methods support reconstruction at arbitrary or continuous scales, they predominantly condition on the relative scale factor and have not sufficiently investigated the absolute spatial resolution itself as a reconstruction prior, thereby disregarding the inherent spatial context of objects, which frequently leads to spatial-related distortions and compromised performance in downstream remote sensing applications. To address these challenges, we introduce SpatialSR, a SRe-centric arbitrary-scale RSISR method that leverages the spatial resolution (SRe) of remote sensing images (RSI) as an essential prior for super-resolution reconstruction. Distinct from existing SR methods, SpatialSR aims to restore high-resolution remote sensing images with a target SRe (typically set to 0.1m/pixel to align with the metadata of commonly used downstream task datasets). Specifically, we design a Spatial Resolution Estimation Module (SREM) to estimate the SRe of low-resolution (LR) images, thereby dynamically determining the required arbitrary scaling factor. Next, we employ a pretrained SRe-to-image diffusion model to extract SRe-specified texture-style codes, which are further incorporated into a carefully designed Resolution-aware Style Consistency Module (RSCM) to constrain the SRe-aware texture consistency of reconstructed images. Furthermore, to overcome the high computational expense inherent in diffusion-based SR methods, we wrap the iterative diffusion process as a Trajectory Consistency (TC) loss, which enables comparable reconstructed texture quality of the generative model in SpatialSR within a single step. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed SpatialSR achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on commonly used RSISR and downstream (image classification and segmentation tasks) datasets.

</details>


### [17] [Just Image Transformers with Late-Stage Pixel Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13120)
*Denis Lukovnikov,Andreas Müller,Asja Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文改进了先前的工作JiT，通过在后训练阶段添加简单的卷积像素路径层，显著提升了图像生成质量。实验表明，这种增强方法无需在去噪早期阶段激活，甚至可以在去噪过程的最后20%阶段激活，从而达到了ImageNet@256上FID为1.78的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JiT虽然提高了训练效率，但图像质量有所下降。因此，作者试图通过引入像素路径层在后训练阶段的调整来改善生成图像的质量。

Method: 作者在JiT的基础上，通过简单的卷积像素路径层的引入，在后训练阶段进行改进，具体可以在去噪过程的最后20%阶段激活这些路径层，以改善生成的图像质量。

Result: 实验结果显示，相比于原始的JiT方法，这种方法在ImageNet@256数据集上取得了FID为1.78的结果，而无需使用额外的损失函数。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法为提高基于扩散模型的图像生成质量提供了一种有效的后训练策略，展示了即使在不增加额外训练复杂度的情况下，仍能实现高质量的图像生成。

Abstract: Recent work on latent-free diffusion transformers has demonstrated the feasibility of training image generators without a latent VAE. This avoids compression artifacts from the VAE and simplifies the training pipeline. In particular, JiT showed that even a standard transformer can be trained to generate images when the model is trained to predict the final image ($x$-prediction). Even though JiT unlocks training efficiency comparable to latent models, it still suffers from reduced image quality likely arising from modeling the image as a collection of large patches. In this work, we revisit JiT and show that adding simple convolutional pixel-pathway layers in a post-training phase significantly improves generation quality. Moreover, we show that the added pixel-pathway does not need to be active in the early denoising stages, and can be activated as late as in the last $20\%$ of the denoising process. Our experiments on ImageNet@256 show improvements compared to the original JiT results, achieving an FID of $1.78$ (from $2.34$ with corrected sampling), without using additional losses.

</details>


### [18] [VIGOR: Benchmarking Visual Rationale Correctness in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13150)
*Yanlin Chen,Tang Li,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VIGOR基准，旨在评估生成视觉解释能力的多模态大模型（MLLMs）。VIGOR通过自动注释框架构建，支持从低级视觉属性到高级运动和关系多个抽象层次的概念和像素级连接。通过对比模型生成的视觉证据与标注的视觉理据，本研究揭示了当前MLLMs在不同概念层次上的视觉理据生成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）评估主要依赖生成正确答案，但这种评估标准无法反映模型输出是否基于正确的视觉证据。因此，为了填补这一评估空白，本文提出VIGOR基准，旨在评估MLLMs的视觉解释能力。

Method: VIGOR的构建采用自动标注框架，结合现有密集监督信息并利用提议式标注方法生成缺失的概念连接，并通过保守的多模式验证确保注释准确性。通过将模型生成的视觉证据与标注的视觉理据进行对比，来评估MLLMs的视觉解释能力。

Result: 实验结果显示，当前MLLMs在视觉理据生成方面表现有限，并且在不同概念层次上存在不同的定位失败问题。VIGOR为研究MLLMs输出与适当视觉证据支持的关系提供了一个基准和扩展性数据构建管道。

Conclusion: VIGOR是一个旨在评估MLLMs生成视觉解释能力的新基准，通过其构建方法和实验结果，揭示了当前MLLMs在视觉理据生成上的局限性，推动了该领域进一步的发展研究。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are typically evaluated by whether they produce correct answers, but correctness alone does not reveal whether their outputs are supported by the right visual evidence. We introduce VIGOR, a benchmark for evaluating VIsually GrOunded Rationales in MLLMs. VIGOR represents visual rationales as explicit concepts paired with pixel-level groundings, covering multiple abstraction levels including low-level visual properties, intermediate structures, objects, parts and high-level motion, relations. To construct VIGOR at scale, we develop an automatic annotation framework that reuses existing dense supervision when available and generates missing concept groundings through proposal-based annotation followed by conservative multimodal verification. Using VIGOR, we evaluate whether MLLMs "look at what they say" by comparing model-derived visual evidence for each expressed concept against annotated ground-truth rationales. Our results show that current MLLMs exhibit limited visual rationale correctness and reveal distinct grounding failures across concept levels. VIGOR provides a benchmark and scalable data construction pipeline for studying whether MLLM outputs are supported by appropriate visual evidence. Code is available at https://github.com/deep-real/VIGOR

</details>


### [19] [Continuous Angular Control in Text-to-Image Generation via Geometric Token Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13172)
*Furkan Güzelant,Basavaraj Sunagad,Aysegul Dundar,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了文本到图像的发散模型在生成模拟时钟时无法精确控制显示时间的问题，并提出了一种几何编码框架来直接通过角度参数化进行生成，提高了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型无法精确控制模拟时钟的显示时间，本研究旨在通过引入一种几何编码框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 研究使用周期性正弦-余弦特征编码每个角度参数，并将其投影到特定角色的标记嵌入中，注入到文本编码器之前。为了在推理时不需定位输入，引入了Stochastic Localization Relaxation (SLR)机制，该机制在训练时从组件级到对象级逐渐放松注意力监督，同时为模拟时钟引入了手交换偏好目标。

Result: 该方法在评估方法中达到63-78%的精确度，而在圆形部分生成中达到了89.5%的准确率，同时在不常见对象类别和目标值上仍保持88.8%和84.9%的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入一种几何编码框架，显著提高了模拟时钟和圆形部分生成的精确度和泛化能力，展示了该方法在处理视觉生成任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can render a photorealistic analog clock but cannot control the time it displays. Across four pretrained models, fewer than 1% of generated clocks place both hands within 6 degrees of the requested time; the models instead collapse onto visually dominant configurations such as the canonical 10:10 pose. The same failure appears when a specific fraction of a circular object is requested, where models return complete or conventionally sliced objects. We observe that these two failures share a geometric structure: both target quantities can be parameterized by angular variables.
  We introduce a geometric token encoding framework that conditions generation directly on these angles. Each angular component is encoded as a periodic sine-cosine feature and projected into a role-specific token embedding that is injected into the prompt before a frozen text encoder. To spatially ground these tokens without requiring localization input at inference, we introduce Stochastic Localization Relaxation (SLR), which stochastically relaxes attention supervision from component-level to object-level to unconstrained localization during training. For clocks, we additionally introduce a hand-swap preference objective that discourages assigning the correct angles to the wrong hand roles. On analog clocks our method reaches 63-78% Acc@6 degrees across evaluation methods, compared with below 1% for the baselines. On circular-fraction generation, it achieves 89.5% Acc@0.10, while maintaining 88.8% on unseen object categories and 84.9% on unseen target values, compared with roughly 20% for pretrained baselines.

</details>


### [20] [GroundCascade: Selective Second Opinions for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13177)
*Adrian Phoulady,Hady Ahmady Phoulady*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GroundCascade是一种无需训练的控制器，通过 ground 插件之间的预测一致性决定何时使用互补模型，减少了对两种模型双阶段预测的无谓调用，从而提高了准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解决方案在细节检测上表现良好，但在保留上下文信息方面不足。GroundCascade通过利用屏幕级和放大级预测的共识，减少无谓调用并提高性能。

Method: GroundCascade采用了一种选择性机制，只有在屏幕上不同模型的预测存在分歧时才会调用第二个模型。在一致性较高的情况下，仅使用主模型的预测。

Result: 在不同数据集上的实验表明，GroundCascade在ScreenSpot-Pro数据集上达到了85.33%，在OSWorld-G-Refined数据集上达到了88.43%，并在UI-Vision数据集上取得72.20%的准确率，均优于其他单一和两阶段解决方案。

Conclusion: GroundCascade通过选择性地调用互补模型，实现了更高的性能，同时降低了调用模型的次数，在现有的比较中证明了其优越性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) grounding maps a screenshot and a natural-language instruction to the screen location where an agent should act. Zoomed views can reveal small targets but may lose the context needed to identify them. We introduce GroundCascade, a training-free controller that uses agreement between a grounder's full-screen and zoomed predictions to decide when to consult a complementary model. It selects between zoomed predictions while preserving corroborated full-screen locations. An optional Finalizer predicts from an unmarked joint crop when disagreement remains (Full); omitting it yields the two-stage Selective variant. GroundCascade reaches 85.33% on ScreenSpot-Pro with Indeed-UI-32B/8B/32B and 88.43% on the visible-target OSWorld-G-Refined subset with Qwen3.6-27B/3.8-27B/3.8-27B, gains of 2.66 and 2.35 percentage points over the stronger standalone two-pass grounder. On UI-Vision, GroundCascade reaches 72.20% category-balanced accuracy, with Selective slightly higher at 72.28%. Across these three evaluations, GroundCascade in Full mode uses 2.42-2.73 calls per request, 31-39% fewer than unconditionally acquiring both models' two-pass predictions. At matched second-model call budgets, selective acquisition gains 1.52-2.56 points over standalone Two-pass, compared with 0.22-0.69 points from stratified random acquisition. GroundCascade leads the official ScreenSpot-Pro leaderboard and exceeds reported GUI-specialist comparisons on OSWorld-G-Refined and UI-Vision, subject to differences in external evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [21] [Multimodal Judges Flag, Humans Decide: A Review-and-Edit Framework for AI-Generated Product Video Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13178)
*Anoubhav Agarwaal,Sukesh Perla,Karthikeyan Mohanraj,Susmita Ghose*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新型的广告审查和编辑流程，通过六项具体问题审查视频广告，由基于视觉-语言、语音和语言模型的裁判将发现的问题指派到相应的修复工具，并进行确认与编辑，此流程能与生成广告的速度保持同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式管道能够将电商产品页面转换为视频广告，需要一个能够快速审查和编辑这些广告的过程，以确保其质量和准确性。

Method: 研究定义了六个具体问题，包括产品特性、场景质量、发音、剧本、音效和字幕，并通过基于视觉-语言、语音和语言模型的裁判进行分配和确认。为每个发现的问题提供相应的修复工具。同时，进行了盲测和有裁判标记的审核，以评估效果。

Result: 研究人员对120个广告进行了审核，结果显示在60%的场景中，产品问题被标记，相比之下，没有裁判标记的情况下，两名审核员只分别标记了26%的场景。产品裁判对于审查看到了84%的召回率，但同时也标记了太多干净的场景，最终审核员需要决定哪些是真正的缺陷，哪些是误报。

Conclusion: 该系统成功地与广告生成过程保持了同步，并通过裁判系统的辅助提高了审核的准确性和效率。为广告审查和编辑提供了一种创新的方法。相关数据和平台模板已开源。

Abstract: Generative pipelines can now turn every product page of an e-commerce catalog into a short video ad, but deciding whether such an ad is usable is not one good-or-bad call. We describe how we operationalized the review and editing of these ads so that both can keep pace with generation: reviewers go through each ad scene by scene and answer six questions, on the product, scene quality, pronunciation, script, sound and captions; judges built from vision-language, speech and language models file each finding under the matching question, and reviewers accept or reject each finding and add what the judges missed. Each confirmed issue then opens an edit tool matched to it. The questions and the routing from a finding to its fix do not depend on the models that generate the ad. Across 120 ads from our pipeline, reviewed with judge findings on screen, reviewers marked the product wrong on 60% of scenes, and script, caption or sound problems on under 10% each. On 60 of these ads, they marked the product wrong on 58% of scenes, far more than the 26% marked by two reviewers who later saw the ads with the judges hidden; one reviewer who saw the ads both ways marked 57% with judge findings and 19% without. On 120 blind-labeled scenes, a product judge that watches the clip with the catalog photos reached AUC 0.84, while video-quality and photo-similarity scores were close to chance. We tune the judges for recall: the product judge catches nearly all defects but also flags many clean scenes, so reviewers decide; together, the judges widen what reviewers mark and reviewers add what the judges miss. Without judge findings, one reviewer's marking faded over a session. We release a review-portal template and our review data (120 ads and 562 scenes with judge findings and human marks, plus 120 blind-labeled scenes with baseline scores) at https://github.com/anoubhav/ad-review-and-edit.

</details>


### [22] [X Marks the Spot: Diffusion for Image Geolocalization with Location Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13180)
*Brian Wei,Chih-Chuan Hsu,Tianyi Gao,Daniel Cher,Eric Xing,Danielle Beaulieu,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Location Autoencoder (LAE)和基于隐空间扩散的模型DIG，通过预训练的位置编码器隐空间进行坐标预测，显著提高了参数化地理定位的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于传统的基于扩散的方法生成的坐标在空间上不稳定，提出了一种新的方法，利用预训练的位置编码器隐空间进行坐标预测。

Method: 采用Location Autoencoder (LAE)和基于隐空间扩散的模型DIG。LAE利用冻结的位置编码器和学习到的解码器将嵌入重新映射回具有千米级精度的坐标。DIG在图像特征条件下，将噪声传输到位置嵌入。

Result: 在OSV-5M、Im2GPS3k和YFCC4k三个基准上，DIG的表现优于基于坐标的生成模型，并且是第一个优于检索方法的生成模型，进一步提高了检索和重排序系统的性能。

Conclusion: 这种方法在参数化地理定位领域达到了新的最佳水平，并提供了一个开源代码和模型，可用于进一步研究。

Abstract: Image geolocalization predicts the location at which a photo was taken. Accurate geolocalization requires discriminating between both physically and semantically adjacent areas on Earth. However, prior diffusion-based approaches predict locations by generating coordinates, which are semantically unstable over space. We avoid this limitation by learning geolocalization behavior in the pretrained representation spaces of location encoders, which provide both positional and semantic understanding. To make this space usable for coordinate prediction, we introduce a Location Autoencoder (LAE), which pairs a frozen location encoder with a learned decoder mapping embeddings back to coordinates with kilometer-level accuracy worldwide. In the LAE space, we train DIG, a latent diffusion model that transports noise to location embeddings conditioned on image features. DIG consistently outperforms coordinate-based generative models and sets a new state of the art for parametric geolocalization on three benchmarks: OSV-5M, Im2GPS3k, and YFCC4k. Notably, DIG is the first generative model to outperform retrieval-based methods, and further improves retrieve-and-rerank systems when used as a prior. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [23] [Comparative Evaluation of Deep Learning Models for Geographic Atrophy Segmentation in En Face OCT Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13191)
*Utkarsh Doshi,Adarsh Gadari,Sharat Chandra Vupparaboina,Joseph DeCicco,Giulia Gregori,Nicola Valsecchi,Nasiq Hasan,Elham Sadeghi,Josè-Alain Sahel,Jay Chhablani,Kiran Kumar Vupparaboina,Sandeep Chandra Bollepalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究对比了五种深度学习架构在共Facing OCT图像中黄斑萎缩(GA)分割任务上的性能，最终nnUnet模型表现最优，显示出在有限数据条件下GA量化时最可靠的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有的深度学习方法通常使用单一架构和单一设备数据集进行评估，限制了对相对性能和跨设备鲁棒性的理解，因此需要进行系统性的模型对比，以提高黄斑萎缩的诊断、监测和发展个性化治疗策略的精准度。

Method: 训练和系统性对比五种深度学习架构(Nested U-Net, ResU-Net, nnUnet, Swin-Unet和MedSegDiff)，采用一种包含标准化和修补的三级预处理技术处理来自两种不同设备的二维OCT图像数据集。

Result: 在来自两种不同设备(Cirrus和Spectralis)的OCT扫描图像的保留验证集上，nnUnet模型的平均Dice相似性系数最高，为0.8882 ± 0.0033。其他模型的Dice分数分别为Nested U-Net (0.7932 ± 0.0423)，ResU-Net (0.8453 ± 0.0074)，Swin-Unet (0.6886 ± 0.0294)，MedSegDiff模型 (0.7959 ± 0.0242)。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，nnUnet框架在Facing OCT图像中黄斑萎缩分割任务上表现出最可靠的表现，适用于有限数据条件下的GA量化，提供最佳的准确性和稳定性平衡。

Abstract: Background: Geographic atrophy (GA) is an advanced form of age-related macular degeneration (AMD). It causes irreversible vision loss due to progressive degeneration of the retinal pigment epithelium (RPE) and photoreceptors. Accurate segmentation of GA in en-face image is crucial for early diagnosis, monitoring disease progression, and developing personalized treatment strategies. However, existing deep learning approaches are typically evaluated using single architectures and single-device datasets, limiting insight into relative performance and cross-device robustness. Methods: We trained and systematically compared five deep learning architectures for GA segmentation: Nested U-Net, ResU-Net, nnUnet, Swin-Unet and MedSegDiff model, together with a thresholding based classical baseline. The models were trained on a dataset of en-face OCT images derived from 269 OCT volumes from two different devices, following a three-step preprocessing technique involving normalization and inpainting to enhance the quality of the en-face images. Results: On the fixed held-out validation set comprising OCT scans from both Cirrus (R) (Carl Zeiss Meditec, Inc., USA) and Spectralis (R) (Heidelberg Engineering, Germany) devices, nnUnet achieved the highest mean Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) of 0.8882 +/- 0.0033. The other models yielded the following Dice scores: Nested U-Net (0.7932 +/- 0.0423), ResU-Net (0.8453 +/- 0.0074), Swin-Unet (0.6886 +/- 0.0294), and Diffusion model (0.7959 +/- 0.0242). Conclusions: nnUnet framework, demonstrated most reliable performance for GA segmentation in en-face OCT under limited data conditions. It offers the best balance of accuracy, stability, and efficiency for GA quantification.

</details>


### [24] [Multimodal Road Closure Mapping via Cross-Modal Graph Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13192)
*Izzeddin Teeti,Vaibhav Kango,Nidhi Sridhar,Mohamed Moustafa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种统一的多模态多任务框架，用于联合识别道路关闭状态和关联受影响的道路段，通过结合地图数据、卫星图像和街景图像，在实际场景中实现了高于现有模型的分类和关联精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过解决现有方法在道路关闭识别中的局限性，即依赖手动地图更新或仅检测相关对象而无法准确关联道路段的问题，该论文旨在提高导航和物流系统的效率和精度。

Method: 论文构建了一个融合图结构地图数据、卫星图像与投影街道检测以及街景图像的多模态模型。该模型使用特定模态编码器和跨模态变压器注意力机制，实现视觉线索和网络拓扑的显式推理。

Result: 在大规模实际场景中，该方法的路障分类准确率达到72.7%，道路段关联准确率达到60%，在分类准确性上超过了最先进的视觉-语言模型28个百分点，同时模型更小（大约大了4000倍）且推理速度更快（大约快了100倍）。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了首个同时考虑路障分类和道路段关联的基准，证实了针对具体任务的架构先验偏差在结构地理空间理解上优于通用多模态模型。

Abstract: Accurate and timely identification of road closures is critical for large-scale navigation and logistics systems, yet remains challenging due to the need to jointly reason over visual evidence, spatial context, and road network topology. Existing approaches either rely on manual map updates or focus on detecting closure-related objects without resolving which road segment is affected. In this work, we introduce a unified multimodal and multitask framework that jointly performs road closure classification (no, partial, complete) and road segment association within a single learning architecture. Our model integrates graph-structured map data, satellite imagery augmented with projected street-level detections, and street-view images using modality-specific encoders and cross-modal transformer attention, enabling explicit reasoning over visual cues and network topology. Evaluated on nearly 20K real-world scenarios collected from a large-scale commercial vehicle fleet, our approach achieves 72.7% closure classification accuracy and 60% road segment association accuracy, outperforming frontier vision-language models by over 28 percentage points on closure classification while being over 4,000x smaller and 100x faster at inference. Extensive ablations demonstrate the importance of cross-modal attention, edge-aware pooling, and graph message passing for accurate geospatial reasoning. This work establishes the first benchmark for joint road closure classification and segment-level attribution, and shows that task-specific architectural inductive biases substantially outperform general-purpose multimodal models for structured geospatial understanding.

</details>


### [25] [Image Quality and the Reliability of Fabric-Density Measurement: Counting Threads from Smartphone Photos Through a Pick Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13200)
*Anoubhav Agarwaal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用智能手机照片来快速检查织物线密度的方法，通过任务导向的图像质量评估标准进行评价。引入了PickGlass-TPI数据集并评估了多种模型，发现没有任何方法在误差范围在±5%内与参考值相符，但一些模型例如Molmo2-4B和GPT-6.1-sol表现接近优秀，且可靠性低于人工复拍，表明未来需要在模型的鲁棒性和一致性上下功夫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高织物线密度检测的速度和记录功能，本文引入了智能手机摄像并通过标记线密度的实验数据集进行模型的基准测试。

Method: 作者利用PickGlass-TPI数据集对不同模型进行评估，包括前沿和开源的视觉语言模型、卷积神经网络、专门的计数器以及无需训练的光谱计数器。通过对比不同模型的误差范围和稳定性来评估模型性能。

Result: 没有任何模型在误差范围在±5%内与参考值相符，最好的模型偏差在25%-27%之间。参考值在重复拍摄中的误差中位数为9%，表明目前单张照片还不足以满足误差范围在±5%的要求。

Conclusion: 尽管现有的计数模型接近于表现优秀水平，但它们在重复一致性和可靠性方面仍然存在不足。未来的研究需要着重提高模型的鲁棒性，确保其误差范围能够满足工业标准。

Abstract: Thread density, the threads per inch along and across a woven fabric, is a basic specification that buyers check whenever fabric is sold. Without laboratory instruments, inspectors count it slowly, by eye, through a pick glass, a folding 10$\times$ magnifier with a one-inch window. A smartphone photo through that glass could make the check faster and leave a record. We judge such a photo by what it is used for, as in task-based image quality: it is good if the thread count from it is within $\pm$5% of the true count, a common tolerance in fabric specifications. We introduce PickGlass-TPI: 76 factory scenes of 44 fabric patches, each counted in an open annotation tool by a textile-trained annotator, whose counts are the reference. On it we benchmark frontier and open vision-language models, CNNs, counting specialists and a training-free spectral counter (PG-Lattice). No method agrees with the reference within the tolerance. The best methods are off by about a quarter: a small open model that we tuned only on synthetic images (Molmo2-4B, 25%) is level with the best frontier model, GPT-6.1-sol (27% when asked directly, 25% given four magnified close-ups of the window). Both err less than a non-expert annotator (37%), but only the tuned model also beats, on both error measures, a baseline that guesses each scene's density as the average of the others. PG-Lattice most often repeats its count within 5% across two photos of the same patch, but its misses, like GPT-6.1-sol's errors, are often a factor of two. Even the reference repeats a count across such re-shoots only to a median 9%, so one photo cannot yet be shown to meet $\pm$5%; the image quality that matters is reliability across re-shoots. Generic no-reference quality scores chosen before labeling do not rank PG-Lattice's error, and generative enhancement raises perceptual scores but does not reliably improve the count.

</details>


### [26] [Before Words, Beyond Speech: Evaluating Nonverbal Social Reasoning in Early Childhood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13213)
*Marie Amale Huynh,Laura Bravo-Sánchez,Lauren Klein,Nick Haber,Philip Fisher,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NEST (Naturalistic Early-childhood Social inTeractions) 为重点于非言语社交推理的儿童为中心基准，提出了一种新的评价框架，并揭示了现有的视觉-语言模型在解码具体行为和人际线索方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态视频理解基准主要关注成人之间的对话驱动交互，而忽视了儿童在早期学习过程中通过目光接触、手势和物理接触进行的社会学习。NEST旨在填补这一空白，提供首个专注于儿童的非言语社交推理基准。

Method: NEST基准包含了多个手动标注的短视频片段，这些片段覆盖了自然环境、文化背景和身心发展阶段的多样性。基准还引入了一种分层评价框架，将基础的互动线索逐步构建到更高级的社会推理层级：情境、行为、人际和开放式描述。

Result: 对最先进的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的广泛分析表明，尽管在情境识别方面表现出色，但模型解码具体的、行为和人际线索方面存在显著差距。这种差距追溯到模型的一些偏见，包括因果关系理解不足和时间敏感性。

Conclusion: NEST为推动基于社会的AI技术在儿童早期学习中的应用提供了严格的测试平台，展示了视觉-语言模型在非言语社交推理方面的局限性。

Abstract: Children's earliest years are a period of intense social learning, during which meaning is built through gaze, gesture, and physical contact. Yet, this critical aspect of social interaction remains under-evaluated in multimodal video understanding, as existing benchmarks prioritize the conversation-driven interactions of adults. We address this gap with \bench ($\textbf{N}$aturalistic $\textbf{E}$arly-childhood $\textbf{S}$ocial in$\textbf{T}$eractions), the first child-centric benchmark for nonverbal social reasoning. \bench comprises \totalvids manually annotated clips ($\sim$12s) curated for diversity across naturalistic settings, cultural contexts, and developmental stages. Beyond a lack of data, targeted model improvement in this domain is hindered by coarse evaluation schemas that conflate basic perception with higher-order reasoning. To resolve this, \bench introduces a compositional schema that builds atomic interaction cues into progressive levels of social reasoning: contextual, behavioral, interpersonal, and open-ended descriptions. Extensive analysis of state-of-the-art Vision-Language Models (VLMs) reveals a stark performance collapse: despite strong contextual recognition, models exhibit substantial gaps in decoding precise behavioral and interpersonal cues. We trace these failures to specific biases, including poor consequence grounding and temporal sensitivity. Finally, we ground \bench in real-world applications by evaluating VLMs using domain adaptation strategies. Ultimately, \bench provides a rigorous testbed for advancing socially grounded artificial intelligence in early childhood. Project page: https://laubravo.github.io/nest_website/.

</details>


### [27] [Rethinking Evaluation for Video Capsule Endoscopy: Validation Protocol, Source-Video Diversity, and Cross-Dataset Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13245)
*Smriti Regmi,Debesh Jha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DMSNet的网络，它结合了自适应多尺度特征融合、可变形病灶细化、边缘导向注意力和稀疏特征选择。在Kvasir-Capsule数据集上使用随机帧级和源视频分割验证协议进行基准测试。结果表明，DMSNet在随机帧级验证下的完整测试集和源视频分割子集上分别获得了0.442 ± 0.020和0.456 ± 0.037的MCC。此外，通过增加训练源视频的多样性，评估了不同架构在外部CV2024数据集上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究VCE分类中的模型排名和分类性能依赖于验证协议、训练源视频的多样性以及跨数据集迁移的问题，旨在提出新的模型并探讨模型性能的评估方法。

Method: 提出DMSNet，采用自适应多尺度特征融合、可变形病灶细化、边缘导向注意力和稀疏特征选择技术。在Kvasir-Capsule数据集上进行定量实验并评估不同架构模型的性能在随机帧级和源视频分割验证协议下的表现。

Result: DMSNet在Kvasir-Capsule的完整测试集和源视频分割子集上分别获得0.442 ± 0.020和0.456 ± 0.037的MCC。在外部CV2024数据集上，ConvNeXt-Tiny获得了最高的MCC 0.207。增加最大源视频覆盖率从4到8，MCC在19种评估架构中有17种得到提高。

Conclusion: 研究指出，模型排名和分类性能很大程度取决于评估协议的选择，训练源视频多样性的增加可能有助于提高分类性能，但模型的整体表现仍然受到数据集差异的影响，需要进行源视频感知的验证以及严格的外部评估。

Abstract: Automated video capsule endoscopy (VCE) classification remains challenging due to severe class imbalance, limited source-video diversity, and variations in image appearance across datasets. Although deep learning models have demonstrated promising performance on established benchmarks, their reliability under different validation protocols and external dataset shift remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce DMSNet, a deformable adaptive multi-scale edge-guided attention network integrating adaptive multi-scale feature fusion, deformable lesion refinement, edge-guided attention, and sparse feature selection. We benchmark DMSNet against 18 representative convolutional, transformer-based, and pretrained vision architectures on Kvasir-Capsule under random frame-level and source-video-disjoint validation protocols. We further identify source-video overlap between the original training and test splits and evaluate model performance on both the complete test set and a source-video-disjoint subset. Under random frame-level validation, DMSNet achieves an MCC of 0.442 $\pm$ 0.020 on complete test set and 0.456 $\pm$ 0.037 on the source-video-disjoint subset. On the external CV2024 dataset, ConvNeXt-Tiny achieves the highest MCC of 0.207, while performance degrades substantially across architectures relative to in-domain evaluation. We further investigate source-video diversity using a per-class cap of 200 training frames. With the total training-set size held fixed, increasing the maximum source-video coverage from four to eight improves MCC for 17 of 19 evaluated architectures. Overall, these findings demonstrate that model rankings and classification performance depend substantially on evaluation protocol, training-source diversity, and cross-dataset shift, emphasizing the need for source-aware validation and rigorous external evaluation of VCE classification systems.

</details>


### [28] [Adaptive Residual Quantization for Memory-Efficient Temporal Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13262)
*Gerard Donahue,Guven Gergerli,Ayush Gupta,Reza Ghoddoosian,Enna Sachdeva,Faizan Siddiqui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARQ-VAE 提出了一种紧凑且无需标签的记忆表示方法，用于动作分割任务，通过自适应地量化和截断帧级视频特征，减少存储需求，同时保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前动作分割方法依赖大量预提取帧特征和密集的帧级标注，而对长视频进行增量和无监督分割时，存储和播放这些特征构成了实际瓶颈。

Method: ARQ-VAE 方法学习离散化的残差量化帧级视频特征，并根据每帧自适应截断残差链，仅保留最佳重建每个帧特征的残差前缀。还提出一种轻量级的微调阶段进一步提高保留代码的重建质量。

Result: ARQ-VAE 在标准动作分割基准测试中展示了与先前重演和压缩基线相比显著改进的存储-性能权衡。在 Breakfast 数据集上，最终表示将原 I3D 特征库的训练集存储从 28 GB 减少到 10 MB，同时保持强大的下游分割性能；在 ASFormer 模型下，实现了71.5 Edit 和 51.2 F1@50 的表现。

Conclusion: ARQ-VAE 提供了一种实用的记忆表示方法，适用于动作分割任务，并为进一步的数据集压缩应用开启了前景。

Abstract: Temporal action segmentation (TAS) is commonly trained on large banks of pre-extracted frame features, but storing and replaying those features becomes a practical bottleneck for long-form video, especially in incremental TAS where past-task data must remain available to mitigate catastrophic forgetting. Recent TAS replay and condensation methods reduce this burden, but they rely on dense frame-wise annotations and allocate storage through fixed segment-level structures, limiting their flexibility for long procedural videos whose frame complexity varies substantially over time. We introduce Adaptive Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (ARQ-VAE), a compact and label-free memory representation for TAS features that learns a discrete residual quantization of frame-level video features and then adaptively truncates the residual chain on a per-frame basis, retaining only the residual prefix that best reconstructs each frame feature. This yields a practical compact feature memory that does not depend on frame-wise labels and can therefore support multiple TAS settings with the same stored representation, while a lightweight fine-tuning stage further improves reconstruction quality for the truncated codes that are actually retained. Across standard TAS benchmarks, ARQ-VAE achieves a substantially improved storage-performance trade-off over prior replay and condensation baselines. On Breakfast, our final representation reduces training-set storage from 28 GB for the original I3D feature bank to 10 MB while retaining strong downstream segmentation performance; with ASFormer, it achieves 71.5 Edit and 51.2 F1@50 in the supervised setting. We further show that the same label-free memory remains effective for incremental and unsupervised TAS. We position ARQ-VAE as a practical memory representation for TAS and a promising direction for broader dataset condensation applications.

</details>


### [29] [RetinexGS: Retinex-Inspired Gaussian Splatting for Low-Light RAW Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13280)
*Yulang Fei,Huaiyuan Weng,Chul Min Yeum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RetinexGS 在低光照场景中通过结合 Retinex 灵感的外观建模和传感器信息的优化，利用 RAW 图像提供线性传感器测量和噪声元数据来改进 3D 重建，特别是在暗光区域实现了更稳定的颜色和更完整的重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的 3D 贝塞尔绘制方式在严重欠曝光的图像中存在问题，如不稳定色彩、假象和重建不完整。RAW 图像提供了一种天然基于噪声感知重建的途径，因为它们保存了线性的传感器测量值并提供了噪声元数据访问途径。

Method: RetinexGS 结合了一种 Retinex 灵感的外观建模方法与传感器导向的优化技术，将每个高斯色分解为视图无关的基础色和共享的位置和视图相关的调制场，并对这两个部分进行单独约束，从而限制了基于噪声观察的学习。通过使用 DNG 噪声元数据来给解码损失加权，以及通过畸变感知渲染避开空间重采样以实现 RAW 保持，RetinexGS 在黑暗区域引入了深度导向的初始化。

Result: 与 LE3D 和 RawNeRF 相比，RetinexGS 在五个场景的 mean sRGB PSNR 上分别高出 2.18 dB 和 2.71 dB，实现了更优的低光创新视角渲染。

Conclusion: RetinexGS 改进了在低光照条件下的 3DGS 质量，提供了一种更有效的方式来处理欠曝光图像，并增强了在暗区域的重构性能。

Abstract: Standard 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) pipelines struggle with severely underexposed images, producing unstable colors, floating artifacts, and incomplete reconstructions in dark regions. To better account for noise in these low-light observations, RAW images offer a natural basis for noise-aware reconstruction by preserving linear sensor measurements and providing access to noise metadata. We present RetinexGS, a RAW-domain 3DGS framework that combines Retinex-inspired appearance modeling with sensor-informed optimization for low-light novel-view synthesis. We factorize each Gaussian's color into a view-independent base color and a shared, position- and view-dependent modulation field, regularizing the two components separately to constrain appearance learning from noisy observations. We use DNG noise metadata to weight the reconstruction loss for signal-dependent noise, while distortion-aware rendering avoids spatially resampling RAW targets. To address sparse geometry in dark regions, we introduce depth-guided initialization. We evaluate RetinexGS on three public RawNeRF scenes and two custom-captured low-light RAW scenes. With all methods trained on the same RAW inputs for each scene, RetinexGS outperforms LE3D and RawNeRF in mean sRGB PSNR by 2.18 dB and 2.71 dB respectively, across the five scenes, while supporting real-time novel-view rendering.

</details>


### [30] [Domain adaptation of a video Foundation model for Heart Failure Diagnosis from Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13297)
*Mahyar Bolhassani,Tijana Geroski,Narayana S. Singam,Amir Amini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用预训练视频基础模型（TimeSformer）进行心脏四腔视视频中射血分数降低型心力衰竭（HFrEF）的自动诊断的可行性，并提出了一种阶段感知帧生成策略，该策略捕捉了收缩期和舒张期的动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文章指出，尽管基础模型在计算机视觉领域的快速发展，但它们在医学成像领域的应用，特别是针对时空超声心动图视频的定制基础模型，仍然受到限制。因此，探索将预训练的视频基础模型（TimeSformer）微调来自动诊断HFrEF具有重要意义。

Method: 研究通过分割空间时间注意力机制、逐层学习率衰减及多帧推断策略增强模型在有限数据下的鲁棒性，对公开的RVENet A4C数据集进行了模型微调。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提出模型的全面微调优于仅分类器微调和3D CNN基线模型。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在医学成像领域利用基础视频模型进行超声心动图心力衰竭诊断的潜力，鼓励进一步的领域适应研究。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of foundation models has transformed computer vision, yet their adaptation to medical imaging, particularly purpose built foundation models for spatio temporal echocardiography videos, remains limited. In this work, we explore the feasibility of fine tuning a pretrained foundation model for video (TimeSformer) for automated diagnosis of reduced ejection fraction heart failure (HFrEF) from apical four chamber videos. The model is finetuned on the publicly available RVENet A4C dataset with a phase aware clip generation strategy that captures complementary systolic and diastolic dynamics. We employ divided space time attention, layer wise learning rate decay, and multi clip inference to enhance robustness under limited data. Experimental results show that full fine tuning of the proposed model outperforms classifier only tuning and a 3D CNN baseline. These findings highlight the potential of leveraging foundation video models for echocardiography based heart failure diagnosis and motivates further domain adaptation studies in medical imaging.

</details>


### [31] [DUO: Dual Readouts of Diffusion Models for OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13304)
*Alireza Javid,Shirin Shoushtari,Nuria González Prelcic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DUO的OOD检测方法，结合了重建误差和特征灵敏度，即使在重建误差不准确的情况下也能提供有效的信息。DUO在平均AUROC上提高了约6个百分点，并在近OOD和高分辨率基准测试上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于重建误差的OOD检测方法在某些情况下可能不可靠，本文旨在提出一种新的方法来克服这一问题。

Method: 本文提出了一种名为DUO的方法，通过分析中间去噪特征的敏感性来提供可靠的OOD检测信号，即使在重建误差不准确的情况下也能保持信息的有效性。

Result: DUO方法在平均AUROC上提高了约6个百分点，在近OOD和高分辨率基准测试上达到了当前最佳性能。

Conclusion: DUO是一种完全无监督且无需训练的OOD检测方法，能够显著提高OOD检测的性能，特别是在一些具有挑战性的基准测试中展现出优越的表现。

Abstract: Diffusion models are powerful generative methods for learning and sampling from complex data distributions. Safely deploying these models requires detecting inputs that fall outside their training distribution. Because diffusion models recover clean samples from noisy inputs, reconstruction error is often used for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. This approach assumes that in-distribution (ID) inputs are reconstructed more accurately than OOD inputs. However, OOD inputs may be reconstructed equally well or even better, making this score unreliable. We therefore propose the leading sensitivity spectrum of intermediate denoising features as a complementary OOD signal and demonstrate that it remains informative when reconstruction error is misleading. We introduce DUal-readout of diffusion models for OOD detection (DUO), a fully unsupervised, training-free OOD detector that combines reconstruction error and feature sensitivity. DUO improves average AUROC by approximately 6% points over the strongest baselines while substantially reducing inference cost, and achieves SOTA on near-OOD and high-resolution benchmarks.

</details>


### [32] [Domain-Adversarial Adaptation from MURA to In-House Radiographs for Improved Adult Elbow Fracture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13308)
*M. Bolhassani,W. Furtado,D. Duvall,MJ Negahdar,J. Joshi,S. Contractor,A. Amini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过实施域对抗神经网络（DANN），将大型公共数据集MURA与小型机构特定数据集UofL的特征分布对齐，以优化肘部骨折分类。DANN通过减少域偏见并提高目标性能，优于源仅和朴素合并基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在有限的本地数据集上实现可靠的临床泛化，研究将公共大型数据集MURA与较小的机构特定数据集UofL的数据分布进行对齐。

Method: 研究采用预训练的卷积骨干网络来适应灰度输入，使用梯度反转机制的域头通过对抗学习鼓励域不变特征。通过四个设置对比：源仅、目标仅（有限标签）、朴素合并以及DANN（MURA到UofL），并对抗学习的权重进行渐进性调整，以改善稳定性和目标性能。

Result: 结果表明DANN优于源仅和朴素合并基线，通过减少域偏见，没有过度拟合小的目标集。消融实验显示，对抗学习权重的逐渐变化、数据集尺寸感知领域的损失以及域平衡抽样都提高了稳定性和目标性能。

Conclusion: 域对齐是实现从公共数据到本地数据集中的稳健临床泛化的实用路径。

Abstract: We studied domain shift in adult elbow fracture classification from radiographs when training on a large public dataset and deploying on a small, institution-specific dataset (UofL). We implemented a domain-adversarial neural network (DANN) to align feature distributions across source (MURA) and target (UofL) domains while optimizing fracture classification. A pretrained convolutional backbone was adapted to grayscale inputs; the task head predicts fracture presence, and a gradient-reversal-based domain head encourages domain-invariant features via adversarial learning. We compared four settings: (1) Source-only, (2) Target-only (limited labels), (3) Naive merge (pooled training), and (4) DANN (MURA to UofL). Evaluation on UofL test data uses AUC-ROC, and accuracy. DANN outperformed Source-only and Naive merge baselines by reducing domain bias without overfitting to the small target set. Ablations showed that progressive adversarial weight, dataset-size-aware domain loss, and domain-balanced sampling improved stability and target performance. Results demonstrated that domain alignment is a practical path to robust clinical generalization from public to local data.

</details>


### [33] [ABRR: Transforming Temporal Sentence Grounding with Wasserstein-Adaptive Refinement and Precision Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13310)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Junhao Dong,Daizong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的、模型无关的后处理框架ABRR，通过平滑时间分布、利用最优传输对边界进行细化以及基于质量的重新排名，解决了时间段对齐和排名优化问题，显著提升了时间语义检索的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了改进基于Transformer的时间语义检索（TSG）方法中由于时间下采样引起的量化误差和基于语义匹配的次优排名，本文提出了ABRR框架。

Method: ABRR框架包括三个创新组件：1）通过高斯核卷积平滑时间分布；2）利用2- Wasserstein距离和Sinkhorn迭代对齐粗略的片段边界；3）基于质量重新排序，利用定位精度和语义相关性的复合得分重排预测。

Result: 实验表明，ABRR框架在基准数据集Charades-STA、ActivityNet Captions和QVHighlights上的一致性改进，尤其在严格的IoU阈值（例如，IoU=0.7）上，同时提高了排名性能。

Conclusion: ABRR框架因其计算效率以及与现有模型的兼容性，成为真实世界时间语义检索应用的理想解决方案。

Abstract: Temporal Sentence Grounding (TSG) aims to localize temporal segments in untrimmed videos that correspond to natural language queries, a critical task for video understanding with applications in video retrieval, summarization, and human-computer interaction. Despite significant progress driven by transformer-based models, existing TSG methods suffer from two key limitations: quantization errors due to temporal downsampling, which misalign segment boundaries, and suboptimal ranking of predictions based on semantic matching rather than localization precision. To address these challenges, we propose the Adaptive Boundary Refinement and Ranking (ABRR) framework, a novel, model-agnostic post-processing approach that enhances the performance of any TSG model without retraining. ABRR integrates three innovative components: (1) Temporal Distribution Smoothing, which mitigates noise in predicted score distributions using Gaussian kernel convolution; (2) Boundary Refinement via Optimal Transport, which leverages the 2-Wasserstein distance and Sinkhorn iterations to align coarse segment boundaries with true temporal moments, grounded in Monge-Kantorovich optimal transport theory; and (3) Quality-Driven Re-Ranking, which reorders predictions using a composite score balancing localization precision and semantic relevance. By correcting quantization errors and prioritizing accurately localized segments, ABRR achieves substantial improvements over state-of-the-art TSG models. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets, including Charades-STA, ActivityNet Captions, and QVHighlights, demonstrate consistent gains at stringent IoU thresholds (e.g., IoU=0.7), alongside enhanced ranking performance. The framework's computational efficiency and compatibility with existing models make it a versatile and impactful solution for real-world TSG applications.

</details>


### [34] [Uni-Loc: Weakly Supervised 3D CT Pathology Localization via Spatially Grounded Vision-Language Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13330)
*Hao-Chih Lee,Zahi A. Fayad,Xueyan Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Uni-Loc, a vision-language framework, achieves high accuracy in 3D pathology localization and classification on five tasks, outperforming existing methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D CT scan localization of abnormalities is crucial for clinical applications. Existing methods have limitations in either localization or classification accuracy.

Method: Uni-Loc uses a vision-language framework to link pathology classification and 3D localization. It employs a token-to-voxel upsampling module and gradient-guided thresholding to achieve high-resolution localization and identify pathology regions.

Result: Uni-Loc demonstrates macro-averaged voxel-level AUROC/AUPR of 96.1% / 34.5%, outperforming relevance propagation and gradient saliency. It also shows improved classification AUROC on CT-RATE and RAD-ChestCT datasets.

Conclusion: Uni-Loc effectively performs 3D pathology localization and classification without requiring pathology-specific voxel-level supervision.

Abstract: Accurate localization of abnormalities in three-dimensional (3D) CT scans is essential for linking automated predictions with clinically relevant pathological findings. We propose Uni-Loc, a vision-language framework that unifies pathology classification and 3D localization using scan-level supervision. Uni-Loc derives both predictions and localization maps from spatial similarities between pathology-conditioned queries and image features.
