TL;DR: 该研究在多种应用场景中评估了人类与AI协作的计数方法，发现现代多模态模型作为起点效果良好，而微调检测器在大规模标注和重复部署时更有优势。通过模型预测优先标注提高了效率，且模型排名质量可通过小样本集估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献旨在解决在大量图像集合中估计目标物体总数的科学和政策决策问题，强调在不同领域通过人类与AI协作的计数方法的选择指导。

Method: 研究人员使用了人类与AI协作的共同框架，在生物医学显微镜学、地面和航空野生动物调查以及城市建筑上空图像等12个任务中进行评估。

Result: 研究发现，现代多模态模型在很多任务中提供了强大的起点，微调检测器在大规模标注和重复部署时更有优势。使用模型预测优先标注提升了效率，模型排名质量可以通过小样本集估计。

Conclusion: 该研究为新任务中的模型、估算器和适应策略选择提供了实用指导，证实了在实际应用场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Many scientific and policy decisions require estimating the total number of target objects across a large image collection. Exhaustive human labeling is often infeasible, while raw model predictions can be biased, especially under domain shift. Recent human-AI estimation methods combine imperfect model predictions with limited human verification to obtain reliable collection-level estimates. However, existing work provides little guidance on how practitioners should choose among models, estimators, and adaptation strategies for a new deployment. We study these choices within a common human-in-the-loop counting framework across 12 tasks spanning biomedical microscopy, ground-level and aerial wildlife surveys, and overhead imagery of urban structures. Our results show that modern multimodal models provide a strong, training-free starting point for many tasks, while fine-tuned detectors become more advantageous at larger labeling budgets and across repeated deployments. Estimation efficiency improves further when model predictions are used to prioritize which units humans label, rather than sampling uniformly. We also find that each model's ranking quality can be estimated with a small pilot set, yielding practical guidance for selecting strategies on new counting tasks. In a field deployment on aerial pronghorn surveys, a task-specific adaptation of these methods meets the survey's $\pm 5\%$ tolerance after reviewing a median of only $3.2\%$ of collected images.

</details>


### [108] [OnlyAct: Extracting Action Representations from Foundation Models for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14420)
*Runjia Li,Minghao Chen,Junyu Xie,Philip Torr,Andrea Vedaldi,Tomas Jakab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需动作标注的对比适应框架，用于从预训练的视频模型中提取动作信息，改进了世界模型的动作表达性、场景不变性和新场景中的行为生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的世界模型在训练过程中受限于稀少且不兼容的动作标注信息，本文提出的方法旨在克服这一问题，通过利用预训练的模型来提取动作信息，从而提高模型的泛化能力。

Method: 本文介绍了一种无标签对比适应框架，该框架能够在不依赖动作标注的情况下，通过对比相似动作的不同场景帧对和不同动作的同一场景帧对，来调整预训练空间的几何结构，以提取动作信息，而不需要设置重建瓶颈。

Result: 实验结果表明，与无标注的潜在动作方法相比，本文提出的方法在动作表达性和场景不变性上具有显著优势，生成的模型能在新场景中更准确地转移所展示的动作。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法为无需标注动作标签的世界模型训练提供了一种有效的新策略，能够显著提高动作表达性和场景的不变性。

Abstract: Video world models promise general-purpose interactive simulators, but their training is limited by action supervision: action labels are scarce, fragmented across incompatible specifications, and do not scale with the unlabeled video pretraining corpus. A common workaround jointly trains an action encoder to compress consecutive frames into a latent action and a decoder to reconstruct the next frame from the previous frame and that latent, forcing the latent to capture only new information. This has two limitations: learning the action space from scratch can limit generalization to unseen domains, and the bottleneck trades expressiveness for separation, with tighter settings losing nuance and looser ones risking scene leakage under domain shifts. We argue for a different starting point: rather than learning actions from scratch, we start from a pretrained foundation model (V-JEPA 2), whose embeddings of a frame pair already carry substantial action information after large-scale, diverse video pretraining. The remaining challenge is to extract the action while discarding the scene. We propose a label-free contrastive adaptation framework that pulls together frame pairs sharing the same motion under a different scene and pushes apart pairs from the same scene with different motion, reshaping the geometry of the pretrained space without imposing a reconstruction bottleneck. We introduce a unified evaluation protocol along three axes (action expressiveness, scene invariance, and action-conditioned video generation quality) across robot manipulation, camera navigation, game, and driving benchmarks, most of which are unseen during adaptation. Compared with annotation-free latent-action methods, our representations are substantially more expressive while matching their scene invariance, and they yield world models that transfer demonstrated actions to new scenes more faithfully.

</details>


### [109] [Metric-Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization for Explainable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14436)
*Luka Dragar,Peter Peer,Vitomir Štruc,Borut Batagelj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种分离预测-解释管道，即先使用视觉基础模型进行分类，然后使用Qwen3-VL-8B模型生成带有决策的图像证据解释，对于伪造图像，第二个Qwen3-VL-8B模型生成简洁的非专家总结，同时通过GRPO优化解释模型，最终在复杂BERTScore-F1和综合评估中名列前茅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的联合视觉-语言模型在鉴别伪造内容时存在不足，经常为篡改过的图像生成真实的叙述。这个研究旨在提高可解释的深度伪造检测能力，从而在实际应用中提供对预测结果的合理解释。

Method: 该研究设计了一个分离的预测-解释管道。首先使用视觉基础模型进行分类，然后由Qwen3-VL-8B模型生成基于决策的图像证据解释。对于伪造图像，该过程进一步生成简洁的非专家总结。同时，通过一种新的优化方法，即匹配条件的GRPO，来优化解释模型。

Result: 该方法在Complex BERTScore-F1评分上提高了复杂BertScore-F1分，从0.686提升至0.712，并且在指标对接时也提升了定位（实体和证据F1）。这项研究最终在复杂BERTScore-F1排名首位，在所有项目中排名第七。

Conclusion: 通过改进的分离预测-解释管道及优化方法，该研究显著提高了深度伪造检测的可解释性，在多项评估指标中表现出色。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors are commonly evaluated on real/fake classification alone, but real-world applications require explanations of the visual evidence behind each prediction. Joint vision-language models struggle with authenticity assessment, producing "real" narratives for manipulated images. To address this, we propose a decoupled predict-then-explain pipeline as our submission to the ACM Multimedia 2026 Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge, with methods trained and evaluated on the XPlainVerse benchmark. A vision-foundation-model ensemble first classifies the image, then a Qwen3-VL-8B model generates a decision-conditioned explanation of the visual evidence. For fake images, a second Qwen3-VL-8B compresses that text into a short non-expert summary without using the image. We further apply metric-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to optimize both explainers against the challenge criteria. Under matched conditions, GRPO raises complex BERTScore-F1 from 0.686 to 0.712 and also improves grounding (entity and evidence F1); our submission ranked first on complex BERTScore-F1 and seventh overall.

</details>


### [110] [Textual Response-Aware Guidance for Zero-Shot Adversarial Robustness of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40309)
*Yubo Cui,Xianchao Guan,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析了视觉表示漂移的限制，提出了Textual Response-Aware Guidance (TRAG) 方法，该方法使用预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部变化的响应来约束视觉表示漂移，从而在保持预训练图像-文本关系的同时提升鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性微调方法依赖预训练模型指导以保持图像-文本对齐并提升鲁棒性，但存在局限性。研究旨在通过提出TRAG来改进这些方法，解决预训练图像-文本关系的保持问题。

Method: TRAG方法通过利用预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部变化的响应，构建锚定级别的约束条件，然后使用预训练图像-锚相关性进行聚合，形成图像自适应约束。

Result: 实验结果显示，TRAG方法在多种架构和任务上表现出先进的干净和鲁棒性能。进一步分析表明，该方法有助于保持预训练的图像-文本关系。

Conclusion: TRAG方法通过改进视觉表示漂移的约束，提升了VLMs的鲁棒性，并保持了预训练的图像-文本关系，展示了在对抗性攻击下的优越性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot generalization but remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Existing adversarial fine-tuning methods often use pre-trained model guidance to preserve image--text alignment while improving robustness. Our analysis of visual representation drift reveals complementary limitations of two representative forms of guidance. Matching visual features constrains all directions equally, providing broad coverage without semantic emphasis. Matching prediction distributions prioritizes semantically relevant directions, but remains limited by the candidate texts and prediction probabilities. These limitations can hinder preservation of pre-trained image--text relationships and degrade zero-shot performance. We therefore propose Textual Response-Aware Guidance (TRAG), which constrains visual representation drift using the pre-trained text encoder's responses to local variations in input embeddings. TRAG constructs anchor-level constraints from the response directions and magnitudes around semantic anchors, then aggregates them using pre-trained image--anchor relevance, yielding an image-adaptive constraint. Extensive experiments across architectures and tasks demonstrate state-of-the-art clean and robust performance. Further analysis shows that TRAG helps preserve pre-trained image--text relationships.

</details>


### [111] [UGG: Unified Generative Geometry Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14440)
*Amani Kiruga,Zhiyi Li,Ruojin Cai,Hansen Lillemark,Xiaoshan Wu,Ayush Tewari,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UGG是一种统一的4D场景理解框架，它通过生成模型联合建模RGB视频和几何表示，提供一种灵活的方式来处理多样化的、异构的数据集，并在推断时搜索多个可能的场景假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的4D场景理解方法分为直接几何预测的前馈模型和用于视觉合成的生成模型。而UGG通过联合建模RGB视频和几何信息来解决4D场景理解问题，从而克服了现有方法中的局限性。

Method: UGG采用生成模型，通过调整不同模态和时间中的噪声级别，将视觉生成和几何预测转化为条件完成任务。该模型能够处理具有不同子集的几何和视觉注释的多样化数据集，并在推断时利用从观察到的RGB帧中提取的几何证据来改进几何预测。

Result: UGG在多种场景下（包括受过挑战的稀疏观测条件下）展现了优于非统一基线方法的效果，特别是在相机控制视频生成、深度估计和相机姿态预测方面。

Conclusion: UGG提供了一种新的方法来解决4D场景理解的问题，通过生成模型技术实现了RGB视频与几何信息的有效联合建模，展示了其在多种任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Understanding 4D scenes is a core challenge in visual intelligence, requiring models to jointly capture scene geometry, appearance, and dynamics from partial observations. However, existing approaches are subdivided into feedforward models for direct geometric prediction, and generative models for visual synthesis. We introduce UGG, a unified framework for 4D scene understanding that jointly models RGB video and geometric representations such as depth and camera pose. By assigning different noise levels across modalities and time, UGG formulates visual generation and geometric prediction as conditional completion within a single generative model. This flexible formulation enables the model to learn from diverse, heterogeneous datasets with different subsets of geometric and visual annotations. Furthermore, our generative formulation enables inference-time search over multiple plausible scene hypotheses, using geometric evidence extracted from observed RGB frames to improve geometric prediction without ground truth geometry. We demonstrate UGG's effectiveness over non-unified baselines across camera-controlled video generation, depth estimation, and camera pose prediction, including in challenging sparse observation settings. Code and visualizations are available at https://ugg-model.github.io/

</details>


### [112] [A Lightweight Differentiable Method for Real-Time Skin Rendering and Digitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14451)
*Mario Marquez-Balduque,Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Jorge Lopez-Moreno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接估计适用于实时渲染管线的皮肤下散射参数的方法，通过不同iable屏幕空间下散射（SSSS）框架优化空间变化的底纹和散射距离，同时使用低成本单光源单视角设置进行捕获，保证了不同肤色人群的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前逆渲染技术依赖于离线路径追踪来估计物理属性，这些属性难以直接应用于游戏引擎等交互式应用程序。因此，本文开发了直接估计适用于实时渲染的皮肤下散射参数的方法，填补了这一空白。

Method: 本文提出了一种可微屏幕空间下散射（SSSS）框架，通过联合优化空间变化的底纹和散射距离来估计皮肤下散射参数。同时，将单光源单视角捕捉设置用于获取可访问且实际的外观。优化过程使用带蒙版的损失函数，瞄准下散射效果显著的区域。

Result: 结果表明，本文的方法可以从轻量级输入中有效地恢复散射参数，产生与标准实时着色器直接兼容的真实皮肤外观。

Conclusion: 本文的方法为低成本、单光源和单视角设置提供了真实皮肤显示参数的估计，适用于实时渲染引擎，经过不同肤色人群的实际数据和合成数据的验证。

Abstract: Inverse rendering for skin typically relies on offline path tracing to estimate physical properties, which fail to map directly to the approximate shaders utilized in interactive applications, such as game engines. We bridge this gap by directly estimating subsurface scattering parameters tailored for real-time rendering pipelines. We introduce a differentiable Screen-Space Subsurface Scattering (SSSS) framework that enables the joint optimization of spatially varying base textures and scattering distances. To account for local variations in skin thickness, we estimate these parameters across distinct facial regions, generating a blended profile map ready for production runtime engines. Our pipeline utilizes a low-cost, single-light, single-view capture setup designed for accessible and practical appearance acquisition. Optimization is driven by a masked loss function targeting regions where subsurface scattering effects are dominant. We validate our approach on a real-world dataset of $45$ individuals across the Monk Skin Tone Scale, ensuring demographic generalization. Furthermore, we complement this evaluation with synthetic data, a multi-light setup validating our single-light assumption, and a tilted-view configuration confirming angular consistency. Results demonstrate that our method effectively recovers scattering parameters from lightweight inputs, yielding realistic skin appearance directly compatible with standard real-time shaders.

</details>


### [113] [C$^2$-Routing: Complementary Cropping and Compression for Efficient Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14469)
*Guorui Lu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C$^2$-Routing 通过自适应地选择裁剪或压缩策略，并引入安全门机制以保护准确性，进一步减少了 VQA 过程中的计算量和视觉令牌使用，同时保持了任务得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决视觉问答(VQA)中对视觉-语言模型(VLMs)的大量计算需求，本文提出了一种模型无关的输入减少方法C$^2$-Routing，可以在广泛的应用场景和模型中使用，即使在黑盒设置下也能有效减少计算成本。

Method: C$^2$-Routing 方法根据输入图像和问题自适应选择裁剪或压缩，同时设计了一个安全门机制根据减少输入下的预测联合正确性分布来指导回退，以保持准确性。

Result: 在三个 VLMs 和 18 个涵盖局部细节、全局场景、场景文字和文档的数据集上，C$^2$- Routing 方法将端到端 FLOPs 减少了 36.53%，视觉令牌减少了 42.36%，平均任务分数下降了 0.77%。

Conclusion: C$^2$-Routing 方法证明了其有效性，能在保持 VQA 准确性的同时，显著减少计算要求，适用于各种视觉语言模型。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) imposes substantial computational overhead on vision-language models (VLMs), motivating model-agnostic input reduction methods that can be readily applied across diverse VLMs, including in black-box settings. Simply enforcing a visual-token cap reduces computation, but it can discard information needed to answer the question. A single reduction strategy also struggles to accommodate different scenarios. Our key observation is that cropping and compression are complementary: cropping highlights small targets and local details, whereas whole-image compression better preserves context for large targets or distributed evidence. Based on this observation, we propose C$^2$-Routing, which adaptively selects cropping or compression according to the input image and question. Since both strategies may still discard key information, we further design a safety gate that uses the predicted joint correctness distribution under original and reduced inputs to guide fallback and protect accuracy. To accommodate consumer GPU resource constraints, all training and evaluation experiments run on RTX 3090 GPUs. To measure gains beyond token capping, we use full-image baselines with the same cap of 1,024 visual tokens. We conduct extensive evaluation on three VLMs and 18 datasets spanning local details, global scenes, scene text, and documents. With the visual budget already capped, C$^2$-Routing further reduces end-to-end FLOPs by 36.53% and visual tokens by 42.36% on average, with a mean relative task-score decrease of only 0.77%.

</details>


### [114] [BoviShift: A Six-Year Multi-Attribute Audit of Visual Cattle Phenotyping Across Animals and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14476)
*Mobasshir Bhuiya Shagor,Nilambar Halder Tonmoy,Md Saef Ullah Miah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过引入BoviShift数据集，涵盖2021至2026年的六年连续牛群市场信息，评估了不同的数据分割策略对不同指标的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过计算机视觉的方法对牲畜进行非接触式评估，可以提高效率和准确性。然而，由于图像和视频帧之间的关联性，这带来的可靠性评估挑战。为此，研究人员构建了BoviShift数据集，并探讨了各种数据分割策略以提高牲畜特征估计的可靠性。

Method: 该研究采用了固定ResNet-50多任务基准模型，并通过两种数据分割策略评估了五个属性：体重、年龄、高度、品种和毛色。研究还测试了单种子集、单个视角聚合和视频帧聚合对性能的影响。

Result: 不同数据分割策略在不同任务上的表现有差异，尤其是品种识别任务。未来年份的表现具有特定属性和群体的依赖性。在2025年，平均体重和年龄无法超越基线；但在2025年，高度表现优于基线；毛色表现从2025年过渡到2026年有所下降。多任务训练和零样本视频帧聚合显示了模型在数据域间的局限。

Conclusion: BoviShift数据集为连续年度的牛群视觉信息整合提供了最长的时间跨度，并且展示了未来的牲畜特征估评估中的挑战。

Abstract: Computer vision offers a scalable, non-contact means of estimating livestock traits, but correlated images and video frames make reliable evaluation difficult. Image-random splitting can place views of the same animal in both training and test sets, while deployment further requires transfer to animals captured in future years. We introduce BoviShift, a corpus reconstructed from six consecutive annual cohorts of an operational cattle marketplace collected from 2021 to 2026. It contains 2{,}657 cattle, 10{,}610 still images, and 2{,}566 walk-around videos across nine recorded breed categories. We evaluate three splitting strategies through four configurations: image-random and pooled cattle-disjoint splits over the same development cohorts, followed by separate future-2025 and locked future-2026 evaluations. Using a fixed ResNet-50 multi-task baseline, we assess weight, age, height, breed, and coat color. Differences between image-random and cattle-disjoint splitting are task-dependent rather than a universal leakage penalty. Future-cohort behavior is also attribute- and cohort-specific: breed declines relative to pooled evaluation; mean weight and age performance fails to beat constant baselines in 2025; height remains above baseline; and color transfers well to 2025 but degrades in 2026. Multi-task training improves all five point estimates in a single-seed ablation, still-view aggregation helps selectively, and zero-shot video-frame aggregation exposes a still-to-video domain gap. To our knowledge, BoviShift is the longest-spanning cattle-vision resource combining consecutive annual cohorts with physical and visual attributes.

</details>


### [115] [RS-JEPA: Remote Sensing Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture with Scan-Path Views for Cross-Sensor Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14496)
*Tanishq Rachamalla,Srishti Kaushik,Muhammad Attique Khan,Gustau Camps-Valls,Swalpa Kumar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RS-JEPA 提出了一种紧凑的联合嵌入预测框架，利用 Vision Mamba 编码器在不同传感器的遥感图像间进行检索，该方法通过引入扫描路径视图和对抗视图耦合优化了检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法存在局限性，如需重建特定传感器的像素细节、仅与图像的配准双胞胎对齐或依赖于大型变压器模型。为此，RS-JEPA 旨在提供一种参数量小、性能优越的解决方案。

Method: RS-JEPA 利用Vision Mamba编码器开发了联合嵌入预测框架。通过引入扫描路径视图和对抗视图耦合，优化图像阅读顺序作为自监督视图，从而改善跨传感器遥感图像检索性能。

Result: 在 SAR-光学 (BEN-14K, CBRSIR-VS) 和 Panchromatic-多光谱 (DSRSID) 数据集上，RS-JEPA 的指标已超越十二种重实现的方法，特别是在 F1@5 上达到最高性能。其网络参数量仅为 3.88M（训练时为 14.57M），远少于先前的 JEPA 方案。

Conclusion: RS-JEPA 通过有效的自监督学习框架，显著提高了跨不同传感器的遥感图像检索能力，具有良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Cross-sensor remote sensing image retrieval aims to find scenes with the same content across heterogeneous sensors, such as SAR and optical or panchromatic (PAN) and multispectral (MS) imagery, without manual labels. Existing self-supervised methods either reconstruct sensor-specific pixel detail, align each image only with its co-registered twin, or rely on large transformers with 118-173M parameters (and up to 398M). We introduce RS-JEPA, a compact joint-embedding predictive framework built on a Vision Mamba encoder shared by both sensors. Since a state-space encoder reads an image as an ordered sequence of patches, we propose scan-path views, which treat two reading orders of the same image as two views with identical pixels and predict the hidden patches in one order from the visible patches in the other.To keep this prediction challenging, we propose Adversarial View Coupling (AVC), which learns the hardest split of the visible patches, and a neighbourhood consensus loss that aligns the scene similarities of both sensors. Extensive experiments on SAR-optical (BEN-14K, CBRSIR-VS) and PAN-MS (DSRSID) benchmarks demonstrate that RS-JEPA achieves the highest F1@5 among twelve re-implemented methods (72.92, 80.35 and 94.43) with a retrieval network of only 3.88M parameters (14.57M in training, 8-16times fewer than prior JEPA retrievers). Like prior methods, RS-JEPA trains on co-registered pairs. Our results highlight reading order as a source of self-supervised views. code: https://github.com/Tanishq251/RSJEPA.

</details>


### [116] [Measuring Robustness and Efficiency in a Connectome-Constrained Fly Visual System Model: Linking an Adversarial and a Perceptual Illusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14498)
*Alexandre Garcia-Duran,Alejandro Rodriguez-Garcia,Manuel Molano-Mazón,Alexandre Hyafil,Srikanth Ramaswamy,Janne K. Lappalainen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过将受果蝇视觉系统启发的网络（FlyNet）与多个人工神经网络模型进行比较，在一项碰撞检测任务中评估了其性能。尽管FlyNet对对抗性攻击更易受攻击，但它在计算资源有限的情况下更具有抗常见视频干扰的能力。详细连接组约束的模型在大多数测试的扰动下仍比FlyNet更稳健，表明结构约束在效率而非鲁棒性方面具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者旨在探讨受生物神经系统启发的神经网络在计算和能源效率方面的潜在优势，同时评估其对各种干扰和对抗性攻击的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用了一个可微分3D渲染管道，比较了FlyNet与其他几种模型（包括CNNs、RAFT、sCTRNN、CTRNN）在碰撞检测任务上的表现。

Result: FlyNet虽然对对抗性扰动是不稳健的，但由于计算量更小，其在处理常见视频干扰时更具鲁棒性。而详细连接组约束的模型则展示了更稳定的性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，连接组约束模型在鲁棒性方面有所提升，但这主要体现在对抗性攻击的抗性上，并非所有类型的干扰都具有优异表现，而生物和神经网络的低级别运动统计相似性揭示了易受攻击的机制。

Abstract: Biological visual systems compute reliably under tight metabolic and wiring constraints, whereas artificial vision systems consume far more energy and can be sensitive to adversarial perturbations. Whether networks with brain-like wiring are more robust at lower computational cost is unclear. We address this by benchmarking a task-optimized, continuous-time recurrent neural network of the fly visual system constrained by its synaptic connectome (FlyNet) against artificial neural networks. We compare it against CNNs, RAFT, and continuous-time recurrent neural networks without detailed connectome constraints (sCTRNN, CTRNN) on a collision-detection task within a differentiable 3D rendering pipeline. FlyNet is adversarially vulnerable but, relative to its compute, more robust to common video corruptions than RAFT, while using up to five orders of magnitude fewer FLOPs. The CTRNNs remain more robust than FlyNet across almost all tested perturbations, suggesting that connectome constraints benefit efficiency, through their low edge count, over robustness. Recurrence improves robustness to white-box attacks in RAFT and temporal context improves robustness to common corruptions. FlyNet's position and velocity representations degrade under adversarial textures but recover under counterfactual textures that restore correct classification while staying equally far from training, ruling out unfamiliar textures as the cause of failure. The reverse-phi illusion similarly disrupts these representations and inverts the same low-level motion statistic as the adversarial textures. Optimizing this input statistic alone, without access to the network, suffices to induce classification failure, attributing both perturbations to the same circuit vulnerability. Connectome-constrained models can thus surface adversarial failure mechanisms as testable hypotheses about biological perceptual vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [117] [Toward Foundation Detection Models via Example-Based Grounding in EM-DETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14518)
*Sheethal Bhat,Bogdan Georgescu,Awais Mansoor,Florin C. Ghesu,Sasa Grbic,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的多域基础模型方法，通过整合具有不同注释领域的医学图像数据集，采用学生-教师训练策略和基于示例的联合学习框架，实现跨模态的细粒度检测。该模型在CXR和乳腺X光图像领域达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于医学领域标注数据集稀缺且标准不一，现有的CAD系统难以开发出鲁棒的基础模型。为了克服这一挑战，该研究提出了一种结合不同疾病领域标注数据集的新方法，旨在促进跨模态的细粒度检测和大规模应用。

Method: 研究采用了学生-教师训练策略来结合不同标签空间的数据集，以缓解灾难性遗忘并提升特征学习。此外，基于之前提出的基于示例的EM-DETR框架，研究人员将其调整为可以从多个特定领域数据集中联合学习，实现跨模态的细腻检测。

Result: 该研究提出的方法在CXR和乳腺X光图像识别中在图像级别AUC得分上比之前SOTA提高了1个百分点，并在CXR和乳腺X光图像领域的mAP50上取得领先结果，超越了之前单一数据集训练的EM-DETR 2-3个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个综合不同医学图像数据集的方法，构建了一个多域基础模型，在跨模态检测任务上达到了业界领先的效果，为利用异质数据同时保持稳健性提供了一种有希望的解决方案。

Abstract: DeepLearning (DL)-based Computer-Aided Diagnostic (CAD) systems have shown great promise in supporting clinicians, but their performance heavily depends on the availability of large, well-annotated datasets. In medical lesion detection, the scarcity of comprehensive, stan dardized annotations and the heterogeneity of existing datasets limit the development of robust foundation models. In this work, we introduce a novel approach to building a multi-domain foundation model for le sion detection by integrating diverse medical image datasets annotated for both anatomical structures and abnormalities. We first propose a student-teacher training strategy to effectively combine datasets with heterogeneous label spaces, mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and im proving feature learning. In addition, building on our previously pro posed example-based EM-DETR framework, we adapt it to jointly learn from multiple domain-specific datasets, enabling fine-grained, example driven detection across modalities. Our model achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) results, with image-level AUC scores exceeding previous SOTA by 1% point on four key findings in CXRs. We achieve a leading mAP50 in CXRs and mammography, surpassing EM-DETR trained on a single dataset by 2-3% points. This approach paves the way for scalable foundation models in medical imaging that leverage heterogeneous data while maintaining robust and generalizable performance.

</details>


### [118] [Medical Image Segmentation: Foundation Model Adaptation versus Task-Specific Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14519)
*Emre Kutlusan,Yongmin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比基础模型（如DINOv2）的适应策略与现成任务模型在跨机构数据上的表现，发现适应后的基础模型在外部数据集上的泛化能力更强。只有少量基础模型需要适应，并且所需的适应容量取决于成像领域而非适应方法。同时，研究指出广泛使用的息肉测试集包含重复的另一基准的数据集中的图像，因此基础模型适应在跨机构应用场景中是合理的，适应容量应基于目标领域选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学图像分割领域使用的基础模型虽然性能优越，但现有比较实验多局限于测试集数据集且未充分验证模型在新型临床场地的表现”，目的是探讨不同基础模型适应策略的效果，进一步证明基础模型在跨机构场景下的适用性，并为适应策略的选择提供依据。

Method: 本文采用了一种名为DINOv2的基础模型，并使用了从轻微调（仅调整偏差项）到全微调的不同策略，进行了对比实验。同时，将基础模型与从零开始训练的UNet和ResUNet模型进行了比较。研究选取了结肠镜、乳腺超声和皮肤活检三种图像数据，并在训练源数据集外以及不同医疗机构的数据集上进行了测试。

Result: 实验结果显示，经过一定程度适应的基础模型在外部数据集上的泛化能力优于专门针对任务训练的模型。但在训练分布内的表现，特定任务的现成模型仍然具有竞争力，尤其是在丰富的训练数据下。基础模型虽然在某些情况下能更准确地界定边界，但更可能忽略小型病变。此外，广泛使用的息肉测试集被发现在重复其他基准数据集的图像。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，在需要基础模型在跨机构环境中发挥性能的场景下，适应基础模型是必要的。适应后的基础模型表现出更好的泛化能力，其中的适应程度取决于成像领域，而非具体的适应方法。

Abstract: Foundation models pretrained on large natural-image collections are increasingly adapted for medical image segmentation, in the expectation that they will replace models trained for each task. Most comparisons, however, use data from the training distribution and a single task-specific baseline whose configuration is rarely justified, and therefore provide limited evidence of performance at new clinical sites. It also remains unclear how much of a foundation model must be adapted, and what determines this. We adapted a foundation model, DINOv2, with strategies ranging from tuning only its bias terms to full fine-tuning, and compared it with U-Nets and ResU-Nets trained from scratch. Experiments covered colonoscopy, breast ultrasound and dermoscopy, with testing on held-out data from the training source and on independent datasets from other institutions. The adapted foundation model generalised better on every external dataset. Within the training distribution, the task-specific models remained competitive when training data were abundant, and delineated boundaries more precisely, while the foundation model more often missed small lesions. Only a small fraction of the foundation model required adaptation, and the capacity needed was determined by the imaging domain rather than by the adaptation method. A widely used polyp test set was also found to duplicate images from another benchmark. Foundation-model adaptation is therefore warranted where models must work across sites, with capacity selected on the target domain.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [From Relevant Posts to Attributable Spans: Explicit Symptom Evidence for Depression Questionnaire Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12900)
*Sumanth Manduru,Carlotta Domeniconi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SREE 提出了一种基于证据的方法，用于从社交媒体历史中提取与抑郁症状相关的特定文本片段，选择最具代表性的子集，并在此基础上进行条件推断，从而在提高精确度和召回率的同时，保留了较少的帖子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于语义相似性的方法可能引入误导性的或非归属的证据，特别是在多症状帖子和他人经历讨论中。SREE的目标是提供一种更可靠的方法，用于从社交媒体中识别和推理抑郁症状的相关证据。

Method: SREE采用数据本地化和基于证据的方法。首先，它提取了第一人称和症状特定的文本片段。然后，使用设施定位启发式算法选择最具代表性的子集。最后，通过证据门控推理进行症状推断。

Result: 与最先进的检索方法相比，SREE在帖子级别的精确度提高了70%，F1提高了59%，并且保留的帖子数量至少比这些方法少30%。在多症状句子上，即使检索预算加倍的情况下，SREE也优于密集检索。在所有三个eRisk数据集上，SREE在精确项级别上达到了最高的精确度，同时在顺序和汇总指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: SREE 的成功表明，通过本地化归属证据和控制其组成，可以提高抑郁问卷响应推理的准确性。

Abstract: Longitudinal social-media histories offer an opportunity to assess depression, potentially supporting the inference of standardized depression questionnaire responses. However, this task requires evidence that is semantically relevant to a symptom and attributable to the user, while being sufficiently specific to support an individual questionnaire item. Existing approaches rely on semantic similarity to retrieve posts, which can introduce misleading or non-attributable evidence, particularly in multi-symptom posts and discussions of others' experiences. We propose SREE (Symptom Retrieval and Evidence-Grounded InfErence), an evidence-grounded pipeline that extracts first-person and symptom-specific text spans, selects a representative subset using a Facility Location-inspired objective, and performs Evidence-Gated Inference over the selected evidence. We evaluate evidence retrieval on BDI-Sen and evidence-conditioned questionnaire inference on eRisk 2019-2021. Compared with state-of-the-art retrieval approaches, SREE achieves relative improvements of 70% in post-level precision and 59% in F1, while retaining at least 30% fewer posts. On multi-symptom sentences, it also outperforms dense retrieval in precision and F1 even with twice the retrieval budget. Across all three eRisk collections, SREE achieves the highest exact item-level agreement while remaining competitive on ordinal and aggregate metrics. These results highlight the importance of localizing attributable evidence and controlling its composition before inferring depression questionnaire responses.

</details>


### [120] [Rethinking in Spikes: Mitigating Hallucinations in MDLMs with Step-Aware Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12901)
*Zhongxing Xu,Zhonghua Wang,Zhe Qian,Shiyan Su,Ming Hu,Xiaocheng Zou,Wei Feng,Mingquan Lin,Yuyin Zhou,Yifan Peng,Hamid Rezatofighi,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StepRefine是一种新的插件式解码策略，允许在出现熵峰值时利用语义上下文实现可靠的去噪。该方法通过在熵峰值步骤使用概率加权嵌入并进行多次连续精炼，同时通过精炼控制器监控分布稳定性来减少不必要的循环，从而显著降低MDLM在多模态基准测试中的幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDLM在迭代去噪过程中表现出整体熵下降趋势，但局部熵尖峰与幻象密切相关。现有方法无法有效识别这些异常步骤，导致在熵尖峰阶段失去了背景的理解和多样性假设。

Method: StepRefine方法的核心在于基于步骤的语义上下文感知切换机制，将离散去噪与潜在细化相结合。具体包括在熵尖峰步骤使用概率加权嵌入存储多样化的推理假设，并通过概率加权嵌入进行多次连续细化。此外，引入一个精炼控制器来监控分布稳定性，减少不必要的循环，并在必要时切换回标准离散去噪。

Result: StepRefine方法在不同MDLM上得到了广泛的实验验证，显著降低了多模态基准测试中的幻觉现象。

Conclusion: StepRefine通过引入语义感知的切换机制，有效提升了MDLM的去噪效果，特别是在处理包含熵尖峰的场景时，显著降低了幻觉现象，是一次重要的技术改进。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal diffusion language models (MDLMs) have exhibited strong global modeling capabilities through iterative refinement. We observe that mask uncertainty follows an overall downward trend during iterative denoising, while a few denoising steps exhibit localized entropy spikes closely associated with hallucinations. We argue that anomalous steps can be identified by measuring the entropy deviation of each denoising step from its neighboring temporal window. We hypothesize that discrete token commitment discards the distributional information at masked positions, limiting the continued interaction between candidate semantics and the context during entropy spikes. With this goal, we present StepRefine, a plug-and-play decoding strategy that leverages semantic context to achieve reliable denoising. The core of our method lies in a step-aware switch between discrete denoising and latent refinement. The model employs probability-weighted embeddings at entropy spike steps to preserve diverse reasoning hypotheses and performs multiple rounds of continuous refinement. Meanwhile, a refinement controller monitors distributional stability to reduce unnecessary loops and switch the model back to standard discrete denoising. Through extensive experiments, StepRefine demonstrates significant hallucination mitigation across different MDLMs on multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [121] [TimeOmni-o1: Bidirectionally Coupling Time Series Foundation Models and LLMs for Multimodal Reasoning and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12902)
*Ziyang Zhang,Shenyi Li,Wen Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TimeOmni-o1 是一种将预训练时间序列基础模型 (TSFM) 和语言模型 (LLM) 双向结合的模型，适用于时间序列理解和预测，MMTR 数据集被构建用于模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的时间序列和文本处理模型在理解和预测方面各有不足，TimeOmni-o1 的设计旨在结合 TsFM 和 LLM 的优势，以提高在两者任务上的表现。

Method: TimeOmni-o1 模型架构包括一个双向连接的预训练时间序列基础模型和语言模型。TSFM 将时间序列数据压缩编码后输入给 LLM，LLM 对时间和文本进行联合推理，然后反馈给 TSFM 进行预测。

Result: TimeOmni-o1 在理解时间序列和预测方面均优于现有模型，特别是在多个领域中表现突出。此外，在未专门优化时间序列任务的基准测试中也表现出色，超越专门的时间序列模型。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了 TimeOmni-o1 这一创新模型，结合了语言模型和时间序列基础模型的能力，显著提升了处理时间序列和文本的综合性表现。

Abstract: Time series from real-world systems are often accompanied by text, such as a report on an event that affects them. Two common tasks on these series are understanding, e.g., answering questions about a series, and forecasting its future values. Both tasks require reasoning over the series and the text together. Moreover, understanding outputs text while forecasting outputs a time series, so serving both requires a model that reads and generates both modalities. Large language models (LLMs) read time series as digit tokens and handle long series poorly. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast well from large-scale pretraining but typically neither read nor generate text. Time series language models add a time series modality to the LLM but forecast on the LLM side, leaving the TSFM's pretrained forecasting ability unused. We present TimeOmni-o1, which couples a pretrained TSFM and an LLM bidirectionally. In one direction, the TSFM encodes the series in compressed form for the LLM, which reasons over it together with the text. In the other, the LLM's reasoning is fed back into the TSFM, which produces the forecast conditioned on it. One model architecture thus reads and generates both series and text, reasons in text, and draws on the TSFM's pretrained forecasting ability. To train it, we build MMTR, a multimodal time series reasoning dataset with annotated reasoning traces, covering both tasks across 12 domains with 500k+ samples. On MMTR, TimeOmni-o1 outperforms existing models on both understanding and forecasting across most domains, as well as the LLM and the TSFM inside it when used individually. The gains carry over to three open benchmarks, where it outperforms the dedicated time series models.

</details>


### [122] [SELD: Detecting Silent Evidence Loss in Context Compression beyond the Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12903)
*Wentao Lei,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个无需训练和标签的 Silent Evidence Loss Detector (SELD)，通过比较压缩前后阅读器模型对未选择答案的偏好变化来检测沉默证据损失。实验表明，SELD 在多种压缩器、压缩率和模型下表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语言模型在处理长输入时会进行上下文压缩，但压缩后保留的证据不足以支持回答，这可能导致信息丢失而答案保持不变的问题。现有的检查方法无法检测到这种问题。

Method: SELD 方法基于当关键证据被移除后，阅读器模型对未选择答案的偏好会发生变化。通过对比压缩前后的偏好变化，可以发现那些现有检查方法无法检测到的证据损失。

Result: 在 SEL-Pairs 基准测试中，SELD 在五种阅读器模型上的表现优于现有强基线，在包含相同答案的配对中检测到移除证据的编辑命中率高达 83%，而最强基线的命中率为 70%。

Conclusion: 研究证明，在压缩后的上下文中，即使答案没有改变，也可以检测到证据丢失的情况，SELD 提供了一种有效的方法来识别和解决这个问题。

Abstract: Context compression is now routine for language models with long inputs, but a compressed context is useful only if it keeps the evidence for the answer. Existing checks compare the reader model's answer or its most likely words, and thus miss a frequent failure, silent evidence loss: key evidence is removed while the answer stays the same, leaving the answer unsupported and risky for other models that reuse the context. To address this challenge, we propose SELD (Silent Evidence Loss Detector), which needs no training and no labels. Its principle is that when evidence is lost, a reader model's preferences among the answers it leaves unsaid shift even if its answer does not, so comparing these preferences before and after compression reveals losses that answer checks cannot see. We also build SEL-Pairs, a benchmark of length-matched evidence and control deletions from three question-answering datasets. Quantitative and qualitative experiments with five reader models on it show that SELD consistently outperforms strong and recent baselines across compressors, compression rates and models, identifying the evidence-removing edit in 83% of matched pairs with unchanged answers, against 70% for the strongest baseline. These results show that evidence loss in compressed contexts can be detected even when the answer is unchanged.

</details>


### [123] [Actionable Feedback: Improving Model Capabilities from User Edit Data on Clinical Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12904)
*Daniel P. Jeong,Yewon Byun,John Giorgi,Peng Xu,Zachary C. Lipton,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种方法来处理大型语言模型在临床文档流程中的编辑数据，以便更准确地进行微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床文档流程中，大型语言模型的编辑数据可能包含不必要的信息，这会导致模型学习错误的模式。

Method: 研究首先开发了临床编辑行为的分类法，然后基于MIMIC-III数据集生成半合成的编辑数据，并引入了简单的过滤方法来识别有效的编辑内容。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于半合成数据集的微调比直接使用原始编辑输出提高了医生一致性指标，同时保持了对输入语境的忠实性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法能够更有效地利用临床编辑数据进行模型微调，提高模型输出的准确性和相关性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed in clinical documentation workflows, where clinicians review and edit their outputs before use. In principle, these clinician edits represent a scalable source of supervision for model training. In practice, not all edits represent actionable feedback: for instance, on data drawn from real-world deployment of an AI clinical documentation product, our review suggests that over 45% of edited outputs contain user-added information that is not directly supported by the inputs to the model. Naively fine-tuning on such edits risks teaching the model to hallucinate. In this paper, we develop a method to address this challenge. Given that real clinician edits on deployed documentation tools are not publicly available, we first develop a taxonomy of clinician-editing behaviors, informed by data from a deployed AI clinical documentation product. Then, we develop a method for generating semi-synthetic clinician-edit data, informed by our taxonomy, on a discharge-summary generation task based on MIMIC-III, a public dataset. Finally, we introduce simple filtering methods for identifying actionable edits, with accuracy evaluated by expert review. We show empirically, on our semi-synthetic dataset, that naive fine-tuning on raw edited output increases ungrounded content, while our approach of progressively filtering toward grounded edits enables improvement on clinician-alignment metrics while retaining faithfulness to the input context.

</details>


### [124] [Brasilidade: Measuring Brazilian Identity for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12906)
*José Roberto Homeli da Silva,Felipe Jorge do Nascimento,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,William Alberto Cruz Castañeda,Marcellus Amadeus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了针对巴西葡萄牙语评价的基准测试方法，称为Brasilidade。此测试涵盖了语言、语法和巴西葡萄牙语变异的标准化任务。结果显示，模型大小不是决定性能的关键因素，一些中型模型在测试中超过了大型模型。尽管有专门针对葡萄牙语的模型，但在评估中并没有表现出色。展示了特定任务等级下的大量细微差别，强调了需要专门针对巴西葡萄牙语的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有的大型语言模型评估方法未能全面涵盖巴西葡萄牙语的特性和挑战，研究者旨在通过Brasilidade方法，提供一种更加符合巴西语言特性的评价工具。

Method: 研究者设计了一个新的评估基准Brasilidade，包括四个不同领域的标准化任务。通过比较不同规模和类型的模型在这些任务上的表现，来全面评估模型在巴西葡萄牙语环境下的应用效果。

Result: 研究结果显示，单纯依赖模型大小来预测其表现是不准确的，部分中型模型表现优于大型模型。特别设计的葡萄牙语模型在某些任务上的表现并未超越普通模型。不同任务的难度差异很大，有些任务对大多数模型来说难度不大，但也有相当复杂的任务让模型难以应对。这表明，针对巴西葡萄牙语的特别基准测试是必要的。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入Brasilidade方法，强调了需要针对巴西葡萄牙语进行更细致的模型评价，为未来进行改进提供了依据。

Abstract: This work presents a new benchmark that aims to address the limitations of current methods for evaluating LLMs in Brazilian Portuguese. We introduced Brasilidade, an assessment methodology comprised of standardized tasks distributed across four different domains, and we are making available the tasks from its first domain, which address nuances of the language, grammar, and variations of Brazilian Portuguese. The results show that model size, by itself, does not determine performance; several medium-sized models outperformed larger models in the protocols evaluated. Specific models for Portuguese, in general, did not perform above average, despite their targeted training. Different task levels revealed significant nuances: while some tasks proved easy for most models, others posed extreme challenges, and some yielded mixed results, with wide variations in performance. This clearly illustrates the need for benchmarks that address the limitations of the models in Brazilian Portuguese, allowing future training cycles to seek to overcome them. Project repository https://github.com/soberania-oficial/brasilidade

</details>


### [125] [IntentTwin: Deconfounding Candidate Support and the Oracle-to-Deployment Gap in Offline Web Behavior Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12918)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Daren Zha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了现有基准测试中的候选支持混淆问题，并提出了支持平衡候选集以解决此问题。进一步展示了目标自免费的双路径提案者，以及其在两个数据集上的性能，表明支持组成在离线评估中至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于识别并解决现有方法可能存在的候选支持混淆问题，以确保离线基准测试结果能够有效预测线上环境的表现。

Method: 研究方法首先定义了支持可辨别率（SIR），从而量化候选集中支持差异带来的影响。接着，通过构造支持平衡的候选集并评估目标自免费的双路径提案系统，展示了如何控制支持组成。

Result: 在经过筛选的OPeRA数据集和Mind2Web数据集上，研究分别展示了支持不平衡和平衡的情况下不同模型的表现差异，特别是在OOV（未见过的词汇）行上的精确度。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，当离线评估结果用于推断实际应用效果时，除了需要保证候选集准确性之外，还应控制支持组成这一因素。

Abstract: Offline web-behavior benchmarks frequently insert the demonstrated action into a finite candidate set and then measure reranking. We show that this protocol can create a candidate-support confound: when the target is outside training support while all distractors are sampled from that support, support membership identifies the gold action without modeling behavior. We formalize this effect with support-identifiability rate (SIR), construct support-balanced candidate sets, and evaluate a target-free dual-path proposer that combines support retrieval with open-vocabulary DOM/LM proposals. On filtered OPeRA (4,282/469/992 train/dev/test steps), the original protocol has SIR 1.0000 on target-OOV rows and a support-only rule reaches 1.0000 OOV Exact@1. Support balancing reduces SIR to 0.0000; the strongest conditional reranker obtains 0.7214 overall Exact@1, 0.7988 on IV rows, and 0.5668 on OOV rows. The target-free dual-path system reaches Proposal Recall@16 0.6915 and end-to-end Exact@1 0.5212, compared with oracle conditional Exact@1 0.7450. On Mind2Web, the same audit yields SIR 0.2380 and an oracle-to-proposal gap of 0.2125. A 200-row semantic audit labels 0.7150 of exact-key OOV targets as semantically novel, 0.2450 as locator/string variation, and 0.0400 as ambiguous (Cohen's $κ=0.8173$). These measurements show why support composition must be controlled alongside candidate accuracy when offline results are used to reason about deployment.

</details>


### [126] [Capacity Allocation at the Source: Sparse Target Optimization for LLM Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12995)
*Dongyao Chen,Jiayun Lei,Zhiyuan Ji,Zhiying Deng,Wei Liu,Xiaobo Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个简单有效的 l1 正则化方法，通过修剪每个知识向量不必要的部分，减少知识间的干扰，从而更好地共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要集中在保留其他知识的参数编辑阶段上，而上游目标隐藏状态的构建也对模型能力空间的分配发挥了重要作用。因此，研究者认为需要进一步关注这一点。

Method: 提出了一个 l1 正则化方法，通过修剪每个知识向量不必要的部分，只保留必要的组件。

Result: 与多种大规模语言模型及基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法有效。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法能够有效减少知识间的干扰，改进知识编辑的效果。

Abstract: Knowledge editing has emerged as a promising approach for efficiently updating embedded knowledge in large language models (LLMs). It first computes an ideal target hidden state that steers the LLM toward the new output, and then edits the model parameters so that the original hidden state is mapped to this target. While most existing methods focus on the second stage of parameter editing in preserving other knowledge, we find that the upstream construction of the target hidden state also plays an important role in allocating the model's limited capacity space. We first establish a theoretical connection between interference in the parameter space and interference between target hidden states, showing that the two are positively correlated. Building on this insight, we propose a simple yet effective $\ell_1$ regularization method that prunes away unnecessary ``tentacles'' of each knowledge vector, retaining only the essential components. With fewer extraneous tentacles, each edit affects less other knowledge, enabling better coexistence. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs and benchmark datasets verify the effectiveness of our method. Code: https://github.com/wintersdragon-c/STO.

</details>


### [127] [khammoon: From a Linguistic Guideline to a Thai Morpheme Segmenter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13012)
*Warit Sirichotedumrong,Adisai Na-Thalang,Chanakan Wittayasakpan,Kritsadha Phatcharoen,Kunat Pipatanakul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种基于形态学的分词方法，将分词粒度细化到khammoon（最小意义单元），旨在消除复合词带来的歧义问题。该方法在部分词性标注、命名实体识别和情感分类等任务中表现良好，同时提供了一个具有高一致性的人工标注准则，并开源了相关资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的分词工具难以处理泰国语中的复合词，导致下游任务表示模糊和不一致。

Method: 文章提出了一个基于形态学的分词方法，强调了khammoon作为最小有意义单元的重要性，并且构建了一套具有高一致性的决定指南，利用大规模语言模型进行标注，并通过人工纠正系统性错误。最后，设计了一个轻量级的100万参数卷积双向LSTM分词器。

Result: 该方法在多种下游任务中展现出了比传统基于单词的基线模型更好的性能，在部分词性标注、命名实体识别和情感分类等任务中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于形态学的分词方法不仅解决了混合词的问题，还提高了模型的表示质量，且未对下游性能产生负面影响，并提供了一套高效的分词工具和资源。

Abstract: Thai is written without spaces between words, making segmentation a foundational step in most Thai NLP pipelines. Existing tools primarily reproduce the word-level conventions of corpora such as BEST; however, because the notion of a "word" in Thai lacks a unified linguistic consensus, these conventions struggle with productive compounding, leading to arbitrary boundaries and ambiguity in downstream representations. Instead, we propose operating at the morphological level, focusing on khammoon, the smallest atomic unit that carries meaning. Segmenting at the morphemic level provides an explicit, consistent morphological representation that eliminates compounding ambiguity. Crucially, this representation benefits downstream learning: models trained on morphemes perform on par with or exceed word-level baselines across part-of-speech tagging, named-entity recognition, and sentiment classification, demonstrating that linguistic consistency improves representation quality without downstream performance penalty. To operationalize this representation, we translate linguistic principles into a formal decision guideline with near-perfect human agreement ($κ\approx 0.99$), scale annotation with an LLM, and correct systematic errors with human labels. Finally, we train khammoon, a lightweight 1M-parameter convolutional BiLSTM segmenter designed for efficient CPU inference, achieving a boundary F1 of 0.992 on out-of-domain text. The guideline, datasets, model, and library are all publicly released.

</details>


### [128] [The Indic Token Tax in 2026: Measuring Tokenizer. Disparity and Its Cost for the Languages of India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13058)
*Johin Johny Arimbur,Ankur Ashit Ghosh,Shervin Sam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究比较了九个公开语言模型的分词器在不同印度语言下的表现，结果显示不同的分词器对同一篇文章的分词结果差异很大，导致不同语言的成本、速度和能容纳的文本量存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估语言模型API的公平性，研究者测量并分析了不同语言模型分词器在处理印度25种语言时的表现差异。

Method: 研究者使用FLORES-200和PHINC的数据集测量了九个公开语言模型分词器在印度25种语言和13,732个希姆林语-英语社交媒体对的表现，并进行了比较分析。

Result: 研究发现，不同语言模型分词器在处理不同语言时，需要的分词数量相差较大，例如在GPT-4o和GPT-4的分词器中，某些语言如奥里亚语需要5.01倍于英语的分词数量，而Sarvam-30B/105B 的分词器在所有语言中都低于2.6倍。同时，分词器在单词内部边界处与语素边界对齐的准确度在35%之内。

Conclusion: 报告结论指出了目前语言模型分词器存在的公平性问题，并提出了一种新的分词器来解决部分问题。

Abstract: Commercial language model APIs bill per token, so a tokenizer that needs more tokens for the same content in one language makes that language more expensive, slower, and able to fit less text in a fixed context window. We measure this disparity for nine public tokenizers, including those of GPT-4, GPT-4o, Qwen2.5, DeepSeek-V3, Mistral-Nemo and four Sarvam models, on all 25 languages of India available in FLORES-200 (2,009 professionally translated sentences aligned with English) and on 13,732 human-translated Hinglish-English social media pairs from PHINC. On GPT-4o's tokenizer the same content needs between 1.57 times (Hindi) and 5.01 times (Odia) the tokens of English, and 13.7 times for Santali in Ol Chiki script; GPT-4's tokenizer needs up to 12.8 times. Sarvam-30B/105B is the only third-party tokenizer that stays below 2.6 times on every language (median 1.53). In an illustrative scenario using official list prices accessed on 5 November 2026 and stated assumptions, a support bot handling 100,000 chats a month on GPT-4o would cost about INR 56,640 in English and INR 102,829 in Marathi; these are estimates, not measured production costs. Against an openly licensed Hindi and Marathi morphological segmentation set, no tokenizer places more than 35% of its word-internal boundaries on morpheme boundaries (boundary F1 at most 0.48). In a companion paper we present a tokenizer that reduces this tax. Code, pinned artifacts and raw token counts are available at https://github.com/HavonAI/indic-token-tax.

</details>


### [129] [A Multi-Word Tokenizer for 204 Languages That Needs Fewer Tokens Than GPT-4o's, Including on English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13059)
*Johin Johny,Ankur Ghosh,Shervin Sam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对印度语言在GPT-4o分词器中的高成本问题，训练了 Havon v3 和 v4 分词器，它们在保持分词质量的同时减少了需要的标记数量，尤其是在英语、印地语和罗曼语系语言上效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度语言在GPT-4o分词器中需要的标记数量显著高于英语，导致更高的成本和更长的处理时间。因此，研究团队开发了新的分词器Havon v3 和 v4，以优化标记需求，降低成本。

Method: 研究团队训练了Havon v3 和 v4 分词器，其中 Havon v3 是一个基于字节的BPE分词器，具有262,144个标记和一个预分词器来累积标记与字母以及无空格书写的脚本字符。Havon v4 在 v3 的基础上添加了一个超BPE（SuperBPE）训练阶段。所有版本都与 GPT-4o 分词器的词汇表大小进行了比较。

Result: Havon v3 在 FLORES-200 devtest 中在25种印度语言和172种204种语言中需要的分词数量比GPT-4o少，仅比其172种语言多。Havon v4 在200,019 词汇量下减少了6.1%的分词数量，而在182种204种语言中减少了更多的分词数量，尤其是在英语、印地语和罗曼语系语言上效果显著。

Conclusion: Havon v5 在Havon v4的基础上进一步优化了代码，并使用15种不同语言进行测试，结果显示在减少标记数量方面取得了更好的效果，尤其是对于英语、印地语和罗曼语系语言。尽管这些是压缩结果，并未测试过使用这些分词器训练的模型，但研究为印度语言在现代语言处理模型中的应用提供了重要的改进方法。

Abstract: Languages of India need 1.6 to 14 times as many tokens as English on GPT-4o's tokenizer for the same content, which makes them more expensive and slower to serve. We train tokenizers from scratch on human-written text in about 200 languages. Havon v3 is a byte-level BPE tokenizer with 262,144 tokens and a pre-tokenizer that keeps combining marks with their letters and chunks scripts written without spaces. On FLORES-200 devtest it needs fewer tokens than GPT-4o's o200k_base on all 25 languages of India (median 0.74 of GPT-4o's count) and on 172 of 204 languages, but 7% more on English. Havon v4 adds a second, multi-word training stage (SuperBPE) to v3. At exactly GPT-4o's vocabulary size of 200,019 tokens, v4 needs 6.1% fewer tokens than GPT-4o on English and fewer on 161 of 204 languages and all 25 languages of India (median 0.74); at the same size, v3 needs 9.4% more on English and wins on 133. With 262,144 tokens, v4 needs 9.6% fewer tokens on English and fewer on 182 of 204 languages, and with 524,288 tokens it needs fewer on 203 of 204. The gains are largest for English, Hindi and the Romance languages; scripts without spaces, such as Khmer, Burmese and Tibetan, gain little and lose subword merges. Havon v5 replaces v4's Python-only code sample with permissively licensed code in 15 languages: on code it needs 1.03 to 1.22 times GPT-4o's tokens, against 1.11 to 1.29 for v4, while keeping all 25 languages of India (median 0.74, all significant under a sentence-level bootstrap) and English (0.94). These are compression results only: we have not yet evaluated a model trained with v4 or v5.

</details>


### [130] [Catch It Where It Happens: Real Time Error Correction During LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13107)
*Rotem Elimelech,Michael Shalyt,Ido Kaminer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种在线确定性验证方法，该方法通过对符号数学等场景中的推理步骤进行实时验证，提高了模型的准确性，增加了6-7%的token使用，而未影响模型的响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型常在处理数学、科学和工程工作时出现错误，且这些错误一旦发生就难以发现和纠正，这影响了模型的可靠性和效率。

Method: 该方法通过将验证步骤嵌入到模型的推理过程中，在不调用额外模型或使用GPU资源的情况下，允许在每一步验证后指出错误并让模型从该位置继续计算。

Result: 在ASyMoB基准测试中，使用三种开放权重模型进行测试，该在线验证方法显著提高了准确率，最高提高了5.6个百分点，即降低了14.9%的错误率。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅提升了模型性能，还为每一款新型语言模型提供了直接收益，并随着更多类型的推理步骤增加验证能力，其优势将更加明显。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trusted with mathematical, scientific and engineering work, yet their mistakes are often confidently written and hard to spot. A single wrong step tends to propagate, making the model waste inference tokens following a false premise. Existing safeguards either check only the final answer and restart from scratch, or rely on a verifier LLM that competes for GPU capacity and slows down response time. We propose online, deterministic verification: many kinds of reasoning steps can be validated by code, so a step can be verified in real time, as it is written, without any model call or GPU use. Whenever a check fails, we point out the mistake and let the model continue from the same spot. We demonstrate this approach on symbolic mathematics, where derivations already contain explicit expressions that a computer algebra system can check in milliseconds. Tested on the ASyMoB benchmark with three open-weights models, our online validation improves accuracy by 1.4-5.6 percentage points, corresponding to a 1.8-14.9% net reduction in incorrect answers, while increasing token usage by only 6-7%. Because the verifier is independent of the model, the approach complements progress in LLMs rather than competing with it: every new model can benefit immediately, and the benefit grows as deterministic checkers extend to more kinds of reasoning steps.

</details>


### [131] [HiCAP: Hierarchical Cultural Adaptation via Personality for Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13207)
*Lingzhi Shen,Xiaohao Cai,Yunfei Long,Imran Razzak,Shoaib Jameel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HiCAP 是一种新的框架，结合了个性特征，用于跨文化对话生成，并通过监督微调和基于指令的调优实现了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对话生成方法在处理文化适应性方面较少关注个性对文化表达和沟通风格的影响。

Method: HiCAP 通过个性条件控制机制，首先与文化基调对齐，然后根据个体个性特征细化语言表达和内容。结合监督微调和基于指令的调优，利用动态指令模板和多智能体演示捕捉文化与个性的多种组合。

Result: 在 CulturalPersonas 和 CPED 上的实验显示，HiCAP 在各种对话生成指标中表现最佳，展示了有效的文化适应性和个性建模。

Conclusion: HiCAP 框架为跨文化对话生成提供了新的解决方案，将文化适应性和个性建模结合，增强了模型的灵活性和实用性。

Abstract: Cultural adaptation in dialogue systems refers to the ability to generate responses that conform to linguistic norms and social expectations across different cultures. However, existing dialogue generation approaches pay limited attention to the role of personality in shaping cultural expression and communication style. We therefore propose HiCAP, a novel framework that integrates personality traits into culturally adaptive dialogue generation. HiCAP employs a personality-conditioned control mechanism that hierarchically modulates response style by first aligning it with the cultural tone and then refining linguistic expression and content according to individual personality attributes. To embed these factors, the model combines supervised fine-tuning and instruction-based tuning, supported by dynamic instruction templates and multi-agent demonstrations to capture diverse combinations of culture and personality. Experiments on CulturalPersonas and CPED show that HiCAP achieves the strongest overall performance across various dialogue generation metrics, demonstrating effective cultural adaptability and personality modelling.

</details>


### [132] [Deletion Sensitivity Is a Property of the Model: A Three-Condition Measurement across Seven Open Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13223)
*Hanzhang Jia,Liheng Zeng,Zhiyu Yang,Yi Gao,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过三种渲染条件、七种开放重量推理模型、三个种子、794个任务和官方检查器进行了直接、盲目的多种子测量，首次量化了多轮工具调用中完整推理历史删除的情况。发现推理历史删除的影响在-4.4到+51.3分之间变化，且删除敏感性是模型级别的属性，不会因部署变化而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理模型在多轮交互中的推理历史删除对任务性能的影响未被直接测量，本文尝试通过实测来填补这一空白。

Method: 本文通过3种渲染条件，7种推理模型，3个种子，794个任务进行多种子测量，最终通过官方检查器进行评估。

Result: 推理历史删除的影响在-4.4到+51.3分之间变化，对于模型重新训练或重新发布的重新测量显示删除敏感性是模型级别的属性，不会因部署变化而变化。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明推理历史删除对任务性能有显著影响，该影响是模型级别的属性，不应作为隐藏的成本而存在，而应作为先验信息进入路由和聚合逻辑中。

Abstract: Reasoning models write a reasoning trace at every turn of a multi-turn tool session, and later turns read this history. Across 94k ShareGPT conversations, 73 percent of user interactions span multiple turns. Upstream providers bind each trace to its issuer, so a request forwarded across issuers arrives with its history deleted, and the task cost of this stripping remains unmeasured. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first direct, blind, multi-seed measurement of full reasoning-history deletion in multi-turn tool calling, through three rendering conditions, seven open-weight reasoning models, three seeds, 794 tasks, and the official checker. The same deletion spans -4.4 to +51.3 points with both ends excluding zero, and for the one model re-dosed from 0 to 100 percent deletion, the reading varies by at most 1.5 points. Deletion sensitivity is a model-level property. The reading follows the model itself and stays constant across deployments, and a retrained or re-released model is a new model that a two-cell screen, one retention run and one deletion run, re-reads for the cost of two runs. Deletion sensitivity is thus a measurable property of the model, and the reading must enter routing and aggregation as prior information rather than persist as an invisible default cost.

</details>


### [133] [What drives the emergence of symbolic mechanisms in large language models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13270)
*Melody Zixuan Li,Taylor Webb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了因素如何驱动大型语言模型中符号机制的生成，通过训练神经网络并改变架构和数据分布，发现数据多样性是驱动符号机制生成的关键因素。这表明架构并不是必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机是理解大型语言模型中符号机制是如何生成的，以及这一过程是否受到特定架构的影响。

Method: 研究使用了从头开始训练的神经网络，并通过改变架构和数据分布来分析符号机制的生成。使用表征、注意力和因果中介分析方法来确认生成的符号机制。

Result: 研究发现，随着数据多样性的增加，符号特征变得更加显著。即使在修改的Transformer架构和多层感知机中也观察到了相同的符号签名，这表明架构的先验偏见不是必要条件。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，数据多样性而非特定架构是促进神经网络中生成符号计算的主要因素。

Abstract: Recent work has identified a set of emergent symbolic mechanisms that support abstract reasoning in large language models, but it remains unclear what factors drive the emergence of these mechanisms. To address this question, we trained neural networks from scratch on abstract sequence tasks, varying both architectural and data distributional factors, and investigated the effects of these factors on the emergence of symbolic mechanisms. Using representational, attentional, and causal mediation analyses, we first confirmed that transformer-based language models trained from scratch on our task developed symbolic mechanisms. We then found that, across multiple axes of data diversity, greater diversity produced stronger symbolic signatures. Surprisingly, the same signatures emerged in modified transformer architectures and even multilayer perceptrons, indicating that architectural inductive biases are not necessary for the development of symbolic mechanisms. We also observed consistent patterns in an entity-attribute binding task. Together, these findings identify data diversity, not architecture, as the primary driver of emergent symbolic computation in neural networks.

</details>


### [134] [Measuring Definition Sensitivity in LLM Annotation of Social Science Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13339)
*Bogdan Mamaev,Qizhou Wang,Christopher Leckie,Matteo Vergani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过使用大型语言模型（LLMs）对同一文本使用不同定义进行标注，发现了定义敏感性和文义底线。结果显示，模型的注释取决于提示定义，因此在报告注释结果时，应同时报告所用的定义和模型的敏感性和文义底线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本标注方法通常是耗时且难以扩展的，而利用LLMs进行标注可以提高效率。然而，不同定义的文本标注结果可能不同，这表明模型的注释依赖于提示定义，因此需要进一步研究定义敏感性，以确保结果的准确性。

Method: 研究采用了一种新的方法，即在每个概念和三个LLM中，使用两种不同的定义对文本进行标注，同时观察对同一个文本进行的两次标注。还将其中一种定义重新表达以观察差异。

Result: 研究发现，使用不同的定义标注同一文本每次都有更多的标注变更，而简单的重新表达一个定义的变化幅度较小。这表明模型的注释依赖于提示定义。

Conclusion: 模型的注释依赖于提示定义，因此在报告注释结果时，应同时报告所用的定义和模型的定义敏感性和文义底线。

Abstract: In social science research, text annotation is a fundamental task that is traditionally a costly, hard-to-scale manual process. Researchers have used large language models (LLMs) to perform annotation by writing a definition of a concept into a prompt - a rule that determines whether a label should be attributed to a text depending on its content. However, concepts such as hate speech, fake news, and terrorism typically have several theoretically defensible competing definitions. It is also unknown whether the model actually applied the definition to provide the label or the label was based on the model's pretrained notion of the concept. To test this, we have the model annotate the same texts twice, each time with a different definition. We call the disagreement between the two sets of annotations definition sensitivity. As a control, we paraphrase one definition but leave its requirements unchanged - the disagreement between the annotations with the two wordings becomes the paraphrase floor. Across six concepts and three LLMs, a swap of competing definitions always changes more annotations, on average, than a paraphrase. This shows that the annotations depend on the prompted definition, so any set of annotations should be interpreted together with the definition that produced it. The LLM annotator's definition sensitivity and its paraphrase floor should both be reported in such work.

</details>


### [135] [Understanding Students' Rationales for AI-Mediated Cheating: An Instructor Perspective from Online Forums Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13358)
*Iman Mohammadi,Sina Rismanchian,Ali Keramati,Shayan Salehi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究分析了Reddit平台上200万条关于学生使用AI作弊的师资讨论，发现四个主要动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨了教师认为学生使用AI作弊背后的原因，试图揭示学术界当前的诚信危机。

Method: 通过对Reddit平台r/Professors子版块大量讨论内容的定性分析。

Result: 研究发现了四个主要动机：学术系统游戏、数字学术能力差距、成绩焦虑和不诚实的正常化。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调需要关注学生背后的根本动机，以便采取有效措施维护学术公正。

Abstract: The extensive adoption of AI has precipitated a crisis in academic integrity. While studies attempt to quantify cheating, less is known about the motivations instructors believe students have for using AI in prohibited ways. We analyze 2.01 million posts on r/Professors to understand how instructors perceive students' justifications for AI-mediated cheating. Our qualitative analyses reveal four primary themes: (1) Academic System Gaming, (2) Digital-Academic Literacy Gap, (3) Performance Anxiety, and (4) Normalization of Dishonesty.

</details>


### [136] [Mapping Instructors' Attitudes Towards Cheating with AI: An Exploratory Analysis Using Forum Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13366)
*Iman Mohammadi,Ali Keramati,Sina Rismanchian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大学教授关于使用生成式AI作弊的看法及动机，并分析了他们对防止作弊的策略，以应对未来AI与学术整合的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在通过分析大学教授在r/Professors子版块中关于Cheating的讨论，来了解他们对学术诚信的看法以及防止作弊的策略动机。

Method: 研究者分析了201万个帖子（包括主题和评论），这些帖子讨论了ChatGPT发布后关于Cheating的行为。

Result: 研究结果揭示了教师们为何以及如何应对Cheating的现象，并探讨了AI对未来学术整合的影响。

Conclusion: 未来的研究应在AI与学术诚信之间寻找平衡，既要利用AI带来的好处，又要防止其被不当利用。

Abstract: In teaching and learning environments, cheating and the violation of academic integrity have always been one of the challenges, involving different levels of concerns such as administrative, personal knowledge, learning, and equity. The introduction of large language models has brought cheating in dialogue to the forefront, given the apparent capabilities of these models to complete students' academic assignments. This study aims to leverage recent dialogues among university professors about cheating with Generative AI to better understand their beliefs about academic honesty. In doing so, we analyze a corpus of 2.01 million posts (threads and comments) in the r/Professors subreddit discussing cheating after the release of ChatGPT, providing a better understanding of teachers' motivations to discourage cheating and the strategies they use to do so. We synthesize the why and how of addressing cheating to identify caveats about the future of AI and academic integration.

</details>


### [137] [BioMem: Agentic Memory for Biomedical LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13426)
*Mingchen Li,Yongkang Xiao,Rumeng Li,Rui Zhang,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统地探讨了生物医学领域的代理记忆系统，评估了不同记忆方法在大型语言模型中的表现，并引入了Hybrid Memory Retrieval and Utilization (HMRU)框架，旨在提高生物医学推理和对话记忆的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索代理记忆系统在生物医学领域的应用效果，解决信息保留、检索、更新及有效利用的问题。

Method: 研究选取了五种记忆方法，在四个大型语言模型上进行跨领域评估，包括生物医学知识推理、临床预测和长期对话记忆三种代表性场景。

Result: 研究揭示了记忆构建、证据相关性及对话历史对生物医学下游性能的影响，并基于此提出了Hybrid Memory Retrieval and Utilization (HMRU)框架，该框架在生物医学问题回答和对话记忆基准测试中平均提高了8.6%，在特定的临床预测任务中还取得了额外的收益，减少了24.9%的对话错误率，并缩小了48.0%的性能差距。

Conclusion: 研究提供了生物医学代理记忆的系统表征，并为开发更有效和可靠的增强记忆大型语言模型系统提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated considerable potential in biomedical applications, while agentic memory systems have emerged as a promising approach to preserving and utilizing information across tasks and interactions. However, a fundamental question remains: how effectively and reliably do these systems retain, retrieve, update, and utilize biomedical information? In this study, we systematically investigate agentic memory in the biomedical domain, focusing on task effectiveness, retrieval quality, information fidelity, and long-term robustness. We evaluate five memory approaches across four LLMs in three representative settings: biomedical knowledge reasoning, clinical prediction, and long-term conversational memory. Our analyses reveal how memory construction, evidence relevance, and conversational history shape downstream biomedical performance, highlighting the distinct challenges of retrieving relevant information, preserving its fidelity, and effectively utilizing it for reasoning. Building on these findings, we introduce Hybrid Memory Retrieval and Utilization (HMRU), a training-free framework that jointly enhances evidence accessibility and task-aware utilization to improve biomedical reasoning and long-term conversational memory. HMRU achieves an average relative improvement of 8.6% across biomedical question-answering and conversational memory benchmarks, with additional gains in selected clinical prediction tasks. It further reduces conversational answer errors by 24.9% and closes 48.0% of the performance gap to full-context inference. Our findings provide a systematic characterization of biomedical agentic memory and practical insights into developing more effective and reliable memory-augmented LLM systems.

</details>


### [138] [Inherited Weights: Measuring the Tokenization Premium and the Sovereignty Cost of Language Models for the Global South](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13441)
*Paulo Camara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了各种语言模型的分词优势，并提出了一种主权审计方法，同时还探讨了设计目标的转变，强调了能耗和法律责任下的准确率、校准和风险选择的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示语言模型在其训练过程中继承的基本特性，并提供一种评估分词优势的方法，以增强模型的主权性和性能。

Method: 研究采用了跨语言数据集进行实验，并通过元分析比较了不同分词器和词汇表对模型性能的影响。

Result: 研究发现了分词器对不同语言模型性能的巨大影响，特别是对于一些较小的语言，以及提出了一种简单的主权审计方法。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在设计语言模型时，应考虑能耗和法律责任，而不仅仅是准确率，并提出了新的设计指标。

Abstract: Most language models deployed outside the United States and China are fine-tuned descendants of a small number of pretrained families. We argue that fine-tuning leaves the decisive properties of a model inherited from its base: vocabulary, knowledge, cultural alignment, memorized data and license. We contribute (i) an updated, reproducible measurement of the tokenization premium across 31 languages and 7 tokenizers released in 2023-2024, on 27 articles of the Universal Declaration of Human Rights, with explicit correction for unknown-token collapse. Premiums reach 10x for Amharic in older tokenizers and remain at 6.8x in the newest commercial one; Indigenous South American languages pay between 1.6x and 2.7x under global tokenizers. A vocabulary built from Portuguese lowers the premium for 11 of 13 Latin-script Global South languages relative to every global tokenizer tested, by about 24% for Guarani and Quechua. (ii) An eight-test black-box sovereignty audit that a buyer can run without access to weights. (iii) A regime argument: under energy and liability constraints, accuracy per joule, calibration and selective risk should replace raw accuracy as design objectives, with metrics that abstention cannot inflate. We close with preliminary internal measurements from a proprietary architecture built around these objectives, reported with their limitations. Data and measurement code are included.

</details>


### [139] [Mobile AI Factory: Building Specialized Language Models for Your Mobile Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13509)
*Evan Li,Krishna Deshpande,Wen Cheng,Shyamnath Gollakota*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mobile AI Factory 是一种自动化系统，可以为给定任务和手机构建特定于设备和任务的量化小语言模型（SLM）。该系统通过实验调整超参数并学习真实数据或生成合成示例，确保在设备上运行的模型能够达到或接近最佳性能，同时使用较少的计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前，微型语言模型（SLMs）需要由专家反复调参才能在特定任务和设备上高效运行，这非常耗时且成本高昂。Mobile AI Factory 的目的是通过自动化这些调参过程，使得微型语言模型能够在多种任务和设备上取得接近最佳性能的结果，同时降低资源消耗。

Method: Mobile AI Factory 通过一个决策制定的LLM（称为统筹者）来选择调参、挖掘实际数据或生成合成示例的操作，结合固定规则以防止对准确性的损害。该系统会从小型模型到大型模型进行搜索，并根据不同情况调整其准确性目标。此外，该系统还会使用目标设备的实际测量数据来进行推理，并在不同的推理引擎和后端之间进行优化。

Result: 在十个任务和四款安卓手机上评估 Mobile AI Factory，其性能在五个任务上超过了两个五次采样前沿模型，最多达到 0.34 的改进，并且在另三个任务上与其持平。与完全训练所有 27 种模型变体相比，搜索过程仅用掉了 19 倍少的 GPU 时间。此外，如果缺少目标手机的测量数据，Claude Opus 5.5（另一种语言模型）在简单的、中等难度的情况中无法满足应用程序要求，在 25% 的简单和中等难度案例以及在 60% 的困难案例中不能满足应用程序需求。

Conclusion: Mobile AI Factory 成功实现了为特定任务和手机构建量身定制的微型语言模型，提升了这些模型的性能，节约了计算资源。这项技术可以为移动设备上的小语言模型的应用提供一种有效且自动化的解决方案。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) running on mobile devices can keep user data private and work without connectivity. But they perform well only when fine-tuned for the target task and sized for the target device, a process that today requires repeated expert tuning. We present {\it Mobile AI Factory}, an agentic system that builds a fine-tuned, quantized SLM for a given task and phone. Given a task specification and a target phone, an orchestrator LLM chooses whether to tune hyperparameters, mine real data, or generate synthetic examples, while fixed rules roll back changes that hurt accuracy. The factory searches from small to large models across base models and quantizations, adapts its accuracy goal as it searches, and uses real measurements of the target phone across inference engines and backends. We evaluate Mobile AI Factory on ten tasks and four Android phones. On an unseen testset, its on-device models outperform two five-shot frontier models on five tasks, by up to 0.34, and match them on three more. Its search comes within 0.03 of exhaustively training all 27 model variants on an entity extraction task, while using 19$\times$ less GPU time. Phone measurements also matter: the best configuration changes across phones. Without phone measurements, Claude Opus 5.5, given the full hardware and model specifications, misses the app's requirements in 25\% of easy and moderate cases and in 60\% of hard ones. Code is available at: https://github.com/evanly-gh/SLM_Factory.

</details>


### [140] [Framing-Induced Vulnerability in Large Language Models: Evidence from Chinese News Headlines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13531)
*Mo Zhou,Yiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过生态审计和受控实验发现，新闻标题的措辞对事件的感知重要性和误导性有显著影响，即使是大型语言模型在仅依据标题判断时也存在可靠性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索新闻标题对事件感知的影响，特别是在对新闻信息进行自动评估、路由和管理时，模型对标题措辞的敏感性成为了新闻传播中的一种隐性且关键的决策机制。

Method: 研究使用了一个包含63,926个中国新闻标题的生态审计，以及针对30个真实事件、30个合成事件和29对自然新闻标题的受控实验，测试了四种中国模型。

Result: 研究发现，改变标题框架会改变事件的感知重要性和误导性，且这种影响在不同的模型中都是一致的。情绪强化的标题增加了重要性评级0.65到0.88个点，而正面重构的标题降低了重要性评级0.34到0.52个点。提供文章正文后，这种重要性偏差减少了54到80%，并且所有这些减少都是在p < .01的显著水平上。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，在仅依据标题评估时，大型语言模型存在可靠性差距。标题措辞的敏感性是部署和治理过程中需要检查的可靠性属性之一。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly evaluate, route, and moderate news, so a model's judgment of a headline has become a gatekeeping decision. We ask whether that judgment follows the fact being reported or the way the fact is framed. We combine an ecological audit of 63,926 Chinese news headlines with a controlled experiment. The experiment covers 30 real events, 30 synthetic events, and 29 natural headline pairs, tested on four Chinese models. Changing only the headline frame shifted both perceived misleadingness and event severity, and the direction and ordering of the shifts were the same for DeepSeek, MiMo, Dots, and LongCat. Emotionally intensified headlines raised severity ratings by 0.65 to 0.88 points, whereas positively reframed headlines lowered them by 0.34 to 0.52 points. Supplying the article body reduced this severity bias by 54 to 80 percent, and all four reductions were significant at p < .01. A small human reference study showed the same direction of effect. Headline-only LLM evaluation therefore carries a measurable robustness gap, and framing sensitivity belongs among the reliability properties that deployment and governance check.

</details>


### [141] [How Post-Training Influences Verbalized Algorithmic Computation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13552)
*Niall Dalton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，尽管post-training方法可以提高语言模型对某些内部信息的敏感度，但产生的链式思考（CoT）的质量并不一定会随之提高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解post-training如何改变语言模型使用内部信息和表达链式思考（CoT）的方式。

Method: 通过对比连续的SFT，DPO，和RLVR检查点在多数字加法任务上的表现，使用carry值作为已知的中间状态。

Result: 在使用carry信息的准确性方面，SFT和RLVR的探针表现相似，但操纵carry信息并不能可靠地控制模型输出。RLVR生成的CoT在接收检查点上表现得更加准确，而声明carry相关的语句在RLVR中比随机对照组更受重视。DPO也对carry相关的陈述表现出类似的敏感度，但它没有获得相当的准确性提升。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，post-training调整了语言模型撰写推理和对其内容的敏感性的有用性，虽然提高敏感性并不总是与更好的问题解决表现相匹配。

Abstract: Post-training improves language-model reasoning, but how it changes the use of internal information and verbalized chain of thought (CoT) remains unclear. We study successive SFT, DPO, and RLVR checkpoints of Olmo-3-7B-Instruct on multi-digit addition, using carry values as known intermediate state. Probes recover carry information from internal activations with similar accuracy in SFT and RLVR, although manipulating this information does not reliably control model outputs. In contrast, the usefulness of verbalized reasoning differs: CoTs produced by RLVR yield higher accuracy across receiving checkpoints than those produced by SFT or DPO. We also show that carry-related statements receive higher importance scores after RLVR than matched random controls. DPO shows similar sensitivity to carry-related statements but without comparable accuracy gains. These results suggest that post-training changes the usefulness of written reasoning and sensitivity to its content in meaningful ways, although greater sensitivity does not necessarily coincide with better problem performance.

</details>


### [142] [Are Dense Rewards Worth Their Tokens in GRPO?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13560)
*Vikash Singh,Weicong Chen,Debargha Ganguly,Yanyan Zhang,Mohsen Hariri,Sreehari Sankar,Wang Yang,Chaoda Song,Alexander Nemecek,Osama Zafar,Yuting Shao,Kai Ye,Biyao Zhang,Xinpeng Li,Shouren Wang,Erman Ayday,Jing Ma,Sanmukh Rao Kuppannagari,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入匹配计算协议，比较了不同奖励机制在语言模型训练中的性能。发现尽管某些基于蒙特卡洛奖励的方法在单次更新中表现较好，但在匹配计算资源下，标准的组相对策略优化（GRPO）在最终准确率上更有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法如GRPO对解决方案中每个词给予相同的信用，而密集过程奖励试图分配给中间步骤适当的奖励。研究者提出匹配计算协议，以确保各种训练干预措施得到平等的预算，在生成的 token 数量和训练 GPU 小时数方面进行公正比较。

Method: 研究者设计了匹配计算协议，并在六种不同规模的语言模型上进行了预注册的实验。与对照组相比，干预组优化了训练策略，确保了公平的计算资源分配，同时，通过对蒙特卡洛奖励与其他密集奖励方法的对比测试衡量最佳策略。

Result: 结果显示，在与标准 GRPO 等量训练次数和时间的情况下，蒙特卡洛奖励方法在最终准确率上分别落后了 14.3 个百分点和 7.8 个百分点。即使是蒙特卡罗奖励的四种变体，在大量训练时间内也没有优于标准 GRPO。传统的每 token 梯度测试虽然可以排序奖励效果，但也高估了这些效果。相比之下，在预测成功概率接近于 1/2 的提示上进行训练可以显著提升准确性，特别是在匹配计算资源的条件下表现更为明显。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，训练干预措施应在匹配计算资源的条件下进行验证，这种做法能够揭示一些假象中的进步，并确认其他的真实进步。

Abstract: RLVR trains language models to reason by rewarding correct final answers. A widely used algorithm, group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), gives every token of a solution the same credit, so dense process rewards have been proposed to credit intermediate steps, for example with a Monte-Carlo reward that scores each step by sampling continuations from it. Comparing such a reward with GRPO per training update, or vetting it with a gradient test on a single batch, leaves out what it costs: in our setting, the continuations alone take 91% of all generated tokens. We introduce a matched-compute protocol that gives every training intervention the same budget, measured in generated tokens and training GPU-hours, and tests it in preregistered studies with controls for side effects. Across 1,668 runs on six models, matching compute reverses the ranking. On Qwen2.5-1.5B, the Monte-Carlo reward leads standard GRPO per update, yet ends 14.3 percentage points lower at equal tokens and 7.8 points lower at equal training time, and none of four dense-reward variants outperforms GRPO even with as much training time. A per-token gradient test orders these rewards as training does but overestimates each by one to two points, as much as the gains it is meant to detect. In contrast, training on the prompts whose predicted success is nearest 1/2 improves accuracy at matched compute, by 1.7 points on Qwen3-0.6B and 0.8 on Qwen2.5-1.5B. These gains replicate on fresh seeds and unseen problems, and a simple rule, that the candidate prompts are predicted to fail more often than not, predicted where they appear in five of six preregistered tests. Both gains come early in training, and standard GRPO with twice the initial learning rate comes within half a point of them. We argue that training interventions should be validated at matched compute, which overturns some apparent gains and confirms others.

</details>


### [143] [An evidence-traceable benchmark of clinical reasoning in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13562)
*Zhiqun Zhao,Xiaodong Yang,Bruce Gu,Ming Li,Yi Fu,Song Li,Qi Mei,Zhi Liu,Kai Zhang,Kexue Fu,Jing Wu,Xiaofei Lu,Yule Li,Zheng Wang,Haiquan Lu,Xiaoming Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发了Med Evidence Bench（MEB），包含1,536个由医生构建的项目，涵盖指南知识、标准临床场景和真实世界实验室场景。评估十种大型语言模型的证据追踪分数（ETS）在不同场景中差异显著，但在真实世界场景中识别临床信息的能力超过应用证据和制定建议的能力。自动评分与医生多数意见一致，但证据应用的吻合度较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决医疗大型语言模型评分标准未能追溯到临床证据的问题，研究人员开发了MEB，旨在更准确地评估这些模型在不同医疗场景中的表现。

Method: 研究人员开发了包含1,536个项目的MEB，涵盖指南知识、标准临床场景和真实世界实验室场景。他们对十种大型语言模型进行了评估，通过证据追踪分数（ETS）来衡量模型在不同场景中的表现，并通过自动评分与医生多数意见的比较来验证评分的有效性。

Result: 在十种大型语言模型的评估中，证据追踪分数（ETS）在指南知识方面表现较好，但在标准化及真实世界场景中表现不一。具体而言，真实世界场景中识别临床信息的能力超过应用证据和制定建议的能力。自动评分与医生多数意见一致，但证据应用的吻合度较低。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，指南知识的回溯能力并不能确保患者特定证据的有效应用，也不能证明临床有效性。

Abstract: Benchmark scores for medical large language models (LLMs) are difficult to interpret when scoring criteria cannot be traced to clinical evidence. Here we developed Med Evidence Bench (MEB; https://www.medevidencebench.org.cn/), comprising 1,536 physician-constructed items across three levels: guideline knowledge, standardized clinical scenarios and real-world laboratory scenarios. Each criterion stating a medical claim is linked to a versioned source-text span.
