Across ten LLMs, the Evidence Trace Score (ETS) was 0.975-0.997 for guideline knowledge, 0.292-0.658 for standardized scenarios and 0.182-0.447 for real-world scenarios, and rankings varied across levels. For real-world scenarios, scores for identifying clinical information (mean, 0.770) exceeded those for matching evidence (0.165) or formulating recommendations (0.160). On 1,296 criterion-level judgments, automated grading agreed with the majority vote of three physicians (macro-F1, 0.930; 95% confidence interval, 0.915-0.945), although agreement was lower for evidence application. These findings from Chinese-language materials indicate that guideline recall does not ensure patient-specific evidence use and do not establish clinical effectiveness.

</details>


### [144] [A Concept-Based Interpretable Framework for Clinical Text-based Prediction via Token-Concept Attention without Concept Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13564)
*Jifan Gao,Junjie Hu,John Caskey,Madeline Oguss,Simona Cristea,Anoop Mayampurath,Matthew Churpek,Majid Afshar,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种无需概念监督即可实现概念驱动解释的框架，在临床预测任务中可以保持与黑盒模型相当的性能，并实现患者及群体级别的可解释性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念驱动解释方法通常需要昂贵的概念标注数据，本文提出了一种新的框架，旨在无需概念监督的情况下，通过定制的跨注意力瓶颈实现概念驱动解释，以降低成本并提高临床文本预测的透明度。

Method: 该框架首先将临床文本和医学概念的文字描述通过预训练的语言模型进行分词和嵌入，然后使用一个跨注意力模块估计每个文本词中每个概念的存在性，生成概念相关性得分，再通过池化和线性预测层进行临床结果的建模。

Result: 该框架在两个任务（胸部创伤严重程度分类和ICU死亡率预测）中，其AUC-ROC值与黑盒模型相差在0.02以内。并且能够实现基于患者和群体的可解释性解释，捕捉群体水平的概念重要性以及患者特定的概念贡献。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的框架实现了无需真实概念存在标签的概念驱动解释，提高了临床文本预测的透明度，同时在临床预测任务中保持了强健的性能。

Abstract: Objective: Concept-based interpretation has emerged as a promising approach for explaining blackbox models using human-understandable, high-level concepts. However, most existing methods require annotated concept labels to train the model to identify concepts in the input, and obtaining such annotations in clinical text is often expensive. This study aims to enable concept-based interpretation for language models without concept supervision on clinical prediction tasks. Methods: We propose a novel framework that enables concept-based interpretation without concept supervision through a tailored cross-attention bottleneck. Clinical text and the text descriptions of medical concepts are first tokenized and embedded by a pretrained language model. A cross-attention module then estimates the presence of each concept for each text token, producing concept relevance scores that capture how concepts are involved throughout the note. These scores are pooled and used in a linear prediction layer to model clinical outcomes in an interpretable manner. Results: The performance of the proposed framework was compared against blackbox models across two tasks: chest trauma severity stratification using a private dataset and an in-hospital mortality prediction task using the publicly available MIMIC-III dataset. The proposed framework achieved AUROC values within 0.02 of the corresponding blackbox models. The framework also enabled cohort- and patient-level interpretation, capturing the overall concept importance across the cohort and patient-specific concept contributions. Conclusion: Our proposed framework realizes concept-based interpretation without requiring ground-truth concept presence labels. It improves the transparency of clinical text predictions while maintaining strong performance in clinical prediction tasks.

</details>


### [145] [IFRS Compliance Verification: An Open Multi-LLM Benchmark and Content-Conditioned Ensemble Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13595)
*Aghoghomena Akasukpe,Li Yang,Xinyao Zhou,Chen Shen,Miguel Vargas Martin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个基于多阶段框架和新基准的数据集的解决方案，通过多种大语言模型（LLM）及其集成分类器来提高IFRS财务披露验证的准确性，显著超越了单一模型及投票策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前单个大语言模型（LLM）在高风险应用场景中的准确性不足，简单的集成方法也存在系统偏差的问题。为了解决这个挑战，研究团队开发了一种多阶段框架，并创建了一个新的基准数据集，用于提升IFRS财务披露验证的准确性和可靠度。

Method: 研究团队构建了一个三阶段语言模型适应流水线，包括BERT掩码语言模型、无监督的TSDAE和SimCSE适应，以及监督对比度对齐，并结合了基于梯度提升树优化的Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE)和LLM的概率特征进行集成分类。

Result: 相较单一模型，该多模型集成框架在分歧集中实现了81.7%的加权F1评分，比最强的单个LLM高出10.4个百分点，比多数投票高出14.6个百分点，也优于11种无监督聚合基线。在未知法规的检索管道上达到了45.3%的召回率。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的多阶段框架和多LLM集成方法，显著提高了IFRS财务披露验证的准确性和效果，为未来的大语言模型在财务审计领域的应用提供了强有力的支持和借鉴。

Abstract: Verifying corporate financial disclosures against the International Financial Reporting Standards (IFRS) is a core auditing task in which experts cross-reference disclosures with hundreds of fine-grained regulatory paragraphs: a laborious, costly, and error-prone process that motivates growing interest in large language models (LLMs) as compliance assistants. Yet single-model accuracy remains insufficient for high-stakes use, and naive ensembling is vulnerable to systematic per-model biases that can drag aggregate accuracy below that of the strongest individual annotator. This gap is addressed with a multi-stage framework, supported by a new benchmark of 19,998 IFRS regulation-disclosure pairs comprising 11,161 unanimous multi-LLM labels and 8,837 human annotations on items where the LLMs disagreed. The framework couples a three-stage domain-adapted sentence embedding pipeline (BERT masked language modeling on SEC EDGAR filings, unsupervised TSDAE and SimCSE adaptation, and supervised contrastive alignment) with a learned ensemble classifier that fuses frozen embeddings and LLM probability features from GPT-4.1, Claude Sonnet 4, and Gemini 2.5 Pro via Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) optimized gradient-boosted trees. On the disagreement set, the ensemble achieves 81.7% weighted F1, exceeding the strongest single LLM by 10.4 points and majority voting by 14.6 points, and outperforms eleven unsupervised aggregation baselines; the retrieval pipeline attains Recall@15 of 45.3% on unseen regulations. The dataset, model checkpoints, annotation platform, and classifier implementation will be publicly released.

</details>


### [146] [Compute the Interactions, Not the Attributes: What a Fact Layer Must Supply in the Chinese Sexagenary System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13597)
*Huixue Han,Jiaying Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过将模型与决策层结合，在中国传统的巴azi命理学系统中进行评估。结果显示，模型直接回答问题的准确率较低，尤其是在需结合多种属性的情况下。通过过滤查询的决策层提升了一些模型性能，特别是对于碰撞项的计算。这提示了在规则体系中，决策层应提供计算过的交互，而展示方式需考虑模型和领域特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解决语言模型在规则体系中应用时，确定性事实层对于模型性能的提升作用，特别是在巴azi命理学这样复杂规则体系中的表现。

Method: 通过封闭书籍测试，对11种不同权重的开放模型在巴azi命理学系统的160个碰撞项目上进行评估，以及在具有查询过滤的功能层下的表现对比。

Result: 模型直接回答问题的准确率低，尤其是需要结合多种属性的情况，大部分模型准确率不到1%；静态属性没有一致性影响；完全的关系列表对于大多数直接回答模型帮助较小；过滤查询的决策层显著提升了部分模型，尤其是对于碰撞项的计算；三项专有推理模型在未辅助情况下计算了68-95%的碰撞项。

Conclusion: 在规则限制的领域中，决策层提供计算交互是必要的；但展示方式需要根据模型性能和特定领域进行调整，以优化模型性能。

Abstract: Language-model systems in rule-governed domains often pair the model with a deterministic fact layer, but which facts that layer should supply is rarely measured. We measure it in the Chinese sexagenary system underlying BaZi chart analysis, where every fact has one table-checkable value: 682 items over twelve fact categories and 160 transit--natal collision items, posed closed-book, with the reference table, and with computed facts at increasing levels. In eleven open-weight models answering directly, errors concentrate where attributes must be combined: none gets more than 4\% of sexagenary days or 1\% of collisions right. Static attributes have no consistent effect, and the complete relation list leaves most direct-answering models near zero although it contains the answer. A query-filtered layer (exploratory) raises Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B to 78--92\% and four further models to 96--100\%. Three proprietary reasoning models compute 68--95\% of collisions unaided. On chess positions, under predictions locked in advance, attributes again do not substitute for relations and listed distractors are misattributed. The fact layer must supply computed interactions; how they are presented depends on the model and the domain.

</details>


### [147] [One Byte, One Token: Byteifying a 27B Hybrid-Attention LLM with Self-Generated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13607)
*Liwei Zhang,ZhiMing Tong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过将已训练的27B子词模型直接转换为纯字节级别模型，而不使用补丁结构或局部编码器，实现了在保持大部分能力的同时，将字节级别模型与原始模型在特定任务上的性能差距缩小到配对的Bootstrap区间内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索直接将已训练的大型子词模型转换为字节级别模型的可能性，以简化模型结构并利用现有的字节表示来提高处理效率。

Method: 从Qwen3.8-27B开始，将词汇表从248,320减少到384个条目（包括256个字节符号和特殊标识符），保持其他权重不变，采用两阶段课程训练机制（首先编码，然后阅读），使用4.77 GB的监督字节数据集进行训练，其中对话/行为监督由原始模型自身生成，知识阅读使用百科数据。

Result: 经过训练后，字节级别的模型在MATH-500、HumanEval+、MMLU-Pro和非思考模式的GSM8K任务上与原始模型表现相近，但在某些字符级别任务上（如CUTE、EXECUTE-zho、我们的中文字符套件）表现更佳，但在知识查找方面（如发音和笔画数）表现较差，且处理速度较慢。

Conclusion: 字节级别的模型在处理效率和特定任务上具有优势，但需要优化以保留和恢复更多的语言理解能力。

Abstract: Byte-level BPE fragments Chinese text (for Qwen3.8 on Chinese Wikipedia, 3.84 bytes per token on average; 64% of tokens are single characters) and hides spelling from the model. We ask whether an already-trained 27B subword model can be converted directly into a pure byte-level model (one byte, one token), without a patching structure or a local encoder, while keeping most of its ability. Starting from Qwen3.8-27B (48 Gated DeltaNet layers + 16 full-attention layers), we shrink the vocabulary from 248,320 to 384 entries (the 256 byte symbols already in the BPE vocabulary plus special ids), leave every other weight untouched, and continue training in a two-stage curriculum (encoding first, then reading) on 4.77 GB of supervised bytes: all dialogue/behavioral supervision is generated on-policy by the original model itself (character dictionary, word dictionary, general dialogue, coding sessions), and knowledge reading uses encyclopedia text (54% of supervised bytes). Training takes 178.6 h on 8xH200. On the same GPU and serving stack, the byte model's gap to the original falls within the paired bootstrap interval on MATH-500, HumanEval+, MMLU-Pro and non-thinking GSM8K, and it loses 4-6 points on C-Eval, thinking-mode GSM8K and non-thinking IFEval. Without thinking it leads on every character-level task (CUTE 75.2 -> 84.0, EXECUTE-zho 81.6 -> 93.9, our Chinese character suite 54.4 -> 64.0), but lookup knowledge about Chinese characters such as pronunciation and stroke count drops, and thinking does not recover it. The cost is throughput: per-step speed is identical, but each step yields 3-5x less output; Chinese characters per second is about 1/4 of the original, and prefill at 64K bytes is 6.5x slower. We also document four byte-model failure modes (greedy runaway, sampling breaking UTF-8, ineffective penalty terms on a small vocabulary, repetition) and their fixes.

</details>


### [148] [Post-OCR Correction of Mixed-Script Uzbek Text: Byte-Level ByT5 with an Empirical Homoglyph Noise Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13609)
*Mannon Ochilov,Oybek Narzullayev,Bahodir Zaripov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文关注官方和技术文档中的OCR错误问题，特别是那些由于两种交替使用的字母系统中视觉上相似的字符导致的错误。提出的系统在本地硬件上处理企业地质和ICT基础设施报告，通过结合字节级ByT5模型和基于手动分类错误事件估计采样权重的噪声生成器，降低了字符错误率，并恢复了大部分的同形词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文研究了官方和技术文档中由于使用两种交替字母系统（俄语字母与拉丁字母）造成的OCR错误问题，尤其是同形词（homoglyph）的错误。为了解决这个问题，提出了一种新的基于本地硬件处理的方法。

Method: 该方法结合了字节级ByT5模型和一种基于手动分类错误事件的噪声生成器，并引入了限制Levenshtein距离的保守解码规则。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统在Gold集合上降低了字符错误率，从0.0578降至0.0369，并成功恢复了91.3%的同形词，引入了84个新错误。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法在处理文档中的同形词错误方面表现出色，能够在不离开组织的情况下进行本地化处理，从而降低了字符错误率并提高了文档的准确性。

Abstract: In Uzbek official and technical documents, a large share of OCR errors is caused not by poor image quality but by characters that are visually identical across the two alphabets in use. Because the script transition is still under way, Cyrillic and Latin appear on the same page and often within a single sentence, and in our measurements the density of homoglyph errors on such pages is close to five times higher than in single-script text. We study this problem in a setting where the corpus of engineering-geological and ICT infrastructure reports may not leave the organisation, so every stage was executed on local hardware. The proposed system combines a byte-level ByT5 model with a noise generator whose sampling weights are estimated from 359 manually classified error events, together with a Levenshtein-bounded conservative decoding rule. On a Gold set of 5,204 line pairs it lowers the character error rate from 0.0578 to 0.0369, restores 91.3% of the homoglyphs and introduces only 84 new errors.

</details>


### [149] [Synapse-Control: Making Agent Experience Actionable with Lightweight Runtime Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13615)
*Zhiyuan Huang,Shengan Zheng,Chenghu Yang,Song Zhang,Lingyun Li,Chen Yu,Weiquan Huang,Linpeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Synapse-Control 是一个轻量级的代理运行时，能够通过对经验的再利用来提高复杂工具任务的可靠性。通过引入规则列表和微契约，以及使用预先标记的状态建议对进行微调的小型分类器进行评分，而不是生成诊断，从而提高了成功的案例数量，并减少了辅助控制的时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的系统（例如 Sentry）在执行复杂任务之前调用大模型来生成失败诊断，增加了推理成本，Synapse-Control 设计以减少这种成本并提高执行经验的再利用效率。

Method: Synapse-Control 采用了规则列表和微契约来精选相关建议，并使用一个预先标记过的状态建议对进行微调的小型分类器对这些候选方案进行评分而不是直接生成诊断。此外，还使用了提示值表来限制重复建议的生成。

Result: 在 AppWorld 和 WebShop 上的 1,308 次尝试中，Synapse-Control 达到了 270 次成功（相比基础方法多了 29.8%），替换了 4,987 次生成的诊断调用，且与 Sentry 相比，辅助控制时间分别减少了 50.2% 和 51.0%。

Conclusion: Synapse-Control 显示出通过轻量级代理运行时来有效改进大型语言模型在复杂工具任务中执行表现的能力，通过减少辅助控制时间并提高任务执行的成功率，提供了一种资源利用上的创新方法。

Abstract: Large language model agents reuse execution experience to improve reliability on complex tool-based tasks. Systems such as Sentry call large models to generate failure diagnoses before retrieving advice, adding inference cost to experience reuse. We present Synapse-Control, an agent runtime whose lightweight control plane selects advice and determines when to send it to the task-executing model. Rules shortlist relevant advice, and a lightweight 1.7B classifier fine-tuned on teacher-labeled state-suggestion pairs scores those candidates instead of generating a diagnosis. Micro-contracts encode reusable interface checks, such as required arguments or valid options, without prescribing complete task programs. A Prompt Value Table records earlier guidance and feedback to limit repeated suggestions through cooldown and token-budget checks. Across 1,308 attempts per method in AppWorld and WebShop, Synapse-Control achieves 270 successes (29.8% more than Base), replaces 4,987 generative diagnosis calls, and reduces auxiliary control time relative to Sentry by 50.2% and 51.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [150] [An Interpretable Stylometric Framework for Early Chinese Buddhist Translator Styles: A Case Study on the Attribution of Disputed Works of An Shigao](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13636)
*Yating Pan,Xinyue Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种可解释的文体学框架，通过使用佛学学者定义或审查的功能字符、句型公式、句尾字符、句子长度和词性n-克gram，来鉴别早期中文佛教翻译文本。评估结果显示，T1557和T0101最有可能是安世高翻译的作品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于早期中文佛教翻译需要依据可追溯的文本证据，因此开发一种可解释的文体学框架以便准确鉴别这些文本。

Method: 使用功能字符、句型公式、句尾字符、句子长度和词性n-克gram作为特征，构建从31个已认证翻译文本中已知译者的风格特征，并对比八个有争议文本与安世高的特征档案，评估相似度。

Result: T1557和T0101与安世高的标识文本相似度分别为0.783和0.687，与基于BERT的方法在相同争议文本集上得出的结果一致。

Conclusion: 该文体学框架无需针对特定任务进行训练，便于学者查看支持每项拟议属性的哪种语言特征。此方法在鉴别早期佛教文本方面显示出良好的效果。

Abstract: Attributing early Chinese Buddhist translations requires evidence that scholars can trace back to the texts. We develop an interpretable stylometric framework that uses features defined or reviewed by Buddhist philologists, including function characters, sentence formulas, sentence-final characters, sentence length, and part-of-speech n-grams. We construct stylistic profiles from 31 authenticated translations by seven translators from the Eastern Han through the Western Jin, then compare eight disputed texts with An Shigao's profile. Two texts, T1557 and T0101, show the strongest stylistic similarity to the authenticated An Shigao corpus, with scores of 0.783 and 0.687, respectively. The attributions agree with those reported by a published BERT-based approach on the same set of disputed texts. The framework requires no task-specific training and allows scholars to inspect which linguistic features support each proposed attribution.

</details>


### [151] [OpticalRAG: Pixel-Space Compression for Token-Efficient Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13675)
*Senhao Liu,Yuheng Zhang,Chunyu Wei,Yueguo Chen,Xinran Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OpticalRAG 是一种通过将文档渲染为页面图像并使用冻结的视觉编码器编码，然后通过轻量级的跨注意压缩器提炼到少量注入 token，实现 RAG 技术的新方法，这种方法在不进行昂贵的跨模态训练的情况下，实现了在长文推理、BAMBOO、LooGLE-v2 三个基准上的最佳压缩感知准确度，并提高了封闭书籍 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 RAG 技术受到大型语言模型的 token 预算限制，导致长文档处理昂贵且文本压缩会丢失细粒度信息，这限制了信息密度。OpticalRAG 提出了一种新方法，通过使用视觉编码器提高信息密度和减少 token 使用量，同时避免了昂贵的跨模态训练。

Method: OpticalRAG 利用视觉编码器将文档转为页面图像，冻结视觉编码器并将其与压缩模块共享，采用 Encoder-Bridged Retrieval 技术将文本查询投射到视觉特征空间以检索视觉 token，并使用 Query-Driven Distillation 轻量级跨注意压缩器将检索到的视觉 tokens 凝缩为少量查询条件前缀 token，最后将这些 token 输入到语言模型。

Result: OpticalRAG 在 LongBench-v2、BAMBOO、LooGLE-v2 三个基准测试中达到了最佳的压缩感知准确度 (CAP)，同时仅使用 16 个 token。它还提高了封闭书籍 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 模型 5.17 分。

Conclusion: OpticalRAG 成功打破了传统 RAG 技术的瓶颈，通过引入视觉编码器和创新的压缩机制，实现了高效信息压缩和高准确度文本生成。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is bottlenecked by the token budget of large language models: feeding long documents into the context window is expensive, while aggressive textual compression discards fine-grained information that is irreversibly lost in a one-dimensional token sequence. We argue that this bottleneck is not fundamental to information density but to the choice of compression \emph{modality}. Modern visual encoders can pack the contents of a rendered text page into roughly a hundred visual tokens with little semantic loss, opening a new compression-fidelity tradeoff that purely textual methods cannot reach. We present \textbf{OpticalRAG}, a framework that renders documents as page images, encodes them with a frozen vision encoder, and distills the retrieved visual tokens into as few as $16$ injected tokens before passing them to the LLM. Two ingredients make this work without expensive cross-modal training: (i) \emph{Encoder-Bridged Retrieval}, which projects text-query states into the visual feature space of the frozen encoder, through a projection shared with the compressor, to retrieve visual tokens from a text query, with no contrastive training; and (ii) \emph{Query-Driven Distillation}, a lightweight cross-attention compressor that condenses the retrieved visual tokens into a small set of query-conditioned prefix tokens. On LongBench-v2, BAMBOO, and LooGLE-v2, OpticalRAG achieves the best compression-aware accuracy (CAP) on all three benchmarks while injecting only $16$ tokens, and improves the closed-book Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct backbone by $5.17$ points on LongBench-v2.Code is available at https://github.com/brighterr/OpticalRAG.

</details>


### [152] [You Are What You Read: Indian Political News Media Bias Dataset for Hindi Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13686)
*Kushal Trivedi,Mayank Mahor,Dhruv Sanghvi,Roshni Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了印度最受欢迎的四家印地语新闻机构的448篇文章，涵盖了政治新闻，并由专家进行多注释，按四类偏见（选择偏见、框架偏见、信息操纵偏见和省略偏见）进行分类。每个被标记为有偏见的文章还有一份文本解释，以支持未来的半自动化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于印度的多元文化和语言多样性，以及同一事件由不同的新闻机构以不同语言报道可能会引发偏见问题，因此理解媒体偏见和检测对于印度语境，特别是印地语，是一个未被充分研究的挑战。

Method: 本文通过引入一个多注释、可解释的数据集来研究印度语境下的媒体偏见问题。数据集包括448篇来自四家主要印度新闻机构的印地语政治新闻。文章被分为四类偏见，并由14位专家进行注释。

Result: Cohen's Kappa分数对于简单的偏见/无偏见分类达到了0.81，对于多标签任务，分别是点击诱饵偏见、选择偏见、框架偏见和省略偏见，分别为0.89、0.75、0.81和0.79。平均Kappa值为0.80，这表明注释者之间的强一致性。

Conclusion: 此数据集不仅确保了高质量的标注结果，还为未来相关研究提供了详细的解释，使得语言模型可以在推理过程中包含解释性内容。

Abstract: Media bias understanding and detection remains an under-researched problem for Indian context, specifically for Indic languages, such as, Hindi, which is widely spoken in India. In this paper, we introduce a comprehensive, multi-annotator, explainable dataset consisting of 448 unique articles from four leading Indian news agencies publishing in Hindi. We focus on Hindi, the most spoken and an official language of India. The study of media bias is particularly relevant in the Indian context due to the country's linguistic diversity and large population, where the same event is often reported by multiple news agencies in different languages, thereby introducing scope for bias. The dataset comprises Hindi political news articles annotated by 14 experts and classified into four bias categories - selection bias, framing bias, clickbait bias and omission bias - when an article is identified as biased or unbiased. For each biased article, annotators also provide a textual explanation, enabling future semi-automated approaches to incorporate explainability when language models reason over the dataset. Cohen's Kappa scores were 0.81 for simple bias/no-bias classification reflecting strong inter-annotator agreement. For the multi-label task, the Cohen's Kappa values are 0.89, 0.75, 0.81, and 0.79 for clickbait, selection, framing, and omission biases, respectively, resulting in an average Kappa of 0.80, which also reflects strong agreement.

</details>


### [153] [Do Language Models Feel the Sentiment? Investigating Emotion Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13744)
*Ali Hassan Khan,Saad Bin Ather,Muhammad Saif,Maryam Bashir,Hajra Waheed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct和Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct中评估了GoEmotions提取的情感向量，通过情感探针、激活干预和监督纠正，发现情感信息能够显著影响情感分类精度，但这种机制并不是唯一的情感处理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究致力于探讨语言模型内部表示中的情感向量对情感分类的影响，特别是在大模型中验证情感对模型行为的具体作用。

Method: 研究通过情感探针、情感干预激活、情感监督校正等方法评估了Qwen2.5模型中提取的情感向量，并通过A/B测试和分类改进实验，观察情感对模型效果的影响。

Result: 研究发现，在二分类推特评价数据集上，仅使用情感信息的探针达到88.66%和90.70%的准确率；去情感空间的损失大于去随机等距空间的损失；情感纠正分类器能在亚马逊极性数据集中提高准确性，但并不总优于仅基于差距的基准。

Conclusion: 研究指出，情感信息在模型中起着显著作用，但在情感分类上是否依赖于特定的情感机制并不明确，这为模型理解和情感处理技术提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Emotion vectors are directions in a language model's internal representations that are associated with emotional concepts. Prior work shows that these vectors can influence model behavior, but their role in sentiment classification remains unclear. This study investigates emotion directions in Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct. Eight directions extracted from GoEmotions are evaluated through sentiment probing, activation interventions, and supervised correction. Emotion-only probes achieve 88.66% and 90.70% accuracy on binary TweetEval. In exploratory matched-ablation experiments, raw emotion-space removal causes 2.39 and 1.10 percentage points more accuracy loss than matched isotropic random-subspace removal for 0.5B and 3B, respectively. All four TweetEval raw and denoised effects are significant after Holm adjustment across eight comparisons. An exploratory SST-2 extension shows smaller raw excess losses of 0.66 and 0.57 points, with no effects surviving Holm adjustment. Frozen sentiment-correction classifiers are also tested on 20,000 Amazon Polarity reviews. Emotion-based correction improves accuracy over uncorrected Qwen, but does not consistently outperform margin-only baselines. These results distinguish sentiment information available in model representations, the effects of changing those representations, and their practical value for classification. They do not establish a uniquely emotional mechanism.

</details>


### [154] [Word-Level Alignment of OCR and Ground Truth under Zoning and Reading-Order Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13752)
*Fonseca Cacho,Jorge Ramón*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究重新审视了2019年的zonning问题，提出了一个新的评估协议，并通过多种不同的实验评估了多种POST-OCR校正方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文重新审视了之前的zonning问题，旨在提供一个更准确的评估方法，以便更好地理解POST-OCR校正技术的效果。

Method: 作者使用了一个新的协议来测试在阅读顺序乱序情况下的正确性。他们对不同方法进行了实验，并使用了多种数据集如合成字符噪声，历史报纸，以及几何推导出的阅读顺序等。

Result: 结果显示在合成测试中，2019年的方法在所有测试方法中获得了最高的F1值。然而，该方法对大规模读序变化仍不稳固，当唯一锚点稀缺时，基于锚点的方法会失效。

Conclusion: 尽管存在一些局限性，如不精确的黄金标准、文章顺序惯例等，作者发现基于锚点的方法在处理大多数情况时效果较好，但在缺乏唯一锚点的情况下会表现不佳。

Abstract: OCR post-processing needs OCR text aligned word by word with its ground truth. Data sets rarely provide that mapping, and a monotone aligner can lose its place when zoning errors permute the reading order. We revisit this problem, the Zoning Problem of our 2019 paper, with a protocol that tests correctness under such permutations. We apply OCR-side reading-order permutations with construction-time word-level gold and score correct-match F1, cascade length and recovery. We compare the 2019 location-aware aligner, a re-anchoring extension of it, a RETAS-style baseline (RETAS-lite), a multi-region aligner that combines unique-word and unique n-gram anchors with chunk-to-window assignment, genalog's RETAS and the original Java RETAS. Experiments use synthetic character noise with block moves, paragraph swaps and column interleaving, historical newspaper reading-order permutations, geometry-derived gold for Tesseract OCR, and a small word-box set. Synthetic tests show where local tracking fails after large displacements and where unique-anchor methods collapse when unique anchors are scarce. On our synthetic boilerplate page the 2019 method had the highest F1 among those tested. Historical newspaper and geometry-derived evaluations carry the limitations of approximate gold and the article-order convention. The first multi-region version has a quadratic scaling cost. Local tracking remains fragile under large reading-order changes; the tested anchor-based methods handle many of those cases but fail when unique anchors are scarce. Approximate gold, the article-order convention, missing residual annotation, and the lack of modern multi-column word-level ground truth remain limitations.

</details>


### [155] [LLM Validation Makes Minimal-Pair Benchmarks Easier for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13788)
*Guofang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM验证在验证最小差异对基准方面不如人类验证有效，尽管它们比随机删除项目更能提高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于LLM无需成本高昂的人类验证即可完成任务，因此作者测试了它们在最小差异对基准测试中的效果。

Method: 作者对BLiMP（英语）和QFrBLiMP（魁北克法语）两个公开的最小差异对数据集进行了实验，用三种不同的LLM进行验证，通过强制选择、评估和小组评分的方式，与人类评委进行对比。

Result: 在BLiMP数据集上，强制选择验证者在强制选择判断中比五人评审团更频繁地同意人类的多数意见，但发现的人类拒绝标签的错误对较少。通过点估计，没有验证者的价值超过一个评委。LLM验证导致的准确度提升大于随机删除项目带来的提升。另外，当LSTM预注册验证过滤掉被认为困难的项目时，其准确度提升大于随机删除或一个评委过滤的情况。

Conclusion: LLM验证使最小差异对基准测试更容易接受LM测试的模型。作者提供了代码、验证器输出和项目级得分用于进一步的研究。

Abstract: Minimal-pair benchmarks are only as good as their labels, and LLMs are an obvious substitute for costly human validation. We test it on BLiMP (English) and QFrBLiMP (Quebec French), the two public minimal-pair datasets releasing every forced-choice human judgment. Three LLMs validate by forced choice, by rating, and as a panel, scored against human panels of one, three and five judges in two pre-registered phases. On BLiMP, forced-choice validators agree with held-out human majorities more often than five-judge panels, yet catch far fewer pairs whose label humans reject than one judge does. By point estimate, no validator is worth more than one judge. The errors are not random. On both datasets, across 10 open LMs, human-supported pairs that the pre-registered LLM filter flags are 18.9 points harder than those it accepts, in all 70 LM-validator combinations, and harder than pairs a single human judge flags. Filtering thus removes items on which LMs fail but humans do not, raising accuracy more than random removal. A protocol frozen before any output replicates the gap and the lift on Dutch BLiMP-NL. LLM validation makes minimal-pair benchmarks easier for the models they are meant to test; we release code, validator outputs and item-level scores.

</details>


### [156] [Antalia-2 Mini: A 7.6-million-parameter Turkish text-to-speech model trained entirely on synthetic speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13811)
*Sezgin Saygili,Emre Kaplaner,Oncel Ozgul,Fikri San Koktas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Antalia-2 Mini 是一个单声道土耳其语文本转语音模型，具有7620931个参数，主要用于克隆 VoxCPM2 合成语音。该模型对语音模型的声学谱图微调降低了 Whisper 转录的错误率，并在多个基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出了一个单声道土耳其语文本转语音模型Antalia-2 Mini，目的是提高土耳其语语音合成的质量并提供一个开源解决方案。

Method: 模型通过利用更大的教师模型VoxCPM2的合成语音进行克隆和微调，同时采用自监督的方式学习特定语言的发音特点。

Result: Antalia-2 Mini 在多个基准测试中表现出色，WER 和字符错误率均较低，并且在某些测试中优于其他模型。

Conclusion: 该模型在开源条件下取得了显著的成果，并为进一步优化和应用提供了基础。

Abstract: Antalia-2 Mini is a single-voice Turkish text-to-speech model with 7,620,931 parameters. It reads characters and produces 48 kHz audio at 41 times real time on an Apple M5 laptop CPU (2 threads). Its training audio came only from VoxCPM2 (the teacher), about 300 times larger, cloning one synthetic clip; automatic checks kept about 1,790 h. Fine-tuning the vocoder on the acoustic model's own mel spectrograms cut the word error rate (WER) of Whisper's transcripts from a mean of 3.89% to 1.75% at adjacent pre-release checkpoints. The teacher corrupts the start of a clip when the first word begins with ö, the student learned this, and a spoken prefix we cut from the output hides it. On the 495-sentence Freya-TR-Eval benchmark, where we tuned settings, version 1.0.1 has WER 1.09% and character error rate 0.22% on our public harness. A paired bootstrap cannot distinguish these from EMA Lightning, an 8.6M Turkish model, which scores higher on full-band UTMOS, an English-trained quality proxy. No human listening test exists. Weights, inference code and the benchmark harness are public under Apache-2.0; training code and data are not.

</details>


### [157] [PolyRoute: A Twelve-Language and Code-Mixed Benchmark for Typed-Decision Routing, with Baselines and a Leaderboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13843)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Sarun Kumar K S*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发布了PolyRoute数据集，包含322条多语言消息（12种语言和9种书写系统）、156条混杂或罗马化的消息（如Hinglish、Spanglish、Arabizi）以及967条可选的本地训练行。研究还提供了一种三向部门路由决策的协议。实验结果显示，不同的系统在英语根部检查点的表现为61.2%，在发布时的多语言模型为77.3%，在最佳微调模型为96.6%；TF-IDF结合逻辑回归达到了91.9%的准确率。对于四种保留的语言，三个多语言系统分别获得了77%、90%和93%的分数。对于混杂文本，英语根模组以85%的表现略领先于多语言检查点的79%（p=0.134），分差在4分以上才有显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了填补多语言环境下语言决定模型性能评估的空白，研究发布了一个多语言数据集PolyRoute，以评估模型在不同语言和书写系统中的表现。

Method: 研究团队创建了PolyRoute数据集，包含多种语言和书写系统的消息，并使用大型语言模型（如Qwen3.8-27B）进行标注。实验采用了多种方法，如不同的语言模型系统、TF-IDF和逻辑回归，以及三项面向部门路由的决策协议。

Result: 在英语根部检查点，系统的准确率为61.2%；发布时的多语言模型达到了77.3%的准确率；最佳微调模型的表现最优秀，达到了96.6%。TF-IDF与逻辑回归的组合获得了91.9%的准确率。在四种保留的语言中，三个多语言系统分别获得了77%、90%和93%的高分。对于混杂文本，尽管英语根模型的分数略高，但效果差异不显著。

Conclusion: PolyRoute数据集的发布为多语言环境下的语言决定模型提供了一个基准，不同类型的语言模型和方法（如TF-IDF结合逻辑回归）都有其适用场景，未来的工作可以从完善数据标注策略和优化模型微调策略入手，进一步提升多语言系统的表现。

Abstract: Typed-decision encoders answer a question about a text in one forward pass, but their behaviour outside English is rarely measured on a fixed yardstick. We release PolyRoute: 322 native-language support messages in 12 languages and 9 writing systems, 156 code-mixed or romanised messages (Hinglish, Spanglish, Arabizi), 967 optional native training rows, and a protocol for a three-way department-routing decision. Labels come from Qwen3.8-27B with a blind second pass (91% of candidates kept); an independent model, gemma4-31b, agrees on 97.2% of test items, and the largest character 5-gram similarity between any test text and any training text is 0.33. A leaderboard of 13 systems gives 61.2% for the English-root checkpoint, 77.3% for the shipped multilingual one and 96.6% for the best fine-tune; TF-IDF with logistic regression reaches 91.9%. On four withheld languages the three multilingual systems score 77%, 90% and 93%. On code-mixed text the English root (85%) is nominally ahead of the shipped multilingual checkpoint (79%; p=0.134). The split resolves differences only above about 4 points. All texts and labels are LLM-generated.

</details>


### [158] [Alania-2: Turkish text-to-speech with instant and fine-tuned voice cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13845)
*Sezgin Saygili,Emre Kaplaner,Oncel Ozgul,Fikri San Koktas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Alania-2 是一个488M参数的土耳其语音合成系统，带有两个内置声音，并通过自研的 VoxCPM 架构和语言模型基础结构生成连续音频亚空间。系统通过大量训练数据实现显著改进，尤其是在语音识别准确率方面。Voice cloning 方法和一些工程改进也有助于提高最终的合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 TTS 技术的发展，构建支持多种语言的声音合成系统变得日益重要，而 Alania-2 正是为了满足这一需求而进行开发的。

Method: Alania-2 使用了 VoxCPM 架构，该架构包含一个大模型生成的合成语音，再通过扩散头生成连续音频亚空间。系统通过大量的训练数据进行训练，其中包括大量合成语音。此外，还采用了文本规范化等技术以提高合成语音的质量和可懂度。

Result: Alania-2 达到了1.44%的词错误率，相比之前的系统有了显著的改进。在多个指标上，如语言合成和语音识别方面，Alania-2 表现出了优异的效果，并通过实验验证了特定 Voice Cloning 方法的有效性。此外，还展示了工程优化如何显著加快合成功能的性能。

Conclusion: Alania-2 是一个在声音合成方面有显著改进的土耳其语音合成系统，该系统可以提供高质量的语音合成绩效。通过大量的训练数据和特定的 Voice Cloning 技术，Alania-2 显示出了比传统的语音合成系统更好的性能。此外，系统还实现了快速合成和性能优化。

Abstract: We present Alania-2, a Turkish text-to-speech (TTS) system with two built-in voices, voice design from text descriptions, and two kinds of voice cloning. Alania-2 is a 488M-parameter model with the VoxCPM architecture, which generates continuous audio latents with a diffusion head on top of a language-model backbone. We train it from scratch on 18,195 hours of Turkish speech, 90% of which is synthetic speech produced by a larger teacher model and filtered automatically. A text normalizer rewrites numbers, abbreviations and foreign names into the words a Turkish speaker would say; without it, none of 96 test sentences with digits kept all its critical parts, such as an amount or a date, through synthesis and speech recognition, against 86 with it. On Freya-TR-Eval, a public benchmark of 495 Turkish sentences scored with automatic speech recognition, Alania-2 reaches a word error rate of 1.44%, compared with 5.99% for our previous system. On its built-in voices, it comes within half a point of character error rate of a 2.3B-parameter model at under a quarter of the size, and a 7.6M-parameter single-voice model trained with the same recipe matches the most intelligible systems we measured, which suggests that training data matters more for intelligibility than model size. We also report practical findings on voice cloning: conditioning on the reference audio alone, rather than continuing it, cuts skipped words from 21.9% to 1.7%; 20-30 seconds of reference audio works best; and a low-rank adapter trained in about two minutes raises speaker similarity well above instant cloning. Finally, we describe inference-engine changes that bring first audio down to 32 ms for a single stream. We release our evaluation harness and three Turkish speech and text datasets.

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Passive Imitation: On-Policy Mid-Training Distillation for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13855)
*Ruize Zheng,Xuyang Liu,Siyu Ren,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文引入了一种新的中间训练方法OPMD，它通过从不断演化的学生策略中采样并使用冻结的教师模型的密集token级逆KL奖励进行训练，以解决标准的下一个标记预测(NTP)目标在不同基模型上的不同效果问题。该方法在数学基准测试中提高了Qwen学生的性能，并在此类模型上优于标准NTP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的连续预训练方法在不同模型上的表现差异显著，而标准的下一个标记预测(NTP)目标未能优化每个模型的独特需求。

Method: OPMD方法通过在选定的锚点位置从学生策略中采样路径，并使用冻结的教师模型应用密集的token级逆KL奖励，将中间训练从离线文本模仿重新定义为有查询驱动的知识获取。

Result: 在MATH-500和Minerva测试上，OPMD提高了Qwen学生的性能，并优于NTP标准基线的性能。

Conclusion: OPMD方法可以无缝集成到连续预训练的架构中，作为被动领域适应的模型感知替代方案。

Abstract: Continual pre-training (CPT) adapts large language models (LLMs) to specialized domains, yet its standard next-token prediction (NTP) objective treats every corpus token as a fixed target, regardless of its utility to the current model. In a controlled study on the same 1B-token mathematical corpus, we observe sharply different outcomes across base models: NTP improves Llama-3.2-3B's performance on mathematical benchmarks but substantially degrades Qwen3-1.7B-Base's performance, indicating that, under a fixed training recipe, the same training sequences can have model-dependent effects. To address this problem, we introduce On-Policy Mid-Training Distillation (OPMD), which reformulates mid-training from offline text imitation into active, query-driven knowledge acquisition. At selected anchor positions, OPMD samples trajectories from the evolving student policy and applies dense token-level reverse-KL rewards from a frozen teacher, training the model on states it actually visits rather than only on ground-truth prefixes. This on-policy objective reduces the training-generation mismatch of teacher-forced NTP. On MATH-500 and Minerva, OPMD improves the Qwen student over its base performance and outperforms the evaluated NTP baseline, which degrades on both benchmarks. OPMD integrates into CPT pipelines without added inference-time overhead and offers a model-aware alternative to passive domain adaptation.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond Fluent Responses: Finding User Dissatisfaction in Human-AI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13894)
*Yuka Saito,Ikumi Numaya,Jun Suzuki,Reina Akama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究分析了用户不满意的文本，发现使用用户陈述可以检测到隐含的不满意情绪，且异常检测方法特别有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大语言模型生成的响应质量提高，研究转向考虑用户的偏好和感知价值，从而更好地评估对话系统的实际用户体验。

Method: 通过分析带有自述不满意标签的用户陈述来识别隐含的不满意情绪，使用AUPRC评估，结合异常检测方法。

Result: 模型使用用户陈述在AUPRC方面比17.8%的不满意标签普遍存在高出15个百分点，表明用户陈述揭示了隐含不满意的线索；此外，与前一个用户陈述的变化有助于不满意检测，异常检测方法特别有成效。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，用户声明能够识别对话中的隐含不满意，异常检测方法对此特别有效，有助于改进对话系统的满意度评估。

Abstract: As large language models generate increasingly high-quality responses, recent work on response evaluation has moved beyond response-centered criteria, such as correctness and coherence, toward user-centered criteria, including user preferences and perceived usefulness. This shift is fundamentally important for developing technologies that are truly useful to real-world users. While conventional response evaluation quantifies the quality of the response itself, users' satisfaction in real conversations also depends on their subjective preferences and perceptions of usefulness, which do not necessarily align with response quality. This study investigates whether such user-centered signals can be detected from conversational text. Specifically, we analyze user utterances with self-reported dissatisfaction labels to examine whether user dissatisfaction in text can be detected. In English interaction, models using user utterances exceeded the 17.8% dissatisfaction label prevalence in AUPRC by up to 15 percentage points. These results suggest that user utterances reveal cues to implicit dissatisfaction. Furthermore, changes from the immediately preceding user utterance may improve dissatisfaction detection, with anomaly detection methods appearing particularly effective.

</details>


### [161] [AVCC: Audio-Visual Conversation Corpus for Turn-Taking Prediction with a Human Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13957)
*Haotian Qi,Gabriel Skantze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将AVCC数据集转换为单摄像机和麦克风的多说话人下一个人发言预测（NSP）基准，评估了音频和多模态模型的性能，并提供了标注数据、转录和复现脚本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了改善对话代理和社交机器人的实时交互能力，研究下一发言者预测（NSP）对于及时响应至关重要。然而，目前缺乏单一摄像机和麦克风环境下适用于多说话人场景的数据集和评价标准。

Method: 该研究使用了31小时的AVCC双/三说话人在线对话音频-视觉语料库，构建了一个公开的NSP基准。引入了基于静默间隙的发言人独立7视频测试集，并提供了全语料转录和人类参考基准。

Result: 研究评估了音频模型（Role-Relative VAP模型）和多模态模型（MuVAP模型）的表现，取得了较好的预测结果。

Conclusion: 该项研究不仅改进了NSP任务的数据集质量和评价方法，还提供了重要的基准数据和评估模型，有助于推动NSP技术的发展和应用。

Abstract: } Predicting who will speak next, and when, is a prerequisite for conversational agents and social robots that take turns without waiting for silence. Multiparty next-speaker prediction (NSP) has been studied on corpora with per-speaker sensors, each with its own event definitions. No public benchmark covers the single camera and microphone a robot has, and no model has been compared against how well people perform the same prediction. We turn AVCC, a 31-hour audio-visual corpus of two- and three-speaker online conversation recorded with a single static camera and microphone, into a public NSP benchmark. We add a speaker-disjoint seven-video test partition with 865 hold and 865 shift events at mutual-silence gaps, word-level forced-aligned transcripts for the whole corpus, and a human reference benchmark collected at the same events. We score an audio-only Role-Relative VAP model (RoleVAP) and the multimodal MuVAP model. Labels, transcripts, per-response human data, and the scripts that reproduce every number are released.

</details>


### [162] [On the information rate of phones, syllables and words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13990)
*Herman Kamper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析960小时英语文本，比较了不同语言单位（如音素、音节、词、多词组合）的信息率，发现特定情况下，比音节大且小于长多词组合的单位在编码率方面最为高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究试图回答在编码语音内容时，哪一类语言单位（音素、音节、词等）能最小化位速率，这对语音和语言系统的开发以及人类语言处理理论具有重要意义。

Method: 研究使用了一个包含960小时英语文本的转录库，对比了音素、音节、词、多词组合以及介于这些之间的不同分词方式的信息率。使用了 $n$-gram和变压器语言模型来评估。

Result: 结果显示，当模型容量受限时，超过音节但小于长多词组合的单位能提供最优的信息率。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，在有限的模型能力下，介于音节和长多词之间的单位是对语音内容编码最有效的方式。

Abstract: Which linguistic units minimise the bitrate required to encode speech content: phones, syllables, words, maybe larger units, or something in between? This question has implications for the development of speech and language systems and theories of human language processing. Using a transcribed corpus of 960 hours of English audiobooks, I compare the information rates of phones, syllables, words, multi-word combinations and intermediate tokenisations. While all units are equivalent under a perfect language model, it makes sense to compare these under limited modelling budgets; I use $n$-gram and transformer language models. The lowest bitrates are achieved using words, with slightly longer units sometimes performing best. The results show that, when modelling capacity is limited, units larger than syllables but smaller than long multi-word phrases provide the most efficient representation in terms of information rate.

</details>


### [163] [One Sequence Is Enough: Fingerprinting Language Models from Fixed-Length Integer Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14010)
*Fantong Meng,Chengjie Sun,Shuangjia Yang,Zhiyuan Zhao,Yansheng Pei,Xinyang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过使用NumPrint基准数据集，证明了一单一固定长度整数序列可以作为模型的指纹，并提出SHALLOW MLP和因果变换器来捕捉生成序列中的数值偏好和行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型API和第三方平台的实际返回模型可能与声明不符，导致身份验证需求。研究发现简单的数值选择任务偏好可以作为模型指纹，本研究进一步验证了单一整数序列的识别性能。

Method: 研究首先构建了包含5,036个答案的NumPrint基准数据集，包含了51种模型的响应，使用中文、日文和英文提示要求生成1到355之间的整数。然后提出SHALLOW MLP和因果变换器模型来分析整数序列。

Result: 在51类封闭集评估中，SHALLOW MLP和因果变换器分别达到了92.72%、96.06%和97.12%的识别准确率，超过了所有对比方法。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，单一整数序列可以作为有效的模型指纹，且使用更多的答案能够进一步提高模型识别的准确性。

Abstract: Users of model APIs and third-party platforms often rely on the declared model name to identify the model responding to their requests. However, the actual model may differ from the declared one, motivating identity verification based on observable outputs. Previous studies have shown that preferences in simple numerical-choice tasks can serve as model fingerprints. We investigate whether a single fixed-length integer sequence can combine multiple numerical observations to identify its source model. For this task, we introduce NumPrint, a benchmark of 5,036 answers from 51 models, collected using Chinese, Japanese, and English prompts that request 294 integers between 1 and 355 in one response. We propose the Shallow MLP and Causal Transformer (SMCT) to capture aggregate numerical preferences and behavioral patterns in generated sequences. In 51-class closed-set evaluation, SMCT achieves 92.72% accuracy from a single sequence, outperforming all methods compared in this study. Using two and four answers increases accuracy to 96.06% and 97.12%, respectively. These results show that a single integer sequence contains useful model fingerprints and that additional answers further improve identification.

</details>


### [164] [Program-Aided Patchable Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14019)
*Jiahao Wang,Yuqi Xiong,Qing Yang,Zhipeng Xu,Zhenghao Liu,Zulong Chen,Yubin Bao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PASE是一种通过关联可执行程序来迭代进化技能的框架，实现了技能的模块化维护与更新，相较于传统的自然语言中心的方法，PASE在多个模型和基准测试中的表现更佳，显著提升了技能的进化稳定性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将技能主要表示为自然语言文档，导致技能难以维护和演进。因此，PASE提出了通过关联执行程序来迭代优化技能的想法，以克服这一局限。

Method: PASE利用程序来分离和组织技能中的可重复执行逻辑，从而实现对执行组件的针对性更新，减少反复修订自然语言技能的需求，进一步通过实验验证其优越性。

Result: PASE在各种模型和基准测试中表现优越，相比基线方法取得了超过2.8%的性能提升，且展现出更强的进化稳定性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: PASE提供了一种更有效的技能进化框架，其通过程序化的知识提高了技能的维护性和适应性，为未来的强化技能进化研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Agent skills have emerged as an effective means of enabling LLM agents to solve complex, interactive tasks by encoding reusable knowledge without modifying model parameters. Recent approaches further improve skill adaptation by distilling reusable experience from agent execution trajectories and evolving skills based on downstream task feedback. However, existing methods primarily represent skills as natural language documents, intertwining procedural logic with descriptive knowledge and making skills difficult to maintain and evolve. To address this limitation, we propose PASE (Program-Aided Patchable Skill Evolution), a program-aided framework that iteratively evolves skills by associating them with executable programs. Unlike prior approaches that encode trajectory-derived experience primarily in natural language, PASE separates reusable procedural logic into editable programs, enabling targeted updates to executable components while reducing the need to repeatedly revise natural language skills. Extensive experiments across diverse models and benchmarks demonstrate that PASE consistently outperforms all baselines, by achieving over 2.8% improvements. Further analyses show that PASE exhibits greater evolutionary stability and generalisation capability, highlighting the critical role of programmatic knowledge in skill evolution. We will release all data and code via GitHub.

</details>


### [165] [MISO-BR: An Open Corpus for Misogyny Detection in Brazilian Portuguese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14036)
*Fábio A. S. Passos,Péricles B. C. Miranda,Ana Paula Cavalcanti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了MISO-BR语料库，这是首个开放、文档化并可版本化地标注巴西葡萄牙语的二元性别歧视社交媒体文本语料库，结合了基于字典和目标的抓取和手工注释。通过对几种传统分类器和微调后的BERTimbau模型的实验，表明变换器架构在性能上具有显著优势。虽然取得了显著的成果，但仍存在某些限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为巴西的一个独特研究项目，MISO-BR旨在填补对于巴西语社交媒体上性别歧视缺乏开放可审核数据资源的空白，通过引入此语料库可以更好地理解和处理相关问题。

Method: 研究采用了一种结合字典和目标指导的自动化爬取方法和人工注释的策略，注释者依据一个书面指南进行三者之间的核对。MISO-BR通过比较几种传统分类器（包括多项式朴素贝叶斯、伯努利朴素贝叶斯、线性支持向量机和支持向量机）以及微调后的BERT基线和大型模型来评估不同模型的效果。

Result: 实验结果显示，传统模型的平均宏F1得分为0.651至0.676，而微调后的BERT大模型达到了0.833的宏F1得分，证明了其在性别歧视检测方面的优越性能。尽管基线和大模型在测试集上没有显著区别，但训练成本提高了2.6倍。

Conclusion: 该研究强调，MISO-BR的价值主要在于其透明可审计的流水线，而不仅仅是表现优异的模型。研究指出了MISO-BR面临的一些限制，如人为的类别平衡、来源与标签间的关联以及由字典指导的抽样和自动筛选引入的选择偏差。

Abstract: Misogyny is among the most prevalent forms of online hate speech directed at Brazilian users, yet open and auditable resources dedicated to it in Brazilian Portuguese remain scarce. We introduce MISO-BR, an open, documented and versioned corpus of 896 balanced social media texts annotated for binary misogyny, released with its construction protocol and reproducible baselines. The corpus combines lexicon- and target-guided scraping from X with items imported from OLID-BR, a large language model triage stage whose prompt and decoding parameters are released, and manual annotation by three annotators following a written guide. Inter-annotator reliability reaches a Fleiss' kappa of 0.788 over 816 items. Under a single fixed protocol we compare four classical classifiers (Multinomial and Bernoulli Naive Bayes, a linear SVM and a random forest) with fine-tuned BERTimbau Base and Large. The classical models cluster between 0.651 and 0.676 macro-F1 and are precision-oriented but recall-poor. The transformers reach 0.833 macro-F1 and accuracy (Wilson 95% CI for accuracy [0.77, 0.88]), a gap of roughly 16 points, while Base and Large are indistinguishable on the test set despite a 2.6x difference in training cost. We argue that the value of the resource lies less in its headline scores than in the auditability of its pipeline, and we state three limits that users must reckon with: the artificial class balance, whose effect on precision under realistic prevalence we quantify; the association between provenance and label, which the released source field makes measurable; and the selection bias induced by lexicon-guided sampling and automatic triage. The corpus, annotation guide, models and experimental scripts are publicly available.

</details>


### [166] [HandsOnAid: Building and Evaluating an Offline First-Aid Assistant for Emergency Response Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14059)
*Rezwan Islam Salvi,Saiyma Sittul Muna,Mushfiqur Rahman Mushfique*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了HandsOnAid，一种4位Gemma 2B模型，经过QLoRA微调，用于急救问答。研究通过比较不同配置，发现4位微调模型结合检索机制的配置表现最佳，能在无网络连接的手机设备上快速生成答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧急情况下缺少信号和救护车时，手机上小型语言模型能提供帮助，但其建议必须确保安全性和准确性。

Method: 研究团队使用FirstAidQA数据集（包含5,550个急救问答对）对Gemma 2B模型进行微调，并评估了七个配置版本，包括不同位宽的基线模型、安全门控和检索机制。此外，还使用了一个包含41个问题-答案对照组的银行进行盲评分和关键词审核。

Result: 微调模型在评分上优于基线模型，微调4位模型结合检索机制达到最高平均分为2.51，接近满分5分。设备测试显示，该4位微调模型能在无网络连接的智能设备上迅速生成答案。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，经过充分微调和合理配置的语言模型在无信号紧急情况下可以提供有效的急救指导。

Abstract: Emergencies can occur where there is no phone signal and no ambulance nearby. A small language model on a phone can be helpful in such situations, but its advice needs to be safe and accurate. We built HandsOnAid, a 4-bit Gemma 2B model fine-tuned with QLoRA on FirstAidQA dataset, a corpus of 5,550 first-aid question-answer pairs. We compared seven configurations, including the base, 4-bit and 8-bit fine-tuned models, and versions with a length floor, safety gate, or retrieval. We used a 41-pair question-answer bank with medically reviewed references that followed ANZCOR guidelines, and a strict 0-5 score rubric, with twelve safety overrides. Four frontier LLM judges (GPT-5.6 Sol, Claude Opus 5, DeepSeek V4 Pro, GLM-5.3) blindly scored every answer, with 45 hidden controls used to check judge reliability. Fine-tuning improved the mean score by +1.08 over the base model to 2.34. The best configuration scored 2.51 out of 5 which was the fine-tuned 4-bit model with retrieval. A medical professional also conducted a blind evaluation of 40 answers across the base, the 4-bit fine-tuned, and the retrieval configurations, and the scores were similar to the LLM judges' average scores. A keyword audit found limited coverage of several details of ANZCOR guidelines in the training corpus, that are required by the rubric, which suggests that missing information in the training data could be a cause of the low scores. On a smartphone device (Pixel 7 Pro) with six CPU threads and no network connection, the 4-bit fine-tuned model produced its first displayable text in 6.86 seconds and a complete answer in 14.88 seconds on average over ten questions.

</details>


### [167] [From Bias to Positionality: A critical grammar for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14079)
*Dave Murray-Rust,Elisa Giaccardi,Federico Bomba,Maria Luce Lupetti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文指出了机器学习系统中偏见的定义和使用方式存在误导性，并提出位置性（positionality）是描述这些模型更合适的语法。位置性描述了模型所持的立场及其背后的原因，包括模型创作者、意图行为和实际行为等多个层面。文章还探讨了模型的位置性如何影响其解释、应用及问责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提出一个更全面和准确的框架来理解和评估机器学习模型的偏见，作者认为传统上用偏见来描述模型行为的方式过于简单化，需要引入新的概念来更加细致地揭示模型背后复杂的社会和文化因素。

Method: 作者通过分析现有文献中的模型应用案例以及对位置性的定义和描述进行了深入探讨，从而构建了一个多层次的框架来理解和解释模型在各种不同场景下的行为和影响。

Result: 提出了“位置性”的概念框架，有助于更好地理解机器学习模型中的社会和文化因素，将其置于更大的背景中进行分析，以便更准确地评估这些模型的偏见和潜在影响。

Conclusion: 本文的结论是，在使用和评估机器学习模型时，不仅要关注模型本身的技术特性，还必须充分考虑其位置性所带来的影响，这样才能更全面地理解模型的实际应用意义。

Abstract: Bias has become a default lens for understanding what machine learning systems do and how we should relate to them. Bias is at root a deviation from a standard; in decision making systems we can supply a standard from outside: a law, a protected attribute, a benchmark, a statistical property. However, even there, biased is often used as a shorthand, without stating what the position taken is - a silent ``relative to''. In a general use of language models, nothing supplies the standard: there is no decision to audit and no counterfactual output, so the shorthand fails. We argue that positionality -- the located, articulated, owned and contestable position from which anything is said -- is the better grammar for the question these models now force: who is speaking, from where? Against the vendors' answer of nowhere and the seductive possibility of everywhere, we argue that when a model is taken as-if speaking, it speaks from somewhere, and this means positionality can describe models, and the way they are believed beyond their warrant, even though they are not accountable speakers. A model's positionality is laminar -- laid down across corpus, tuning, system prompt, application and the live conversation. We separate three sets of factors: the positionality of a model's creators, the behaviour they intend for it, and the behaviour it enacts, and situate current work within this matrix. This lets us highlight what each stratum inherits from the one below, with ruptures and collapses where that inheritance is disavowed. We then return to where models speak from, and discuss the implications for how models are made, read and held accountable.

</details>


### [168] [Sckwoky at SHROOM-Visions: Character-Level Hallucination-Span Detection in Image-Conditioned VLMs via Dual-Prompted LoRA Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14100)
*Mikhail Kulakov,Ruslan Solikhov,Ivan Bondarenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该系统在多语言图像条件语言模型响应中识别字符级幻觉跨度，性能显著优于官方基线，尤其是通过重复候选答案增强双向上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在SHROOM-visions 2026任务中，开发高效准确检测图像条件语言模型中字符级幻觉的技术，特别是针对多语言环境下的应用。

Method: 系统基于冻结的Gemma-4-12B-it骨干模型，并通过可训练的LoRA适配器增强，通过重复候选答案来提供近似双向上下文，并结合内部隐藏状态预测幻觉概率、BIO边界和响应级规避分数。

Result: 在闭合测试集上，该系统的字符交并比达到0.4404，超过官方基线的两倍多（0.1854），并在四种语言的IoU排名中位列5-6位。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了通过重复答案和利用图像-答案配对能够有效检测幻觉，且在相同模型不需独立验证的情况下，该方法对于本地问答系统是实用的。

Abstract: We present our system for the SHROOM-visions 2026 shared task on character-level hallucination span detection in image-conditioned vision-language model (VLM) responses, evaluated across English, French, Italian, and Chinese. The detector is built on a frozen Gemma-4-12B-it backbone with trainable LoRA adapters (approximately 10.7M parameters). Because the backbone is a causal decoder, hidden states read from a single copy of an answer cannot attend to subsequent tokens; we therefore repeat the candidate answer within one prompt and extract features from its second occurrence, giving every position approximate bidirectional context in a single non-autoregressive pass. A character-level locator combines intermediate hidden states to predict hallucination probabilities, BIO boundaries, and a response-level abstention score, while separate language-specific classifiers assign one of five hallucination types to each predicted span. On the closed test set, our system reaches a macro-averaged character IoU of 0.4404, more than doubling the official baseline (0.1854), and ranks 5th-6th on IoU across the four languages. On the English tuning split, controlled ablations attribute a gain to the repeated answer that is comparable to that of LoRA adaptation (its held-out gain is smaller and concentrated on hallucinated answers), and they show that the detector relies on the image-answer pairing: shuffling images across examples drives IoU down to the level of the predict-nothing floor or below it in every language. In deployments where the same Gemma-4-12B-it model also generates answers, its base weights can be reused, making the approach practical for local question-answering systems that cannot run a separate verifier.

</details>


### [169] [Visual Similarity in Adult Word Extension and Artificial Word Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14116)
*Patricio M. Vera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究分析了两个共享名称的对象的视觉相似性如何解释它们之间的关联，并探讨了教学示例的作用。通过对成人词汇扩展研究的再分析以及使用人工学习者的实验，研究发现视觉相似性和示例组成共同影响了词汇扩展的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探讨物体名称共享时，视觉相似性对词汇扩展的影响以及额外教学示例所带来的作用。

Method: 研究采用了再分析成人词汇扩展数据的方法，还包括了一个使用相同视觉编码器的人工学习者实验。探索了类别间距离对单词学习准确度的影响，并通过对比不同类别分离度的人工学习结果，分析了视觉相似性和人类推断距离之间的关系。

Result: 研究结果表明，视觉相似性与词汇扩展之间存在显著关联。在人工学习者实验中，类别间距离越远，单词学习的准确性越低。此外，示例的组成也会影响成人词汇扩展的准确度，当示例是下属类别时，单词被扩展到上位类别的概率较小，而当示例是上位类别时，概率大幅增加。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，视觉相似性在解释一般化方面有一定的作用，而示例组成的特性决定了它所不能涵盖的内容。

Abstract: When two objects share a name, how much does their visual similarity explain, and what do the teaching examples add? First, we reanalyzed trial-level data from a large adult word-extension study (Lewis & Frank, 2018), computing each test image's distance to the examples shown in the space of a frozen self-supervised visual encoder (DINOv2). Distance was associated with extension (log odds -3.23 per standard deviation; 520 people), and an exploratory post hoc analysis suggested that this association persisted within the taught domain after controlling for taxonomic level and teaching condition. Example composition also mattered: after three examples, adults extended a word to 1.1% of superordinate items when the examples were subordinate and to 77.5% when they were superordinate. Second, in an artificial learner using the same encoder, words joining two categories were learned less accurately the farther apart the categories were (84.7%, 65.4%, and 53.0% naming accuracy for near, mid, and far pairs). The encoder's distances correlated with human similarity and confusability measures from three datasets (rho = .501-.795), but this representational alignment did not translate into conclusive evidence that human-derived distances predict learner difficulty, and a second encoder trained on one child's experience reproduced the tier ordering but not the pair-level effect. The secondary human data were analyzed under an internal plan fixed before the confirmatory analyses (not externally preregistered). Visual similarity accounts for part of how generalization is organized in both systems, while the composition of examples contributes what the distance we used does not capture.

</details>


### [170] [Representational Atoms in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14168)
*Chenhui Hu,Pengfei Cao,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究定义了基本表征单元（FRUs），并证明了阈值激活稀疏自编码器（TSAEs）可以在适当条件下以高保真度和稳定性识别FRUs。进一步分析表明，具有更高稳定性的FRUs具有更大的单义性，并且可以跨层组合以恢复与人类概念结构相对应的语义组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络内部表示以及它们与人类概念结构之间的关系是阐明模型机制的关键问题。定义基本表征单元（FRUs）并提出了评估准则，旨在提供统一框架来定义、评估和识别FRUs，从而为理解神经网络表示和人类概念结构之间的关系奠定基础。

Method: 研究通过定义基本表征单元（FRUs）并提出评价准则（保真度和稳定性），证明了阈值激活稀疏自编码器（TSAEs）在适当条件下能够以高保真度和稳定性识别FRUs，通过大量实验进一步验证了这一结论。

Result: 研究发现具有更高稳定性的基本表征单元（FRUs）具有更大的单义性，并能够跨层组合以恢复与人类概念结构相对应的语义组织。

Conclusion: 研究为定义、评估和识别神经网络中的基本表征单元提供了统一框架，建立了描述神经网络表示与人类概念结构之间关系的基础。

Abstract: Understanding the internal representations of neural networks and characterizing their relationship with human conceptual structure are two critical questions for elucidating model mechanisms. The former reveals how learned knowledge is organized in internal representations, whereas the latter provides a basis for interpreting these representations through human conceptual structure. Here, we formally define fundamental representational units (FRUs) and establish two evaluation criteria: fidelity and stability. We further prove that threshold-activated sparse autoencoders (TSAEs) can identify FRUs with high fidelity and stability under appropriate conditions, and validate this result through large-scale experiments. Further analyses reveal that FRUs with higher stability exhibit greater monosemanticity and can be composed across layers to recover semantic organization corresponding to human conceptual structure. These results provide a unified framework for defining, evaluating and identifying FRUs in neural networks, and establish a foundation for characterizing the relationship between neural network representations and human conceptual structure.

</details>


### [171] [Stagewise and interpretable emergence of inside-outside computation in Transformer-based language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14221)
*Jack T. Parley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过合成上下文自由文法研究变体句法结构和解析模糊性，发现Transformer的学习动态可以完全解析，并且能够清晰地关联到隐藏的潜在树变量，从而缓解解析模糊并利用广泛上下文信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLMs需要处理自然语言中的句法结构变体和解析模糊性。虽然先前研究表明完全训练的Transformer确实模拟了经典解析算法（如inside-outside），但缺乏理论证明它们是如何学习这些算法的。

Method: 研究使用了合成上下文自由文法，研究Transformer在变量解析树拓扑结构中的学习动态。通过逐阶段的学习曲线，能够分析每个损失阶段并清楚地关联到隐藏的潜在树变量的表现。

Result: 在早期阶段，局部（二元/三元）信息主导且仅涉及网络的前几层。随着训练的进行，Transformer形成了编码-解码结构，在中间部分编码最抽象的潜在信息，随后的一半网络用于逐步细化最终的标记预测，这种逐步学习机制与真实世界中的LLMs中的发现相似。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了Transformer在处理句法结构变体时的组织计算方式，并表明可以通过分析这种结构来理解解析算法的学习过程，从而可能推动理论进步。

Abstract: Variability in syntactic structure and parsing ambiguity are fundamental aspects of natural language which modern LLMs have to learn to deal with proficiently. Previous "post-mortem" studies have shown that fully trained Transformers indeed approximate canonical parsing algorithms such as inside-outside, but little is established theoretically about how this is actually learned. Here, we make a first step towards addressing this by studying the learning dynamics in synthetic context-free grammars with variable parse tree topology. The stagewise learning curves are fully interpretable, in the sense that all of the loss stages a) can be computed analytically, and b) can be clearly associated to the representation of a hidden latent tree variable, which allows to lift ambiguity and utilize information from wider contexts. By probing representations, we visualize the emergence of inside-outside computation across depth and during training: while during early stages local (bigram/trigram) information is used and involves only a few initial layers, gradually the Transformer develops an encoder-decoder structure where the most abstract latent information is encoded in the middle, and the latter half of the network is used to progressively refine the final token prediction. This organization of computation is reminiscent of findings in real-world LLMs, and importantly applies here to a synthetic model which allows full analytical control of the data statistics, thus opening the door to theoretical progress.

</details>


### [172] [StaRSE: Compact Russian Sentence Embeddings with a Sign-Coded Static Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14236)
*Boris Malashenko,Ivan Jarsky,Egor Soldatov,Vladislav Luzhnov,Sviatoslav Stumpf,Vladislav Shmatkov,Viacheslav Shalamov,Andrey Stankevich,Valeria Efimova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StaRSE 是一种紧凑的俄语静态句子编码器，通过在部署时移除 Transformer 推断，将嵌入大小减小到 11.3 MiB，同时保持高质量的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Transformer 句子编码器在 CPU 上进行推理或频繁更新时效率低下，StaRSE 旨在通过简化推理过程来解决这一问题。

Method: StaRSE 通过将 token 方向存储为符号位，token 规模存储在单独的部分，并通过归一化加权平均符号方向来进行句子编码。它还通过对比式后期训练直接优化符号投影的 token 表。

Result: 在 RuMTEB 上，StaRSE 达到任务均值 51.16，比最强的静态基线高 7.98 分。其模型大小为 11.3 MiB，而具有相似质量的上下文编码器的参数大小约为 55.7 MiB 和 CPU 延迟为 1.43 ms，相比之下，StaRSE 的 CPU 延迟为 0.08 ms。

Conclusion: StaRSE 在保持高质量的同时，通过移除推理过程显著降低了模型大小和 CPU 延迟，展示了在资源受限环境中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Transformer sentence encoders provide strong retrieval quality, but their inference cost becomes a bottleneck when embeddings must be produced on CPUs or refreshed frequently. We introduce StaRSE, a compact Russian static sentence encoder that removes Transformer inference at deployment time: token directions are stored as sign bits, token norms are stored separately, and sentences are encoded by mean pooling over norm-scaled sign directions. StaRSE is initialized from ruBERT-base, fine-tuned on Russian adjacent-sentence and task-shaped pairs, and post-trained with a contrastive stage that directly optimizes the sign-projected token table. On RuMTEB, StaRSE reaches a task mean of $51.16$, $7.98$ points above the strongest included static baseline. The packaged model occupies $11.3$ MiB and has a mean CPU latency of $0.08$ ms per sentence, compared with an estimated $55.7$ MiB 16-bit parameter footprint and $1.43$ ms mean CPU latency for the closest-quality contextual encoder. Ablations examine adjacent-sentence supervision, token norms, and direct sign-projected post-training.

</details>


### [173] [Dyship: Leveraging Acoustic Features For Speaker Relationship Classification From Dyadic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14241)
*Kieran Sturges,Thomas Thebaud,Yaohan Guan,Najim Dehak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DyShip框架通过比较五种语音表示方法（频谱特征、MFCC、WavLM、x-vectors和发音运动估计），展示了频谱特征和WavLM在识别语音熟悉度和亲属关系方面的优越性，而x-vectors和发音运动在亲属关系分类中表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探索不同表示方法在推断人际关系中的有效性，研究者引入了DyShip框架，利用Seamless Interaction语料库中的对话数据进行分析。

Method: 研究者选取了五种不同的语音表示方法：频谱特征、MFCC（梅尔频率倒谱系数）、自监督WavLM表示、x-vectors和发音运动估计，对它们在熟悉度和亲属关系分类任务上的表现进行了比较。

Result: 频谱特征在熟悉度分类任务中表现最佳，准确率达到87.81%；WavLM在亲属关系分类任务中表现最好，准确率为85.17%；x-vectors和发音运动估计在亲属关系分类中表现较为突出。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，传统的声学特征在人际关系推断中具有潜在的应用价值，并且不同的表示方法捕捉到了不同的对话线索，进一步的研究可以促进对该领域的理解。

Abstract: We introduce DyShip, a framework for classifying speaker familiarity and kinship from dyadic conversations in the Seamless Interaction corpus. We compare five speech representations: spectrograms, MFCCs, self-supervised WavLM representations, x-vectors, and estimated articulatory movements. Spectral features perform best for familiarity classification, reaching an accuracy of 87.81%, while WavLM achieves the highest kinship accuracy of 85.17%. X-vectors and articulatory features yield weaker results on familiarity classifiaction, but shine for kinship classification, with an AUC of 0.909 for x-vectors. Most classifiers exceed previously reported human and audio language model scores on the same dataset. These findings highlight the potential of conventional acoustic features for relationship inference and motivate further investigation of the conversational cues captured by different representations.

</details>


### [174] [An AI Assistant for Automating Anti-Corruption Expertise of Draft Legislation Based on the TRACE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14244)
*Serhii Zabolotnii,Alina Tarasova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于TRACE架构的AI助手，用于自动化审查立法草案中的腐败风险。通过结合确定性解析核心、模型引导的单个条文评估、基于规则的因素分配以及外部人工监督，该系统评估了100份立法草案，并在匹配和评估风险管理方面表现出一定的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有的法律法规筛查过程复杂且耗时的问题，研究者设计了一种自动化工具来辅助反腐败工作，旨在提高筛查效率并减少人为错误。

Method: 研究使用了TRACE架构，包括确定性解析核心，模型引导的单个条文评估，基于规则的因素分配以及外部人工监督。评估数据来自于乌克兰国家反腐败局公布的132份法案和474项模型提取发现。系统评价标准针对两种风险判断标准：严格标准和宽松标准，评测系统的匹配率。

Result: 在100份立法草案中，系统匹配了0.28到0.43不等的发现，具体取决于所使用的模型。大部分的失败是因为系统筛选出的条款评估为不同的风险。在未用于调优的21项法案中，该系统在严格标准下优于完整的提示方法，但在宽松标准下则不如。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，虽然该系统在识别特定风险管理方面已取得一定成效，仍需改进以提高准确度，特别是在评估不同风险水平方面。未来的工作将集中在优化系统模型和提高外部人工监督的准确性。

Abstract: We present an AI assistant for automating anti-corruption expertise of draft legislation based on the TRACE architecture, designed to screen bills for corruption risks: pinpoint provisions an expert would criticise and name the regulatory defect in each. The pipeline combines a deterministic parsing core, model-guided single-provision qualification, rule-based factor assignment, and external human oversight. To evaluate the assistant, we constructed an open benchmark from the published conclusions of Ukraine's National Agency on Corruption Prevention (NAZK), comprising 132 draft laws with 474 model-extracted findings. A system flag agrees with a finding when it targets a compatible provision and an LLM judge rates the risk as the same (strict) or partly the same (lenient). On 100 draft laws, the pipeline matches 0.28 of the findings within its top ten flags using the local Gemma 4 31B model, 0.30 with Sonnet 5.5, and 0.43-0.46 across all flags (non-blind; the frozen blind baseline achieves 0.21, 95% CI 0.16-0.26). Across all runs, most misses occur on provisions that the system located but qualified as a different risk, and none of the 32 legal-collision findings was matched. On 21 acts held out from tuning, the pipeline outperforms a monolithic prompt containing the full methodology under the strict measure, though not under the lenient one, and Opus 5.5 adds nothing over Sonnet. The reported scores reflect agreement with model-extracted findings rated by an LLM judge whose two access routes agree on 73 of 74 labels, without human expert validation.

</details>


### [175] [Semantic Chains: From Reachable Facts to Justified Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14252)
*Misha Andreev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了通过构建逻辑事实的图模型来找到支持答案的事实并将它们作为一系列连接陈述的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了从提出的问题中找到支持答案的逻辑事实并将其以连贯的方式呈现。

Method: 通过将上下文建模为逻辑事实的图，并使用保护边允许在完整表示中恢复子事实的内容，以及从问题中的对象开始搜索到达根事实，最终形成一个连接问题和完整答案的单链接。

Result: 提出了一个模型，该模型可以在给定表达式的前提下找到证明答案的事实，并且通过保护边能够恢复子事实的内容。同时，论文还提供了一种搜索方法，从问题中的对象出发，达到根事实，覆盖整个事实扩展，从而支持从问题到答案的完整链接。

Conclusion: 最终结论是证明答案的逻辑事实链可以通过上述方法实现，并以单链接形式支持从问题到答案的连接。

Abstract: How can the facts that justify an answer be found from a question and presented as a sequence of connected statements? This paper models a context as a graph of logical facts and takes as given which expressions represent each fact. A parent fact can express a relation among its subfacts, and a preserving edge allows the content of a subfact to be recovered within a full representation of its parent. Suppose a root fact justifies the full answer and a path of preserving edges leads from that root to a fact that designates an object shared by the question and the answer. Under the conditions of the model, a search starting from the objects in the question reaches the root and covers every fact in the selected expansion. The root then admits a text that forms a single link connecting the question to the full answer and justifying it. For chains with several links, the paper gives a complete enumeration of candidate orders within the stated class and specifies when the link texts jointly preserve the justification and support every transition.

</details>


### [176] [Point-in-Time Encoders for Earnings Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14325)
*Alessio Brini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 从412,248份财报电话会议中训练了四个不同前训练食谱的模型版本，并且每个版本只读取截止日期之前的文本。这种专门针对电话会议训练的模型版本在检索答案和预测收益惊喜方面表现更佳，但对预测回报的效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服前瞻偏差（look-ahead bias），研究人员使用不同年份截止的文本训练了四个版本的模型，确保模型在测试时不会提前接触到未来的文本信息，从而提升了模型在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 研究人员选取了412,248个电话会议文本，并依据每年的一个截止日期，将这些文本划分为不同版本的模型训练集。训练后的模型不仅用于直接的问答检索，还进一步用于预测收益惊喜和股票回报。

Result: 经过单独电话会议文本训练的模型版本，在检索答案和预测收益惊喜方面表现优异，但对股票回报预测的效果有限。这些模型的向后版本也表现出更好的回复质量。

Conclusion: 最新的2024版本的模型在某些方面表现出提升，尤其是在收益惊喜的预测上，但它对股票回报预测的效果并不显著。点时间版本的模型主要在检索方面的表现更佳。

Abstract: Text encoders turn earnings calls into vectors for search, for language models that answer from retrieved passages, and for forecasting models. A live retriever can use an encoder trained once on all available text, as finance retrievers are, but a backtest needs an encoder that read no text written after the date of each vector. To address this look-ahead bias, from 412,248 calls we train vintages under four pretraining recipes, with yearly cutoffs from 2010 to 2024, and each vintage reads only text dated on or before its cutoff. We then fine-tune each vintage on the question-answer pairs of the calls themselves. The vintages we train on calls alone, with 33.8M parameters, retrieve answers on held-out calls better than the general retrievers we tested in every test quarter. Their vectors also raise by about half the rank correlation between next-day returns and forecasts built from past returns and firm size. When each vintage continues the previous one, a later vintage retrieves better on earlier quarters than the vintage available at the time, and a point-in-time language model that answers from the passages the later vintage retrieves inherits the gain. The 2024 vintage of our families also forecasts earnings surprises better, although the interval of this gain includes zero on calls that no vintage read. It forecasts returns no better, so point-in-time vintages matter most for retrieval and little for forecasts of returns.

</details>


### [177] [ARE-bench: Benchmarking Agents' Research Ability in Real-World Researcher Sessions and Workspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14364)
*Jiahang Lin,Wanying Ren,Wanghan Xu,Huayu Sha,Yang Shu,Zhonghang Lu,Chengjun Pan,Yi Dong,Shichun Liu,Yuyang Hu,Shihan Dou,Honglin Guo,Huijie Lv,Pujun Zheng,Zhe Sun,Yuankai Ying,Yiding Wang,Jiahao Chen,Zhixiong Yang,Dingbo Yuan,Yicheng Zou,Zhecan Wang,Guoxiu He,Xiaoyu Xing,Qi Zhang,Tao Gui,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ARE-bench 提供了针对研究代理的评估基准，强调基于实际会话背景和正在进行的工作区，这些代理不仅需要完成独立任务，还需尊重先前决策和现有工作空间状态。该基准包括来自研究人员真实项目的75个任务，涵盖七个研究阶段、33项要求和150个子要求，共计1,173个二元核查项。结果显示，最先进的模型在66.2%的任务上未能通过所有核查项，显示出在考虑历史和材料要求时的显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究代理基准主要关注独立任务和相对封闭的工作流程，但在实际项目中，研究人员需要代理处理依赖于之前决策和工作空间状态的逐步任务。ARE-bench 旨在填补这一空白，提供真实会话背景和正在进行的工作空间来评估代理。

Method: ARE-bench 包含了75个任务，基于招募的研究人员的真实项目会话和完整工作区。每个任务均基于真实用户请求，保持其要求，并总结了仍然有效的先前请求作为背景。完整的工作区在边界处重播而无需清理，保留了之前轮次中的艺术品。核查清单来自意图对齐的标准并认可任何有效的解决方法。

Result: 七个前沿模型中的最强者在66.2%的任务上未能通过所有核查项，尤其是在历史和材料或工作空间要求的检验中，这些模型通过率仅为28.4%至40.0%。这表明，没有这些上下文要求评估交付可能会高估研究代理并掩盖它们之间的差异。

Conclusion: ARE-bench 提供了一个全面的评估框架，强调研究代理在现实世界中的表现，从而揭示了当前模型面临的挑战。结果表明，代理在复杂的工作流中表现不佳，尤其是在考虑到完整的工作背景时。

Abstract: Research agents achieve increasingly strong benchmark scores, yet these scores do not necessarily translate into reliable delivery in ongoing research projects. Many research-agent benchmarks emphasize standalone tasks and relatively self-contained workflows in clean or curated workspaces. However, researchers ask agents to complete one step within an ongoing project, where requests depend on earlier session decisions and workspaces contain unfinished files, outdated versions, and failed attempts. We introduce ARE-bench to evaluate whether research agents advance ongoing projects while respecting prior decisions and existing workspace state, grounded in real session context and in-progress workspaces. ARE-bench includes 75 tasks built on agent sessions and full workspaces from recruited researchers' own projects, spanning seven research stages, 33 requirements, and 150 sub-requirements, with 1,173 binary checklist items in total. Each task is anchored at a real user request, preserves its requirements, and summarizes the earlier requests still in effect as background. The complete workspace is replayed to that boundary without cleanup, preserving artifacts from earlier turns. Each checklist is compiled from an intent-aligned rubric and credits any valid solution. Across seven frontier models, the strongest passes every checklist item on only 66.2% of tasks, and Design and Execution rank among the hardest stages for every model. Three models pass about 69% of tasks when only delivery contracts and general norms are checked, but just 28.4% to 40.0% once historical and material or workspace requirements are also checked. Evaluating delivery without these contextual requirements can therefore overestimate research agents and mask differences between them.

</details>


### [178] [Blurring the Block Boundaries: Overlapping Block Denoising for Efficient Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14387)
*Amir Mann,Jonathan Fhima,Shaked Brody,Or Avitan,Asif Zamir,Shahar Tsiper,Niv Nayman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: B3 提出了一种无需训练和超参数的方法，可以在保持生成质量的同时加速块扩散模型，使扩散语言模型(DLMs)在延迟和质量之间达到更好的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的块扩散模型由于每个块的速度受限于最慢的 token，导致整体速度较慢。为了提高 DLMs 的生成速度，研究提出了一种名为 Blurring the Block Boundaries (B3) 的方法。

Method: B3 方法通过在共享前向传递中并行优化两个块，并在模型准备好生成下一个块时才打开下一个块，从而实现加速。它是一个无需训练和超参数设置的加速方法。

Result: 在不同难度级别的 10 个数据集上评估了最先进的 DLMs，B3 在保持与基础模型相同的生成质量的同时提供了 11.5-14.5% 的加速。

Conclusion: B3 提供了一种新的方法，可以无缝地与现有的自我编辑块扩散模型集成，从而在确保生成质量的同时显著提升了 DLMs 的速度，从而推动了延迟-质量权衡的前沿。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as effective alternatives to autoregressive models for latency-critical tasks, enabling faster token generation. Recent advances employ block diffusion with self-editing capabilities, where each block refines tokens iteratively before advancing to the next one. Yet each block is only as fast as its slowest token, and the next block cannot start before it. We suggest Blurring the Block Boundaries (B3), a novel method with which two blocks are refined in parallel within a shared forward pass. B3 calibrates itself on the fly, opening the next block only once the model is ready to start generating it. It is a training-free and hyperparameter-free acceleration method which seamlessly integrates within any self-editing block diffusion model. Evaluated on state-of-the-art DLMs across 10 diverse datasets spanning varied tasks and difficulty levels, B3 results in on par generation quality and 11.5-14.5% speedup compared to the base model, pushing the Pareto frontier for latency-quality trade-offs in DLM inference.

</details>


### [179] [When does text encoding matter for language modeling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14459)
*Eugene Jang,Catherine Arnett,Terra Blevins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，文本编码对字节级语言模型的性能有显著影响。尽管信息密集型语言（如中文、韩语）从压缩编码中受益颇多，但信息量较少的语言（如格鲁吉亚语、泰米尔语）对编码选择敏感度不高。因此，建议语言编码应是语言特定的设计决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的文本编码标准（如UTF-8）在跨语言模型中引发了不平等，本文旨在探讨不同的文本编码策略如何影响模型性能。

Method: 研究通过对四种不同语言的实验，比较了UTF-8与其他多种编码策略（包括其他基于Unicode的方法、特定语言编码及自定义编码）的效果。

Result: 实验发现，信息密集型语言从压缩编码中受益明显，而信息量较少的语言对编码选择变化几乎无反应。

Conclusion: 本文得出结论，文本编码应基于语言的特性进行设计，而非采用统一标准。

Abstract: UTF-8's role as the de facto encoding standard in language models introduces cross-lingual inequities due to its variable-length design. However, the effect that this encoding choice has on model performance remains largely unexamined. In this work, we investigate how text encoding impacts the performance of byte-level language models, comparing UTF-8 with a wide range of other encoding strategies, including other Unicode-based approaches, language-specific encodings, and our own customized encodings. To facilitate our analysis, we propose Aether: a hybrid encoding scheme that uses script-specific encodings for our target setting while maintaining full Unicode coverage through UTF-8 fallbacks, using this framework to model four orthographically diverse languages. Surprisingly, we find that while languages with information-dense characters (Chinese, Korean) significantly benefit from compressive encodings, languages whose characters carry less information individually (Georgian, Tamil) are largely insensitive to encoding choice. In light of these findings, we argue that text encoding should be a language-specific design decision, rather than a universal default.

</details>


### [180] [Pterodactyl: Pushing the Limits of Efficient Automated Translation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14480)
*Evelyn Y. Garland,Carola F. Berger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了1020 Group为WMT26共享任务中的自动质量评估提交的系统Pterodactyl，该系统专为段落级质量评分预测和检测无错误段落设计。Pterodactyl是一种高度可定制、低成本的系统，采用LLM作为评估者的做法，通过ROC分析指导提示工程和模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于WMT26共享任务中自动化质量评估的需求，1020 Group开发了Pterodactyl系统以应对子任务2和3的具体要求，即段落级质量评分预测和无错误段落检测。

Method: Pterodactyl系统基于LLM作为评估者的框架，通过ROC分析指导提示工程和模型选择，旨在实现高效的二元分类以优化翻译筛选和数据整理。

Result: Pterodactyl系统针对不同子任务进行了若干变体设计，并在翻译质量评估的不同场合中得到了有效的应用。

Conclusion: Pterodactyl系统在翻译质量评估领域展现出了良好的性能，特别是在自动质量评估子任务中具有较高的准确性和效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we present 1020 Group's submission to the WMT26 shared task on automated quality evaluation. Our system, Pterodactyl, was engineered specifically for subtasks 2 and 3, that is, segment-level quality score prediction and the detection of error-free segments. Pterodactyl is a highly customizable, ultra-low cost system optimized for binary classification in translation triage and data curation. It is based on an LLM-as-a-judge approach, with prompt engineering and model selection guided by ROC analysis.
