We also developed several variants of Pterodactyl that were specifically adapted for subtasks 3.

</details>


### [181] [Mechanistic Interpretability Needs a Logic of Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14483)
*Zhimin Hu,Chen Jiang,Hanbo Xie,N. Apurva Ratan Murty,Sashank Varma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了神经科学和机制可解释性在从复杂神经系统推断机制方面的共同努力，提出了五项原则以促进更严格的推理过程，并为科学发现提供框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为，随着机制可解释性（MI）工具的进步，关注点已从模型内部的观察转向这些观察为机制解释提供的支持。为了应对这种挑战，提出了一种框架来帮助组织机制推理。

Method: 通过回顾神经科学和机制可解释性领域的研究成果，识别了在复杂神经系统的推理中反复出现的障碍，并据此提出了五项原则。

Result: 提出了五项机制推理的原则组织成一个递归逻辑，支持在模型内外科学发现。

Conclusion: 虽然机制可解释性证据是必要的，但它需要其他证据的支持，才能使模型机制与现实世界联系起来。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly studied not merely as predictive systems, but as complex systems whose internal computations are themselves objects of scientific inquiry. Recent progress in mechanistic interpretability (MI) has made it possible to identify neurons, features, circuits, population structures, and dynamical patterns associated with model behavior. As these tools improve, attention is shifting from what can be observed inside a model to how those observations support mechanistic explanations. We examine the parallel efforts of neuroscience and MI to address this question and identify recurring inferential bottlenecks when inferring mechanisms from complex neural systems. We propose five principles and organize them into a recursive logic for a more rigorous procedure of mechanistic inference. These principles provide a framework that supports scientific discovery in two senses: it helps turn local interpretability findings into cumulative knowledge of mechanisms within models, and it makes those mechanisms sufficiently precise to generate testable hypotheses about domains beyond the models. Evidence beyond MI is ultimately required to establish what model mechanisms reveal about the world.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Priority-Aware Deficit Round Robin for AXI4 Bandwidth Fairness in ASAP-7nm Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12979)
*Tasmin Khan,Tishna Sabrina,Ch. Md. Rakin Haider*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AXI4的QoS感知动态公平分配算法，该算法在不拆分数据包的前提下实现了公平的带宽分配，并且在公平性、读取延迟和数据包完整性方面优于传统的轮询和QoS优先级分配算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代SoC将实时客户端、处理器核心和大量数据引擎放置在相同的AXI4互联上，需要一种公平的QoS管理机制来确保不同客户端间的带宽分配。

Method: 提出的一种基于AXI4的QoS感知动态公平分配算法，通过为每个授予的分组计数来计数，从而在保证每个分组完整性的情况下实现公平的带宽分配。

Result: 在1:16的突发长度不匹配的情况下，该算法能够在满负载下将两个竞争者之间的带宽分配比例恢复到1:1，而传统的轮询和QoS优先级分配算法分别保持为1:16。并且在优先级主客户端的平均读取延迟方面，QoS优先级分配算法为95个时钟周期，QoS感知动态公平分配算法为67个时钟周期，性能提高了29%。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种用于AXI4互联的QoS感知动态公平分配算法的设计和实现，并通过实验证明了其在公平性、读取延迟和数据包完整性方面的卓越性能。

Abstract: Modern systems-on-chip place real-time clients, processor cores and bulk data engines on the same AXI4 interconnect, where they contend for shared slaves. AXI4 fixes the ordering rules and carries a 4-bit quality of service (QoS) field, but leaves the service policy to the implementer, and that policy determines how long each master waits and how much bandwidth it receives once a slave saturates. We propose a QoS-aware deficit round robin (DRR) architecture that charges each grant in beats, and benchmark it against two architectures built behind the same port list: round robin, and QoS priority with anti-starvation aging. Charging in beats recovers bandwidth fairness while leaving every burst whole, so the address phase is issued once and locality for memory access is preserved. All three are driven by the same deterministic traffic generator and taken from RTL to GDSII on the ASAP7 7-nm predictive PDK. Under a 1:16 burst-length mismatch, the round robin and QoS architectures both hold the beat ratio between the two competing masters at 1:16, while our design brings it to 1:1 at full load. Mean read latency for the priority master falls from 95 cycles under round robin to 67 with QoS and 37 with QoS-aware DRR. At 500 MHz this serves the priority master in 74 ns against 190 ns. The measurements give a per-policy price and a traffic-based guide for choosing among the three.

</details>


### [183] [ICLayout-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Custom Circuit Layout Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12982)
*Zhangmai Li,Tianrui Ma,Zheng Weng,Mo Zou,Zidong Du,Yifan Hao,Yongwei Zhao,Wei Li,Jing Li,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ICLayout-Bench 是一个电路设计基准，评估大型语言模型（LLM）生成高质量定制布局的能力。该基准包括来自 13 种工艺设计套件(PDKs)的 165 个任务，技术节点涵盖 3 纳米到 180 纳米。实验评估了 22 种配置方案，发现更高的推理努力并未一致地提高平均评分，且得分相似的配置可能具有显著不同的模型成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究构建 ICLayout-Bench 基准，旨在评估 LLM 在电路设计中的能力，特别是其生成高质量自定义布局的潜力。

Method: ICLayout-Bench 借助于电子设计自动化（EDA）工具，让代理从晶体管级电路网表生成和优化 GDSII 布局。布局通过物理验证、寄生提取和后布局模拟进行评估。

Result: 在 165 项任务中，涵盖了 13 种 PDKs，技术节点从 3 纳米到 180 纳米不等。实验评估了 22 个配置方案，发现平均得分范围根据任务类型不同，分别是 1.43 到 67.01（私有任务），4.22 到 80.26（公开任务）。即使更高的推理努力不一定能提高平均得分，但在某些情况下，得分相似的配置方案可能具有显著不同的模型成本。

Conclusion: 虽然 LLM 在某些情况下能生成高质量的自定义布局，但目前的技术水平还不足以在所有任务中都表现出色。此外，同样的性能提升可能需要付出更高的计算成本。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are being explored for circuit design, but their ability to produce high-quality custom layouts has not been extensively evaluated. ICLayout-Bench is a benchmark in which agents use electronic design automation (EDA) tools to construct and optimize GDSII layouts from transistor-level circuit netlists. These layouts are evaluated using physical verification, parasitic extraction, and post-layout simulation. Layouts that pass the physical and functional checks are scored on electrical performance and area. The benchmark includes 165 tasks across 13 process design kits (PDKs), covering technology nodes from 3 nm to 180 nm. The tasks span analog, mixed-signal, and digital circuits in open-source and commercial EDA flows. The experiments evaluate 22 configurations spanning seven models on 21 public and 19 private tasks. Mean layout scores range from 4.22 to 80.26 on public tasks and from 1.43 to 67.01 on private tasks, on a 100-point scale. Higher reasoning effort does not consistently improve mean scores, and configurations with comparable scores can incur substantially different model costs.

</details>


### [184] [An Open Implementation Flow for the Xilinx Spartan-3E and Spartan-6 that Runs in the Browser, Checked Byte for Byte Against ISE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13008)
*Pedro Malo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了一种从HDL到可编程比特流的开源实现流程，支持Xilinx从Spartan-3E到Spartan-6的不同设备，并使用ISE 14.7的技术。与ISE相比，该流程在电路和路径长度方面表现较优，且适用于67个开源设计中的大多数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在提供一种替代ISE的方法，利用开源工具支持Xilinx设备的FPGA实现，提高设计的适用性与灵活性。

Method: 研究人员使用Yosys和nextpnr编译到WebAssembly，结合Silinx自有的设备描述、比特数据库和时序模型，构建了一个端到端的开源实现流程，最终通过黑盒观察ISE工具的输出并验证了此流程的准确性。

Result: 在67个开源设计中，该流程在电路使用和路径长度方面优于ISE，具体表现为15%到21%的片使用减少和8%到14%的路径缩短。XST无法处理其中15个设计方案。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，此开源实现流程对于某些设计可以提供更好的优化效果，并且是ISE的有效替代方案之一。

Abstract: The author describes an open implementation flow, from HDL to a programmable bitstream, for the Xilinx Spartan-3E (XC3S100E to XC3S1200E) and Spartan-6 (XC6SLX16, XC6SLX45), devices that only the discontinued ISE 14.7 supports. The flow runs unchanged in a web browser or in Node.js: Yosys and nextpnr compiled to WebAssembly, with nextpnr's Himbaechel framework given Spartan-3E and Spartan-6 architectures generated from Silinx's own device descriptions, bit database and timing model, and Silinx's own bitstream writer. The bit database was built by black-box observation of ISE's documented XDL and bitgen tools, using codeword-batched experiments; the router is given only switches whose bits were measured, and the writer refuses a feature it has not measured. ISE is the oracle: for the same placed and routed design, the bitstream is byte-identical to the one bitgen writes and ISE's DRC is clean. Placement and routing were first done by an in-house annealer and PathFinder router, which nextpnr replaced once it matched them. On 67 open-source designs for the XC3S1200E, compared with ISE at the same optimisation goal (45 designs built by both), the flow uses 15% fewer slices and has 14% shorter critical paths at goal Area, 21% fewer slices and 8% shorter paths at Speed, and 20% fewer slices but 12% longer paths at Fast (geometric means). XST cannot read 15 of the designs. The flow is published as the npm package silinx-fpga (Apache-2.0).

</details>


### [185] [Exploiting What NVIDIA Profilers Hide: Fast and Accurate Analytical GPU Performance Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13762)
*Ziwen Wang,Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Changxi Liu,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 研究介绍了一种名为 ForeSight 的排队分析模型，用于预测 GPU 的性能。通过重新利用固定的延迟阻塞并根据每个硬件单元的压力重新计算排队阻塞，ForeSight 显著提高了预测精度和速度。与竞品相比，ForeSight 在多个 NVIDIA GPU 配置下展示了更好的性能预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 GPU 复杂性的增加，探索其硬件配置的设计空间变得越来越重要。现有的基于循环的模拟器非常缓慢，而分析模型则难以准确预测不同配置下的性能。

Method: 研究提出了一种新的排队分析模型 ForeSight，该模型基于固定延迟阻塞的重用和对每个硬件单元压力的回收，以及一种名为 UnitSight 的自定义剖析器来提供硬件单元的计数值。

Result: ForeSight 相比于竞品在多个 NVIDIA GPU 配置下的预测误差更低，特别是在 LLM 推理中的误差为 2.0%。此外，ForeSight 的运行速度比循环级模拟器 Accel-Sim 快 49 倍，并且比基于时间间隔的模型 GCoM 更快。

Conclusion: 研究建议的 ForeSight 模型为探索不同 GPU 配置时提供了更好的性能预测工具，能够显著提高该领域的性能和效率。

Abstract: GPUs have become essential to many fields, including AI and scientific computing. As GPUs grow more complex, the design space of their hardware configurations becomes increasingly large, yet cycle-level simulators remain too slow to explore it. Analytical models make this exploration much faster. However, existing GPU analytical models fall short of accurately predicting performance across hardware configurations, because they re-estimate or scale every stall of a warp on the target configuration. We observe that fixed-latency stalls stay the same across configurations, whereas queueing stalls vary with the pressure on each hardware unit. Based on this observation, we propose ForeSight, a queueing analytical model that reuses the fixed-latency stalls measured on GPU hardware and recomputes the queueing stalls of each hardware unit from its pressure. To provide ForeSight with the counter values of each hardware unit, we build UnitSight, a profiler that recovers these values, which NVIDIA's official profilers hide by aggregating across units. Our evaluation on hardware configurations of nine NVIDIA GPUs shows that ForeSight predicts kernel execution time with a mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 8.3%, compared with 14.1% for the best baseline. ForeSight also predicts the end-to-end execution time of LLM inference on vLLM with a MAPE of 2.0%. It runs 49x faster than the cycle-level simulator Accel-Sim and faster than interval-based models such as GCoM. With the aid of ForeSight, we explore configurations derived from the H20 GPU for LLM inference and find one with 1.78x its performance/cost.

</details>


### [186] [Tri-Stack-PIM: A Heterogeneous Multi-Die 3D-Integrated Processing-in-Memory Architecture for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13644)
*Shuao Jia,Zichao Ling,Chen Bai,Kang Zhao,Jianwang Zhai*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Tri-Stack-PIM通过将存储、缓冲和计算单元分别置于不同层级的芯片上，并提出了一种新的调度器UBAR，能够最大化吞吐量减少能耗延迟积，获得对现有方案的显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D堆叠PIM架构存在局限性，如物理层、控制器和DMA引擎与计算单元竞争芯片面积，限制了MAC单元的密度，且依赖主机计算引入了通信瓶颈。当前研究旨在通过重新设计架构和调度策略来解决这些问题，以提升大型语言模型（LLM）推理性能。

Method: 设计了一个三层异构架构Tri-Stack-PIM，用DRAM芯片存储数据，用成熟工艺节点的中间缓冲芯片提供物理层、DMA引擎和近内存计算单元，用先进的工艺节点底部芯片提供高密度SIMD MAC单元。同时引入了UBAR调度器，通过跨芯片依赖关系的实时正确性和多时间优先级函数最大化发行槽利用率。

Result: 实验结果表明，Tri-Stack-PIM在不同注意力机制和批量大小的场景下，分别获得了4.95x、2.57x和1.68x的加速效果。与图形处理器（GPU），AttAcc和H2-LLM相比，其EDP分别降低了33-66倍。

Conclusion: Tri-Stack-PIM通过构建层次化异构架构和改进调度策略，显著提升了LLM推理性能，并展示了在不同模型和批量大小下的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference during autoregressive decoding is fundamentally memory-bandwidth-bound. Existing three-dimensional (3D) stacked processing-in-memory (PIM) architectures alleviate the off-chip bandwidth wall, yet remain constrained by several limitations: physical layer (PHY), controllers, and direct memory access (DMA) engines compete with arithmetic units for advanced-node base-die area; hybrid-bonding keep-out zones fragment the base-die floorplan and impede physical design; compute tiles built in a dynamic random access memory (DRAM) process are restricted to low logic density, capping achievable throughput; and nonlinear operations offloaded to the host reintroduce a communication bottleneck. We present Tri-Stack-PIM, a heterogeneous three-die architecture that resolves these tensions by dedicating a top DRAM die to storage, a middle buffer die on a mature process node to PHYs, DMA engines, and near-memory compute units, and a bottom base die on an advanced node exclusively to single-instruction-multiple-data (SIMD) multiply-accumulate (MAC) arrays. We further introduce a Unified Buffer-Aware Ready-based (UBAR) hardware out-of-order micro-operation scheduler that enforces cross-die dependency correctness at runtime while maximizing issue-slot utilization through a multi-term priority function, recovering up to 90% of the cycles that an in-order policy would forfeit. A joint architecture-dataflow co-exploration identifies Pareto-optimal configurations that reduce the energy-delay product (EDP) by 33-66x over naive parameter selection. Evaluated across multi-head, grouped-query, and multi-query attention (MHA, GQA, MQA) mechanisms with batch sizes 1-32, Tri-Stack-PIM achieves geometric-mean speedups of 4.95x, 2.57x, and 1.68x across all three attention mechanisms and batch sizes over a graphics processing unit (GPU) baseline, AttAcc, and H2-LLM, respectively.

</details>


### [187] [Uncovering the Memory Hierarchy of Modern NVIDIA GPUs for Accurate Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13860)
*Ziwen Wang,Jiapeng Zhou,Changxi Liu,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: GemAtlas通过精细的SASS级基准测试，系统地解析了现代NVIDIA GPU的内存层次结构，实现对各种缓存层次的微架构细节的发现。这种解析大幅提升了GPU模拟的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工作使用过时的内存层次结构模型，无法准确描绘现代硬件的复杂性，导致对内存系统的优化策略可能误导。GemAtlas旨在填补这一空白，通过系统逆向工程现代NVIDIA GPU的内存层次结构来提高内存系统建模的准确性和可靠性。

Method: GemAtlas利用细粒度的SASS级基准测试，揭示不同缓存层次的未记录微架构细节，包括缓存组的字组关联、切片路由和集索引哈希函数、替换策略、一致性行为和互连拓扑。

Result: GemAtlas将发现的微架构细节集成到最先进的GPU模拟框架中。结果显示，与现有模型相比，GemAtlas在四个GPU上的平均MAPE从31.69%降低到22.24%。特别是在针对真实的LLM操作符时，MAPE从34.04%降低到23.98%。

Conclusion: 本研究通过详细的逆向工程为现代GPU内存系统的建模提供了准确的基础，为优化策略提供了可靠依据。

Abstract: Emerging artificial intelligence workloads, particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), place unprecedented demands on GPU memory systems. Consequently, architectural innovations increasingly target the memory system to alleviate these bottlenecks. However, existing works still rely on outdated memory hierarchy models that fail to capture the complexities of modern hardware. This fidelity gap leads to inaccurate characterization and could misguide optimization strategies. To bridge this gap, we present GemAtlas, the first systematic reverse engineering of the memory hierarchy in modern NVIDIA GPUs. By developing fine-grained SASS-level benchmarking, we expose critical, previously undocumented micro-architectural details across different cache levels, including cache set associativity, slice-routing and set-indexing hash functions, replacement policies, coherence behavior, and interconnect topologies. We integrate these discovered micro-architecture details into the state-of-the-art GPU simulation framework. The final results demonstrate that GemAtlas reduces the mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) from 31.69% to 22.24% on average across four GPUs. Specifically, GemAtlas reduces the MAPE from 34.04% to 23.98% for real-world LLM operators.

</details>


### [188] [SFU-Sim: Bit-Exact Reverse Engineering of NVIDIA MUFU and Validation of PTX Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13879)
*Haozhi Xie,Tao Su*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SFU-Sim，通过黑盒逆向工程构建NVIDIA MUFU指令的比特精确模型，完全覆盖输入域中的每个输入编码，实现比特级精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档仅提供函数语义和误差边界，不足以确定输出位模式，本文旨在构建比特精确模型以预测GPU上的特殊函数指令行为。

Method: 利用黑盒逆向工程方法，通过模拟单个MUFU指令并结合其在反汇编中的周边操作构建完整PTX路径模型。该方法覆盖了测试Blackwell GPU上的12个常见MUFU形式和两个BF16形式。

Result: SFU-Sim能够在NVIDIA Ampere、Ada Lovelace和Blackwell三个架构的GPU上，对包含的所有输入编码的每个PTX路径进行详尽比较，并且输出与硬件完全一致，无任何差异。

Conclusion: SFU-Sim是一个比特精确的特殊函数组件，适用于指令模拟、硬件设计的功能验证以及数值行为目标的一致性参考。其源代码可在GitHub上公开获得。

Abstract: Reproducing the numerical behavior of a GPU requires predicting the exact output of special function instructions. Vendor documents give only the function semantics and error bounds. These do not fix the output bit pattern. We present SFU-Sim, which builds bit-exact models of NVIDIA multi-function unit (MUFU) instructions by black-box reverse engineering on real GPUs. We first model a single bare MUFU instruction. We then add the surrounding operations found in the disassembly to model full PTX paths. The model covers 12 common MUFU forms and two BF16 forms on the tested Blackwell GPU. Using SIN/COS as a complete case study, we show how we analyze the PTX-to-SASS compilation path and recover the segment structure, the equivalent coefficient tables, and the finite-width arithmetic. On NVIDIA GPUs of three architectures (Ampere, Ada Lovelace, and Blackwell), we exhaustively compare every tested PTX path over its specified input domain. FP16 and FP32 paths cover all $2^{16}$ and $2^{32}$ input encodings, respectively. The model output matches the hardware bit for bit, with zero mismatches. SFU-Sim computes each output directly from its input. It needs neither a lookup table of all inputs nor access to a physical GPU. It can serve as a bit-exact special function component for instruction simulators. It can also serve as an arithmetic reference for hardware design and functional verification that target the same numerical behavior. SFU-Sim and its validation programs are open source at https://github.com/hzX40000000/SFU-Sim

</details>


### [189] [TAPCO-G: LLM-Guided Design-Adaptive Timing and Power Co-Optimization with Graph-Based Physical Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13931)
*Wuqian Tang,Zixiao Wang,Che-Chien Lin,Fong-Hua Fu,Laura Angélica Shion-Korsak,Xinyun Zhang,Bei Yu,Chun-Yao Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: TAPCO-G通过结合基于大型语言模型的优化器自适应和基于图的物理先验，成功进行后置布线时序和功耗联合优化，显著提高了多项关键性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的优化器遵循固定规则进行修复调度，难以应对设计特定瓶颈，而TAPCO-G通过使用物理设计反馈和图基物理先验，增强了优化能力。

Method: 外环调整工具设置并依据反馈修订可执行修复策略，内环通过细化延迟估计和功率筛选来优先考虑物理实现的候选方案。

Result: TAPCO-G在所有八个ASAP7基准测试中表现出了更高的质量评分，平均提高了5.516分，并且比TAPCO高出21.648分。

Conclusion: 该联合优化框架的有效性得到了证实，为提升时序和功耗性能提供了新的可能路径。

Abstract: Post-placement timing and power co-optimization is critical to meeting setup timing targets and reducing power within placement constraints. A cell replacement can improve timing along one path while increasing power or reducing slack on others. Conventional optimizers follow fixed rules for scheduling and accepting repairs, limiting their ability to address design-specific bottlenecks. A large language model (LLM) can propose changes to these rules, but on its own it cannot reliably estimate how a cell replacement affects endpoint slack and power. TAPCO-G combines LLM-guided optimizer adaptation with graph-based physical priors to evaluate candidate cell replacements. The outer loop tunes tool settings using physical-design feedback and revises executable repair policies when tuning stalls. The inner loop anchors delay estimates to static timing analysis snapshots, accounts for upstream driver-load changes, and propagates delay shifts to timing endpoints. It combines these estimates with coarse-to-fine power screening to prioritize candidates for physical trials. TAPCO-G produced placement-legal, logic-equivalent implementations and outscored CERBERUS on all eight ASAP7 benchmarks from the MLCAD 2026 Contest, with a mean quality score gain of 5.516 points, and scored 21.648 points higher than TAPCO on average. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed co-optimization framework.

</details>


### [190] [SkipLane: Write-Once, Conflict-Free Accumulation for Zero-Skipping CNN Engines on FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14495)
*Aditya Saboo,Pratibha Verma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SkipLane 方法通过在每个核行单独处理并在读出时仅对输入通道进行求和，实现了写一次的整个累积路径，避免了冲突并通过多乘法器计算第二个滤波器来节省能耗。该方法在 RTL 模拟中最高支持 70% 稀疏性，比 SCNN 风格的散列积存器加速器快 1.10-1.26 倍，并在 PYNQ-Z2 上以 140 MHz 运行 LeNet-5 而无需额外硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 CNN 加速器设计要么通过读改写（RMW）合并内核行，要么通过附加硬件减少银行冲突，但没有完全消除银行冲突的问题，特别是在处理高度稀疏输入时会导致性能下降。

Method: SkipLane 方法设计了一个独特的累积路径，确保每个核行在单独的通道上处理，存储部分和在三个寄存器中，并每周期最多提交三个完成的列到不会冲突的内存银行中。当计算乘法时，那些因零权值而空闲的乘法器计算第二个滤波器，以节省资源。

Result: SkipLane 在 RTL 模拟中最高可支持 70% 的稀疏性，比 SCNN 风格的散列积存器加速器在 50-80% 的稀疏性范围内快 1.10-1.26 倍。同时，在 PYNQ-Z2 上实现了 140 MHz 的运行速度，并在 21.1k LUT 的硬件资源限制下对 LeNet-5 进行了位准确的运行，特别是在 conv1 中表现出 1.94 倍更少的周期需要，并在 conv2 中达到与密集引擎相近的速度。

Conclusion: SkipLane 是一种有效的稀疏卷积加速器设计，通过优化多核处理、减少资源冲突并利用零权值来额外计算第二个滤波器，实现了高速、高效率的卷积操作，尤其在处理稀疏输入时表现优异。

Abstract: Skipping zero activations saves multiplications in CNN accelerators, but it complicates accumulation: the products in a compressed input window belong to different output columns and must be merged. In our earlier engine, this merging was done by read-modify-write (RMW) of a row buffer, which made sparse inputs slower than dense ones even at 87% sparsity. Existing designs either merge kernel rows through RMW or reduce bank conflicts with extra hardware, without eliminating them. SkipLane makes the entire accumulation path write-once. Each kernel row is processed in its own lane, which holds partial sums in three registers and retires up to three finished columns per cycle into memory banks that cannot conflict. Lanes and input channels are summed only at readout, so no partial sum is ever read back. Multipliers left idle by zero weights compute a second filter in the same pass. In RTL simulation, the engine stays within 0.5% of its frontend bound up to 70% sparsity and is 1.10-1.26$\times$ faster than an SCNN-style scatter accumulator with 18 banks and ideal RMW at 50-80% sparsity. Six engines meet 140 MHz on a PYNQ-Z2 using 21.1k LUTs and run LeNet-5 bit-exact on the board. For conv1, the engine needs 1.94$\times$ fewer cycles than a dense engine (1.26$\times$ faster end to end); for conv2, whose rows are only 13 pixels wide, it reaches 0.87$\times$ the dense speed.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [WINIC: An Automatic Cycle-Accurate Multi-Platform Microbenchmarking Tool for Instruction Performance Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13978)
*Tobias Rühl,Jan Laukemann,Georg Hager,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: WINIC 是一种自动化跨平台微基准测试工具，能够自动为大多数支持的 x86、AArch64 和 RISC-V 微架构收集精准的性能数据，包括吞吐量和基于操作数的延迟指标，无需特权权限，从而提高内核性能预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有的性能模型工具如 OSACA 难以支持多种架构，开发一种能够跨多个微架构收集准确性能数据的工具成为必要，以支持开发者进行更有效的微架构级优化。

Method: WINIC 通过自动化的微基准测试技术，采用寄存器保存和恢复的方法，确保测试过程不影响系统运行。它通过收集汇编指令的精确时钟周期数据来分析性能，并提供基于操作数的延迟指标。

Result: WINIC 工具成功为四种目标微架构自动收集了大量精确结果，匹配性能数据的准确率从 71% 到 99% 不等，并且通过使用基于操作数的性能指标改善了内核性能预测，尤其是在 Neoverse V2 微架构上。

Conclusion: 此研究为不同微架构性能分析和优化提供了坚实的基础，并推动了更广泛的跨平台性能模型的发展。

Abstract: Performance models allow developers to gain important insights into the interaction of their code with the hardware that executes it, and enable them to apply target-specific optimizations. To create in-core performance models for out-of-order processors, architecture-specific performance metrics like latency, throughput, and port binding of individual instructions are indispensable. For x86 microarchitectures, there already exist tools to automatically obtain those values; however, the extension of performance modeling tools like the Open Source Architecture Code Analyzer (OSACA) to support further architectures like RISC-V emphasizes the need for a multi-platform microbenchmarking tool. We present WINIC (What I Need Is Cycles), an automatic cross-platform microbenchmarking tool. Unlike previous tools, it can obtain throughput and operand-based latency metrics for most supported instructions of any 64-bit x86, AArch64, and RISC-V microarchitecture without the need for root permissions or extended system control levels. We cover the methodology of microbenchmarking and the approach of WINIC to collect cycle-accurate performance data for assembly instructions. We show that WINIC can be used to automatically obtain a vast number of accurate results and demonstrate for four target microarchitectures that our results match between 71% and 99% of the performance data of instructions obtained by similar tools and documentation. Furthermore, we investigate how operand-based metrics measured by WINIC can be used to improve the in-core performance predictions on selected micro-kernels, exemplarily for the Neoverse V2 microarchitecture.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Auditable Human-Agent Teams for Autonomous X-ray Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12933)
*Gunik Luthra,Ronald J. Pandolfi,Benjamin T. Diroll,Ricky Ronquillo,Ruipeng Li,Christopher B. Murray,Marcus M. Noack,Kevin G. Yager,Esther H. R. Tsai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过使用编码代理基础设施并基于验证的科学方法，研究展示了在纳米粒子超晶格上的自主同步辐射X射线实验，通过人类与代理的完整对话记录审计研究结果，揭示了认知劳动的分配，并提供了设计原则建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低闭环自动实验的实施障碍，探索长期在物理设备上运行人工系统的可能性，提高自主实验的可访问性，特别是在科学研究中的应用。

Method: 基于验证的科学方法，使用编码代理基础设施进行实验，包括合成人类定义的目标下的纳米粒子超晶格的同步辐射X射线实验，记录人与代理以及代理之间的完整对话并进行审计。

Result: 展示了自主进行的同步辐射X射线实验的效果，对话交流中61-67%的消息涉及解释或判断，其中方法和科学贡献的份额从实验一的46%增加到实验三的56-61%，并且审计显示约59-68%的纠正错误的行为是多人参与的，约一半的决定包含了其他人的输入。

Conclusion: 结果表明，代理在科学实验中的表现提供了多代理科学系统的设计原则和未来代理工具、模型训练以及更广泛自主实验的可访问性的潜在指导。

Abstract: Closed-loop autonomous experimentation can accelerate scientific discovery, but implementation effort limits accessibility. Agentic systems may lower this barrier, although sustained operation on physical instruments remains largely unexplored. Here, using coding-agent infrastructure grounded in validated scientific methods, we demonstrate autonomous synchrotron X-ray experiments on nanoparticle superlattices under evolving, human-defined objectives. Complete human-agent and agent-agent transcripts enable an audit that reconstructs decision provenance and characterizes the division of cognitive labor. Across three experiments, 61-67% of messages involved interpretation or judgment, while the share of methodological and scientific contributions increased from 46% in the setup-focused first experiment to 56-61% in later experiments. Provenance showed that 59-68% of error corrections involved multiple participants and that about half of all decisions incorporated the input of another participant. These results may guide design principles for multi-agent scientific systems and inform future agent harnesses, model training, and accessible autonomous experimentation.

</details>


### [193] [EarthRecord: From Pixels to Land Change with Multimodal Agents over Four Decades of Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12934)
*Haoran Zhang,Huizhen Sun,Hanbin Wen,Jie Leng,Jiaxin Zhu,Zhaohua Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EarthRecord 提供了一个基于未处理的多传感器记录的基准，用于评估多模态代理在地球观测科学中的土地变化分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试没有真实地反映从卫星图像到可靠的土地变化分析的过程，因此无法准确评估多模态代理的能力。

Method: EarthRecord 基于 56 个站点四十年的 Landsat、MODIS、VIIRS 和 Sentinel-2 数据集合，引入了闭卷测试和仪器对测试来评估代理的性能。

Result: 12 种多模态代理中，领先的代理可以完成大部分任务，但在需要从图像映射答案的情况下表现不佳。改变一个卫星的数据会影响一半正确的答案，显示了代理依赖于先前知识而非实际变化。

Conclusion: EarthRecord 为评估多模态代理从像素到土地变化的过程提供了一个有原则的平台，并为未来的研究制定了方向。

Abstract: While multimodal agents now carry out complex data analysis with tools, their ability to turn decades of satellite imagery into reliable land-change analysis, crucial for Earth observation science, remains unmeasured. Existing benchmarks fall short: they pose questions on curated images or give agents preprocessed inputs, leaving out the raw multi-sensor archives where such analysis begins. We introduce EarthRecord, a benchmark built on the raw record of 56 sites: four decades of Landsat, MODIS, VIIRS, and Sentinel-2 data, with cloud masking and cross-sensor harmonization left to the agent. It poses 238 land-change questions across ten analysis tasks, scored against withheld references from global products, agreement among methods, and visual interpretation. Two controls test what answers rest on: the closed book removes the record, and instrument pairs alter one satellite's data while nothing changes on the ground. Evaluations of twelve multimodal agents reveal that leading agents complete most analyses end to end but falter when an answer requires mapping from imagery. Further analysis shows that the record moves answers from prior knowledge to the pixels but not yet to the real change on the ground: when one satellite's data are altered, half of the right answers follow the alteration. EarthRecord thus provides a principled platform for measuring and advancing agents from pixels to land change.

</details>


### [194] [Train on prefill, monitor at decode: Using sparse autoencoders for topic monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12935)
*Rishikesh Mallagundla,Pranav Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者提出了一种名为TopicScope的框架，用于在生成过程中为每个标记分配主题标签。该框架能够在多个话题的对话中进行泛化，并且在模型解码时表现稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的激活基监控器仅限于检测两到三个主题，并且主要关注有害内容。而该研究旨在开发一种能够在生成过程中动态分配主题的监控框架，从而提高监控模型的灵活性和稳定性。

Method: 作者使用SAE特征训练三种检测器架构，并在合成的多话题对话数据集上进行训练。他们研究了在预填充隐藏状态中提取的特征，以研究这些特征是否有助于通用性。

Result: 模型在Gemma-3 1B和4B上预填充隐藏状态提取的特征训练的监控器能够泛化到解码过程，并且即使有偏离主题的内容插入、话题移除或部分重排，分类器仍然具有较高的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可以以较低的成本训练紧凑的主题监控器并在预填充时部署，同时还能在生成过程中提供有效的监控。此外，实验揭示了SAE在安全分类之外的应用场景。

Abstract: Topic monitoring is the task of labelling the topic of each token in a model's response during generation. Activation-based monitors that run at generation time have so far been trained to detect harmful content and are limited to two or three classes. We introduce TopicScope, a framework for training causal topic classifiers on SAE features that assign a topic to every token, so that the label can change partway through a response. We train three detector architectures on synthetic multi-topic conversations covering seven topics. Across Gemma-3 1B and 4B, monitors trained on features extracted from prefill hidden states were able to generalise to decoding. We also found that the classifiers are robust to perturbations, losing at most 5 macro-F1 points when off-topic asides are inserted, topics are removed or sections are reordered. Ablations conducted to understand the contribution of the SAE revealed that classifiers reaching baseline performance without it were several times larger. Our findings show that compact topic monitors can be trained inexpensively in prefill and deployed during generation. More broadly, our work shows where SAEs are useful beyond safety classification. Our code, dataset and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/Mercity-AI/SAE-Guardrails and https://huggingface.co/collections/Mercity/online-guardrails-using-sparse-autoencoders-6ac602940468c62fefb2b9e8

</details>


### [195] [LandslideFM: A Foundation Model Towards Unified Landslide Perception and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12936)
*Shanwen Wang,Sirui Wang,Xin Sun,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LandslideFM 是一种统一的基础模型，它结合了视觉感知任务和滑坡场景的理解，使用有限的标记数据和大量的未标记数据进行半监督学习，同时利用预测的滑坡区域对视觉语言模型进行参数高效的微调，展示了在滑坡分析领域的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型主要针对孤立的视觉任务，需要大量的标注数据，并缺乏深入的分析和场景理解能力。LandslideFM 旨在解决这些问题，通过连接视觉感知任务与滑坡的上下文解释，提供了一种新的解决方案。

Method: LandslideFM 使用 Source-Agreement Graduated Trust 半监督学习框架，结合有限的标签数据和大量的未标签数据进行训练。预测的滑坡区域作为空间指导，用于参数高效的视觉语言模型微调。此外，还提出了 LandslideVL 数据集作为滑坡智能分析的第一个基准。

Result: LandslideFM 在多个公开数据集和 LandslideVL 上的综合实验中表现出优越的定量和定性性能。特别是在专门针对滑坡的推理和场景分析方面，LandslideFM 比通用的视觉语言模型表现更佳。

Conclusion: LandslideFM 通过结合视觉感知和滑坡理解，提供了半监督学习框架和参数高效的视觉语言模型微调方法，展示出在滑坡领域的重要应用潜力，并开放了可参考的代码供研究使用。

Abstract: Intelligent landslide detection is essential for disaster prevention. However, existing models primarily focus on isolated visual tasks, require extensive labeled data, and lack deep analytical and scene-understanding capabilities. To address these limitations, we propose LandslideFM, the first unified foundation model that connects visual perception tasks with contextual interpretation of landslides. Specifically, LandslideFM adapts to multiple visual tasks via a Source-Agreement Graduated Trust semi-supervised learning framework that leverages limited labeled data alongside abundant unlabeled data. Furthermore, predicted landslide regions provide explicit spatial guidance to a Vision-Language Model adapted through parameter-efficient fine-tuning, enabling comprehensive landslide reasoning. To support training and evaluation in this domain, we also present LandslideVL, the first benchmark for intelligent landslide analysis, combining visual imagery and newly introduced textual prompts. Comprehensive experiments across public datasets and LandslideVL demonstrate that LandslideFM achieves superior quantitative and qualitative performance. Most importantly, LandslideFM surpasses general-purpose VLMs in specialized landslide reasoning and scene analysis. Our code is released at https://github.com/wangshanwen001/LandslideFM.

</details>


### [196] [Tracing Psychometric Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12937)
*Chih-Hao Hsu,Feng-Chun B. Chou,Pin-Hao A. Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在内部是否代表了心理学构建，或仅在外显输出上模拟了心理学结构？研究表明，较大的模型可以在行为层面再现和放大跨维度的相关性，并在特定情况下内部结构与人类结构几何学保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型语言模型在其内部是否具备类似于人类心理结构的能力，或者仅仅在外显输出上模仿这种结构。

Method: 使用跨人格范式，评估多个模型在不同规模和版本下的表现，通过对比方向分析和激活探针实验测量几何结构一致性与输出行为之间的关系。

Result: 大型模型在行为层面再现了人类在多个心理量表上的相关性，甚至在矫正了测量衰减之后依然如此。在特定条件下，较大的指令模型的几何结构与人类矩阵几何学保持了一致。探针的激活斜率与人类表现行为的斜率有了显著减少，修正后的探针表现与行为表现间的关系依然显著。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，大型语言模型在内部确实存在可解码性和几何对齐性，但这并不意味着测量的方向直接促进了跨人格的回答。

Abstract: Do large language models represent psychological constructs internally, or do they only generate outputs that resemble psychometric structure? We address this question using a cross-persona paradigm across 14 model variants from two families (Llama 3 and Qwen 2.5, 0.5B-14B, base and instruct). Given responses on one psychological scale from real individuals (N = 272 Taiwanese university students), models predict responses on six other scales. At the behavioral level, larger variants reproduce and amplify human cross-scale correlations; amplification persists after correcting the seven-scale human correlations for measurement attenuation. We then examine whether this structure is reflected internally. Contrastive direction analysis finds geometry aligned with the human matrix in larger instruct variants, but little alignment in base variants under this assay. Across models, geometry strength is associated with behavioral amplification (r = 0.68, p = 0.008; partial r = 0.69 controlling for log size). To relate internal structure to output behavior, a matched activation-probing paradigm shows that probe slopes vary less than behavioral slopes (1.38-1.77 vs 0.52-1.53). A synthetic control shows that ridge probing can inflate these slopes; after subtracting a baseline based on a reliability proxy from another analysis, adjusted slopes remain associated with behavior (r = 0.88). We term the measured probe-behavior gap readout attenuation. These results establish decodability and geometric alignment under the tested assays, without showing that the measured directions mediate the cross-persona answers.

</details>


### [197] [Graph Visualizations in Prompts for Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12938)
*Luiz do Valle Miranda,Andreas L. Opdahl,Grzegorz J. Nalepa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究多模态输入对知识图谱问答（KGQA）的影响，发现图像和文本结合的提示在低复杂度问题上效果显著，视觉展示的设计对总体性能有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在大规模视觉-语言模型中，多模态输入（图像和文本结合）如何改善知识图谱问答的表现，特别是在推理复杂度、模型大小和图像布局的变化下。

Method: 在固定的知识图谱问答管道中，使用1,500个WebQSP和CWQ问题，对六种不同的视觉语言模型进行控制测试，通过比较仅文本提示与文本加图形可视化的效果，分析基于推理复杂性、模型规模和图像布局的变化。

Result: 多模态策略（文本加图像）相较于纯文本策略，在每个问题上平均提高了1.85 F1分，特别是在低复杂度问题上的精度提升了4.65 F1。该结果表明，视觉提示主要帮助简单问题的预测精度提高，但图像布局的设计对最终效果影响显著。分解大型子图进行单独的路径显示能带来最大的改进。

Conclusion: 视觉提示主要通过提高简单问题的预测精度来改善KGQA。建议使用适应性可视化提示策略，选择性地将图表附加上到小型、视觉上稀疏的子图中，这些子图具有良好的布局，从而优化准确性和推理效率。未来的研究应基于有原则的设计理论指导，以更好地实现多模态视觉语言模型的提示策略。

Abstract: We study when and how multimodal input improves knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) in large vision--language models. Using a controlled evaluation over 1,500 WebQSP and CWQ questions and six VLMs within a fixed KGQA pipeline, we compare text-only prompting against text-plus-graph visualization at the per-question level and analyze performance changes as a function of reasoning complexity, model size, and image layout. Adding a graph image yields an average gain of 1.85 F1 points, driven by strong precision improvements on low-complexity questions (+4.65 F1). Mixed-effects and regression analyses show that these multimodal gains do not depend strongly on model scale, but are highly sensitive to graph layout: image-only settings sharply degrade performance, hierarchical Graphviz layouts outperform less organized renderings, and decomposing large subgraphs into separated paths yields the largest improvements. Taken together, the results indicate that visual prompting primarily aids KGQA by enhancing prediction precision in low-complexity contexts, provided that visual design respects cognitive and perceptual constraints. To optimize both accuracy and inference efficiency, we argue for adaptive visual prompting policies that selectively append diagrams to small, visually sparse subgraphs with well-structured layouts, avoiding unnecessary visual clutter and token overhead on complex queries. We conclude by relating these findings to a visual notation design theory, outlining how principled diagram encoding can guide future multimodal VLM prompting.

</details>


### [198] [Reinforcement Learning based control of Cancer Chemotherapy treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12940)
*Jayakody Kankanamalage Chamani Shiranthika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用强化学习技术优化癌症化疗方案的方法，通过传统的Q-learning进行了化疗的模拟，并提出了一个基于保守Q-learning和模型的脱机政策优化算法的数据驱动解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着强化学习在现实场景中的应用逐渐扩大，研究人员开始关注将其应用在个性化医疗和动态治疗方案上。本文旨在探索如何利用强化学习技术优化癌症化疗方案。

Method: 本文首先使用传统的Q-learning对癌症化疗进行了模拟，然后提出了一种基于保守Q-learning和模型的架构的脱机政策优化算法。

Result: 实验结果显示，提出的Chemo RL模型能够很好地处理癌症化疗治疗的时间优化问题，并为年轻肿瘤学家提供决策支持。

Conclusion: 本文为癌症化疗治疗决策提供了一种新的数据驱动的方法，具有保守性和可移植性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning has achieved tremendous success in recent years. Extending its scope from the traditional complex games such as Atari, Go, Pokeman, and Chess, reinforcement learning has caught researchers' attention on modelling real-life scenarios. Personalized medicine and the dynamic treatment regimen are two such preliminary research fields; researchers are focusing their mindsets currently. Estimating the optimal dynamic treatment regimens from the observational real clinical data is being popularized among the researchers, widening its scope towards more advanced versions of reinforcement learning algorithms.
  This research is about a research study that focused on reinforcement learning techniques that have been approached in the context of cancer chemotherapy treatments. Primarily this thesis addresses the result of the study by means of the state-of-the-art on reinforcement learning techniques used in generating the optimal policy for sequential treatments, including the existing online reinforcement learning approaches. Thereby a simulation for cancer chemotherapy via traditional Q-learning would be presented. Secondly, the study will extend towards using offline reinforcement learning in the optimal policy generation. Thus, the proposed solution follows a data-driven approach, which works on the real human colon cancer clinical data.
  The novelty of the proposed solution resides in the implementation of an offline reinforcement learning approach by means of a model-based architecture using the conservative Q-learning and the model-based offline policy optimization algorithm. As per the experimental results and the evaluations, it was revealed that the proposed Chemo RL model caters well the problem of cancer chemotherapy treatment scheduling and serves as a supportive model for the junior oncologists in deciding their treatment decisions.

</details>


### [199] [AI Governance Should Prioritize Control and Knowledge Boundaries Over Limiting Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12941)
*Hendrik Luuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个名为IKC + KC + C的能力电路模型，将其分解为三种途径：智能驱动发现、程序性手艺和直接控制。通过分析仿真实验，研究展示了智能驱动速度上限由竞争智能比例决定，即使在难以完成的任务上也是如此。研究还指出，增加智能加速可能需要一个噪音较大的假设空间，但实际进度仍然受限于关键能力测试所需时间。此外，作者认为限制控制权和强化知识边界是当前安全管理中的主要杠杆，而非单纯限制智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主行动的AI系统快速增加，对其治理成为迫切需要。本文研究旨在为理解和治理这类系统提供一种新的分析框架。

Method: 本文使用了NK适应度景观的随机模拟，分析了智能驱动快速崛起的条件，并通过模拟提出了能力电路模型。

Result: 研究结论显示了智能驱动速度上存在上限，与任务难度无关；同时揭示了智能驱动加速的假定空间需要具备噪音特性但实际进度仍受限于关键能力测试的时间；还发现对于权力系统而言，控制权和知识边界的约束比单纯的智能限制更为关键。

Conclusion: 作者建议应更加关注如何限制控制而非控制智能的提升，以增强AI系统的安全性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of autonomously acting AI agents makes the governance of agentic systems urgent. We propose the competence circuit, summarized as IKC + KC + C, that decomposes an agent's real-world impact into three pathways: intelligence-mediated discovery, procedural know-how, and direct control. Using the canonical fast-takeoff debate as a stress test, we formalize the conditions for intelligence-mediated fast takeoff as a conjunction jointly sufficient for fast catastrophic supremacy, showing that most rely on rapid knowledge acquisition or control attainment rather than intelligence alone. We operationalize epistemic intelligence as hypothesis-selection efficiency and validate this framework via stochastic simulation on NK fitness landscapes across six scenarios spanning the conjunctive chain. An analytic bound supported by the simulations shows that intelligence-driven speedup is capped by the ratio of competing intelligence levels regardless of task difficulty. The simulations also expose a fast-takeoff dilemma: a large intelligence-driven speedup multiplier materializes only when the hypothesis space is noisy, but the absolute timescale remains constrained by the time required for capability-critical tests, so a large multiplier need not imply rapid progress. Simulations also indicate that institutional chokepoints disproportionately slow down superintelligent agents when mandatory authorization delays are large relative to the negligible time these agents spend on unproductive search. We argue that the dominant safety levers are restricting control and enforcing knowledge boundaries rather than limiting intelligence alone, and this applies to agentic systems at any capability level. Purely digital domains weaken these bottlenecks, strengthening the case for architectures that intentionally "put physics in the loop."

</details>


### [200] [Agentic AI Meets Formal Verification in the Era of Agentic Hardware Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13705)
*Wenji Fang,Yuchao Wu,Jing Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI时代硬件形式方法的方法和技术，探讨了代理AI如何在规定、证明构建和推理引擎三个方面推动形式方法的发展，同时讨论了形式方法如何支持可靠的人工智能驱动硬件开发。最后，提出了未来研究的机会，旨在实现一种证明原生的硬件开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI技术的进步，AI生成的硬件设计的正确性变得越来越重要。同时，形式方法的发展降低了使用这些方法的门槛，为硬件验证的自动化和改进提供了新的机会。

Method: 文章首先探讨了代理AI如何在形式方法的三个层面推动其发展：规范、证明构建和推理引擎。接着讨论了形式方法如何支持可靠的人工智能驱动硬件开发，涵盖从RTL生成、错误调试到保持正确性的优化。最后，文章总结了未来研究的机会。

Result: 文章回顾了代表性的研究成果和作者的最新贡献，强调了实验结果中的关键技术和见解。

Conclusion: 文章最后提出了未来研究的机会，旨在实现一种证明原生的硬件开发，其中形式推理将成为自主硬件设计中必不可少的部分。

Abstract: As AI agents become increasingly capable of generating and optimizing hardware circuits, ensuring the correctness of AI-generated designs becomes more important. Meanwhile, advances in AI lower the barriers to developing and applying formal methods, creating new opportunities to automate and improve hardware verification. This invited paper presents an overview of the methodologies and applications of hardware formal methods in the AI era. First, we examine how agentic AI advances hardware formal methods across three levels: specification, proof construction, and reasoning engines. Second, we discuss how formal methods support reliable AI-driven hardware development through RTL generation, debugging, and correctness-preserving optimization. We review representative research alongside our recent contributions, highlighting key technical advances and insights from experimental results. Finally, we discuss future research opportunities toward proof-native hardware development, where formal reasoning could become an integral part of autonomous hardware design.

</details>


### [201] [Tabular Foundation Models vs Tuned Gradient Boosting for Telecom Churn: A Profit- and Calibration-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12942)
*Ismail Hossain Polas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TabPFN-3.5 在电信退订数据集上显著优于调优后的梯度提升树，并且其概率能够驱动阈值策略，但尚未达到生产应用的许可要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估TabPFN系列模型与调优后的GBDT在电信退订数据集上的表现差异，特别是其在收益方面的表现。

Method: 使用TabPFN-2.5, TabPFN-3.5, TabPFN-3.5-Fast, TabICL v2和调优后的GBDT对五个公共的电信退订数据集进行评估，进行10次种子划分。

Result: 在Cell2Cell和伊朗数据集上，TabPFN-3.5比最佳调优GBDT获得了显著更高的预期最大利润；在Orange数据集上虽然表现较好但未能通过校正后的显著性测试。TabICL v2在伊朗数据集上优于最佳调优GBDT但不能全面领先。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3.5 和 TabICL v2 在特定数据集上展现出优于GBDTs的表现，但必须克服许可限制才能实际应用于生产环境。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) predict from a labelled context in a single forward pass. A recent benchmark reported that they decisively outperform tree ensembles for customer churn, but it compared them with untuned gradient-boosted trees (GBDTs) and scored only ranking metrics, while a retention campaign is judged in money. We re-evaluate TabPFN-2.5, TabPFN-3.5, TabPFN-3.5-Fast and TabICL v2 against Optuna-tuned GBDTs on five public telecom churn datasets over 10 seeded splits. We separate three questions: whether a model ranks churners well, whether that ranking earns more expected maximum profit (EMP), and whether its probabilities can drive a threshold policy. With a split-aware test and Holm correction over 20 pre-declared comparisons, TabPFN-3.5 earns significantly more EMP than the best tuned GBDT on Cell2Cell (1.9%) and Iranian (7.6%), and these two gains also hold against each of the three tuned GBDTs separately. On Orange it is ahead in all 10 splits (14.3%), but the gain is not significant after correction (Holm p = 0.155). The splits resample the same datasets, so these results do not stand for independent operators. TabICL v2, the earlier benchmark's leader, beats the best tuned GBDT only on Iranian, not all three of them, and underpredicts Orange churn by 2.1 points on a 7.3% base rate. On the datasets and splits where we tested them, a substantially larger tuning budget and a duplicate-aware split leave the main conclusions unchanged. Retuning the baseline cuts the earlier headline gap from 6.1 to 4.0 PR-AUC points without closing it. The licence of the TabPFN-3.5 weights we used (License v1.0), not scoring cost, is what stands between its gains and production use.

</details>


### [202] [Toward Autonomous RTL and Synthesis Optimization: Benchmarking and Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13713)
*Wenji Fang,Yao Lu,Jinghua Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用大语言模型和自主代理的最新进展来优化硬件设计的方向，包括现实基准测试、基于工具的RTL优化和设计特定的综合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）和自主代理的进步，为硬件设计提供新的优化机会。本文旨在通过三种连接方向来优化自主寄存器传输级（RTL）和综合：基准测试、基于工具的RTL优化和设计特定的合成优化。

Method: 文章首先讨论了基于参考和开放式RTL优化基准，以评估在现实EDA工作流程下的优化机会。接着介绍了名为Dr. RTL的多代理框架，该框架自动进行关键路径分析、RTL重写和实现层评估，并通过可重用优化技能不断改进。最后探索了代理驱动的合成脚本调整及其在技术和独立优化和映射之间的可执行综合算法发现。

Result: 在20个现实世界RTL设计上评估了Dr. RTL，取得了平均关键定时网络改进21%，17%的提前定时网络改进，以及6%的面积减少的效果，与商业综合相比。

Conclusion: 文章最终展望了结合架构、RTL和EDA算法设计空间的AI本位设计自动化，旨在通过验证约束和实施反馈进行联合探索，并提出此方向的研究价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) and autonomous agents are creating new opportunities for hardware design optimization. This paper examines three connected directions toward autonomous RTL and synthesis optimization: (1) Realistic benchmarking. We discuss reference-based and open-ended RTL optimization benchmarks that evaluate optimization opportunities under realistic EDA workflows. (2) Tool-grounded RTL optimization. We present Dr. RTL, a multi-agent framework that automates critical-path analysis, RTL rewriting, and implementation-level evaluation, while continuously improving through reusable optimization skills. Evaluated on 20 real-world RTL designs, Dr.~RTL achieves average WNS/TNS improvements of 21\%/17\% with a 6\% area reduction over commercial synthesis. (3) Design-specific synthesis optimization. We explore agent-driven synthesis script tuning and the discovery of executable synthesis algorithms across intermediate representations, technology-independent optimization, and technology mapping. Finally, we envision AI-native design automation that jointly explores architectural, RTL, and EDA algorithm design spaces, guided by verification constraints and implementation feedback.

</details>


### [203] [SetPrint: Black-Box LLM Fingerprinting via Setwise Correspondence of Response Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12950)
*Jinmo Han,Ju Yeon Kang,Jimin Hong,Sunghwan Ahn,Yonghyeon Jun,Nam Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SetPrint 是一种无需训练的方法，能够同时捕捉响应变化集合内部关系和两个集合之间的对应关系，从而在 LeaFBench 测试集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒语言模型指纹识别方法未能充分利用响应变化之间的内部关系和集合间的对应关系。为了改进这一问题，提出了一种无需训练的方法 SetPrint。

Method: SetPrint 方法通过对每个响应变化相对于其自身集合均值变化的表达，并比较两种模型在相同扰动下产生的变化来进行预测。

Result: SetPrint 在 LeaFBench 单元测试集上表现优于现有的无需训练的黑盒指纹识别方法。

Conclusion: 通过评估两种响应变化之间的内部关系和集合间的对应关系，SetPrint 方法提高了黑盒语言模型指纹识别的性能。

Abstract: Black-box LLM fingerprinting aims to determine whether a target model was derived from a source model when only the output responses to input queries are observable. One way to reveal this relationship is to perturb each query and observe the resulting responses of both models, yielding two corresponding sets of response variations. These sets provide two complementary sources of information: the relations among response changes within each set, and the correspondence of response changes between the two sets. Existing approaches, however, have not fully exploited both. We introduce \textbf{SetPrint}, a training-free method that jointly captures both within-set relations and between-set correspondence. SetPrint expresses each response change relative to the mean change of its own set, and then compares changes from the two models that were induced by the same perturbation. On LeaFBench, SetPrint consistently outperforms existing training-free black-box fingerprinting methods. Further analyses attribute these gains to both within-set relations and between-set correspondence.

</details>


### [204] [Agent Architecture Cost-Success Trade-offs: A Controlled Evaluation across Enterprise-Style SOPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12953)
*Subhrangshu Nandi,Hitesh Arora,Yunfei Bai,Udita Patel,Chirag Shah,Hannah Kwon,Wei Zhang,Weian Chen,Chloe Ruoqi Li,Alberto Sconfienza,Ayush Goyal,Yasmine Lee,Ming Xiong,Prathap Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过比较四种代理架构在13个企业标准操作流程（SOP）中的表现，发现没有一种普遍适用的最佳架构，SOP的特性决定了最适合的架构。虽然多代理或技能增强架构增加了成本，但在某些情况下确实提高了任务成功，但关键在于实现质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前企业使用大型语言模型代理执行SOPs的需求提升，但缺乏针对不同代理架构的有效对比分析。因此，通过固定模型、任务和工具，仅改变架构，研究在13个企业SOP中的表现差异，旨在指导企业更合理地选择合适的代理架构。

Method: 固定模型、任务和工具，改变代理商的架构，通过模型-任务-工具的固定不变，比较四种代理商架构在13个企业SOP中的表现，同时开发了一种技能定义质量评定标准来构建高质量和低质量技能语料库。结果显示了不同架构在各SOP中的成功率，并分析了每种架构的得失。

Result: 研究得出，在执行复杂、分支的SOPs时，结构化的架构可能是有潜力的，但目前尚没有足够的统计证明这一点。多代理或技能增强架构虽然增加了成本负担，但在某些SOPs中确实提高了任务成功，尤其是高质量技能提升了任务成功的概率。同时，研究强调了实现质量的重要性，合理的多代理实现甚至能够超越简单的单代理实现。

Conclusion: 企业应根据具体SOP的需求，综合考量不同架构的成本与收益，选择最适合的架构。未来应进一步研究多代理架构在复杂SOPs中的潜力以及提升实现质量的方法。

Abstract: Enterprises increasingly use large language model agents to execute standard operating procedures (SOPs): long, branching, tool-intensive workflows. Yet there is little controlled evidence for choosing among competing agentic architectures. Holding the model, tasks, and tools fixed and varying only the architecture, we compare four designs (a function-calling single agent, a skill-augmented agent, centralized multi-agent orchestration, and a triage swarm; eight arms) across 13 enterprise SOPs, and introduce a skill-definition quality rubric to build matched high and low-quality skill corpora. Three takeaways emerge. (1)~\textbf{There is no universal best architecture; the right choice is SOP-dependent.} On a given SOP, task success ranges from under $10\%$ on the hardest to near $100\%$ on the easiest; no architecture is statistically separable in aggregate, and added structure only plausibly pays off on complex, branching SOPs (a directional, not yet significant, trend). (2)~\textbf{Added architecture cost must be justified by a task-success gain.} Every coordinated arm (multi-agent or skill-augmented) spends $1.4$--$4.7\times$ the tokens and $2.6$--$5.8\times$ the latency of the single agent for gains that are usually small and rarely significant, making the single agent the efficient default; high-quality skills do lift success (to $73.1\%$ from $67.4\%$) while degraded skills fall below it. (3)~\textbf{Implementation quality outweighs the single- versus multi-agent choice.} A plausible multi-agent implementation loses to the single agent, yet a targeted redesign of it becomes the only arm to significantly beat the baseline ($75.6\%$ vs.\ $67.4\%$).

</details>


### [205] [Physics2Weight: Direct Neural Network Architecture and Weight Generation from Physical Laws Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12954)
*Shogo Sato,Tomoyasu Shimada,Kazuhiko Murasaki,Takashi Sano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Physics2Weight (PtW) 框架，该框架通过直接生成神经网络权重并基于 governing 方程和初始边界条件调整架构，将权重空间编程 (WSP) 扩展到解决偏微分方程，从而提高解的精度和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，大型语言模型 (LLMs) 的进展让权重空间编程 (WSP) 成为了可能，即直接生成神经网络权重而无需基于梯度的优化。然而，将 WSP 进一步推广到物理问题，尤其是达到解的准确性和物理一致性仍然极具挑战性。

Method: Physics2Weight (PtW) 引入了物理约束提示和基于物理指导的架构编辑，并与 WSP 的迭代权重精炼相结合。

Result: 在四个 PDE（热传递、对流、Burgers' 和 Allen-Cahn 方程）上的实验表明，PtW 的解误差低于传统的物理感知神经网络，但仍对非线性 PDE 的 governing 方程约束满足存在局限性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 Physics2Weight (PtW) 框架为解决偏微分方程提供了一种新的方法，通过直接生成权重并结合物理约束来提高解的精度和物理一致性，但仍需进一步研究以克服非线性 PDE 的限制。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled weight-space programming (WSP), which directly generates neural network weights without gradient-based optimization. However, extending WSP to physical problems remains challenging, particularly in achieving both solution accuracy and physical consistency. In this paper, we propose Physics2Weight (PtW), a framework that extends WSP to solving partial differential equations by directly generating neural network weights and editing architectures based on governing equations and initial and boundary conditions. PtW introduces physics-constrained prompting and physics-guided architecture editing, combined with iterative weight refinement from WSP, to improve solution accuracy and physical consistency without gradient-based optimization. Experiments on four PDEs, namely the heat, advection, Burgers', and Allen--Cahn equations, demonstrate lower solution errors than conventional physics-informed neural networks, while revealing limitations in satisfying the governing equations for nonlinear PDEs.

</details>


### [206] [Embodied Science Agents: Making Embodied Experimental Design Learnable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12958)
*Yihang Che,Zhongwei Yu,Yang Li,Xingyu Lu,Hongxia Yu,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了Embodied Science Agent (ESA)，旨在解决自驾车实验室(SDL)中的基础模型整合问题，通过结合数字智能与物理体现，旨在实现自我驱动的科学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自驾车实验室存在一个根本悖论，即虽然增强了语言的复杂性，但与物理现实的联系却减弱了。当前架构导致了体化实验直觉的发展受阻。解决这一悖论的关键是‘可学性’的嵌入，涉及认知学习、实验学习和体化学习三个相互独立的维度。

Method: ESA由三个协同工作的组件组成：数字代理提供基于记忆的推理和贝叶斯优化的认知性和实验性可学性；体化代理提供通过可泛化的仪器控制体现性可学性；以及体化统一界面用于无缝协调。

Result: ESA在自主水处理实验中得到了验证，能够在超过2亿种可能条件下导航。连续进行15轮实验后，最终解决方案透过率从82%提升到99.7%，导致剩余染料浓度减少了98.5%，接近去色。同时，学习到的视觉-运动策略在不同仪器位置上具有广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过ESA为未来自驾车实验室的发展提供了一种具有里程碑意义的方法，朝着自我驱动科学研究迈出了一大步。

Abstract: Recent advances in large models have accelerated AI for Science toward agentic science, where AI agents autonomously conduct scientific discovery. Realising this vision requires closing the loop between computational reasoning and physical experimentation, positioning self-driving laboratories (SDLs) as critical enablers. However, existing SDLs exhibit a fundamental paradox: foundation model integration has increased linguistic sophistication while severing physical grounding. By treating models as disembodied planners that output symbolic workflows for separate robotic systems, current architectures inherently block the development of embodied experimental intuition. We argue that overcoming this paradox requires embedding \emph{learnability} along three interdependent axes: cognitive learnability (learning what to investigate), experimental learnability (learning where to search), and embodied learnability (learning how to act). Building on this view, we introduce the Embodied Science Agent (ESA), which unifies digital intelligence with physical embodiment through three synergistic components: a Digital Agent that provides cognitive and experimental learnability via memory-based reasoning and Bayesian optimisation, an Embodied Agent that provides embodied learnability via generalisable instrument control, and an Embodied Unified Interface for seamless coordination. We validated ESA in autonomous wastewater treatment experiments, navigating over 200 million possible conditions. Through 15 continuous rounds, ESA raised the final solution transmittance from 82\% to 99.7\%, corresponding to a 98.5\% reduction in residual dye concentration and near-complete decolourisation, while its learned visuomotor policies generalised across varying instrument placements, a promising step toward next-generation SDLs for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [207] [Monitoring One Channel Is Not Communication Control: Causal Evidence for Adaptive Reallocation of Joint Message--Route Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12959)
*Liu Hung Ming,Tian Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 监测可见信道会影响学习代理的通信方式，但不会消除通信的必要性。通过部分覆盖和完全覆盖实验，证明了路线的语义性，并进一步探讨了编码机制在不同监测条件下的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究监测可见信道对学得通信协议和路线策略的影响，以及监测如何改变通信行为但不改变其根本动机。

Method: 该研究通过一种端对端的游戏测试，其中发送者和接收者在没有通信监督的情况下学习离散协议和路由策略。研究者进行了两组独立的实验并分析了监测对通信协议和表现的影响。

Result: 在线部分覆盖增加了阴影路由的比例，完全覆盖则去除了大多数选择性迁移。实验数据表明，路由具有语义性，去除语义后接收者的表现明显下降。实施静默成本后，有18/20的策略完全静默，但完全静默的严格闸门未能完全停止通信。

Conclusion: 实验结论支持在部分监测下适应性重新分配联合消息-路由编码，而不是开放世界的路由发现、自发加密或普遍的防御机制。

Abstract: Monitoring a visible channel can change how learned agents communicate without removing why communication is useful. We test this distinction in an end-to-end game where a sender and receiver learn a discrete protocol and route policy without communication supervision. In two independent 20-seed batches, 20/20 and 19/20 protocols form. Online partial coverage increases shadow-route share over no monitoring by .701 [.574,.826] and .575 [.412,.737], while task accuracy remains .905/.913 and signed frozen message contribution remains .592/.563. Complete coverage removes most selective migration without removing communication. Frozen interventions, including a fresh pre-outcome confirmation, show that route is semantic: joint message--route shuffling changes .668 of receiver predictions and route alone retains .388 accuracy versus .25 chance. A corrected explicit-silence cost makes 18/20 policies fully silent and lowers mean sending to .050, but its strict complete-suppression gate fails because two seeds retain communication. Failed semantic-evasion and operational gates further bound the result. The evidence supports adaptive reallocation of a joint message--route code under partial monitoring, not open-world route discovery, emergent encryption, or a universal defense.

</details>


### [208] [IIT Patna at "ICPR 2026 TVRID Competition on Privacy-Preserving Person Re-Identification from Top-View RGB-Depth Camera"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12960)
*Abhinav Kumar,Swagatam Pati,Advait Deshmukh,Aditya Mehrotra,Bibek Das,Kumari Priya,Utsav Kumar Nareti,Saba Akram,Chandranath Adak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究概述了ICPR 2026 TVRID竞赛的设置及参赛团队的方法。竞赛使用四台Intel RealSense D455相机的RGB和Depth流从不同视角捕获86名身份信息，评估指标为mAP和CMC@1。通过两个排名前10的团队的方法展示了深度特性对于隐私保护Re-ID的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着越来越多的摄像头用于监视和识别个人，传统的人脸识别方法可能会泄露个人隐私。通过将焦点转向身体形状和深度信息，该竞赛旨在评估在保护隐私的同时进行个人再识别的有效方法。

Method: 竞赛采用了四台同步的Intel RealSense D455相机，从不同的视点（平坦、上升、下降、斜坡）收集数据，利用RGB和Depth流数据进行分析。参赛团队使用了不同的方法，包括深度表征和监督，以提高识别率。

Result: Methodology 1 获得93.51%的mAP和90.42%的CMC@1（Depth流），排在第4名，而Methodology 2 在RGB流中获得99.63%的mAP和99.35%的CMC@1，在Depth流中达到86.26%的mAP和80.34%的CMC@1。这些结果表明深度信息对于实现隐私保护的Re-ID具有重要意义。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，深度信息在隐私保护的Re-ID任务中尤为重要，且比传统的基于颜色的识别方法性能更优。

Abstract: Privacy-aware person re-identification (Re-ID) from top-view cameras replaces frontal appearance with anthropometric shape, while depth sensing further reduces color-based identity cues. The ICPR 2026 TVRID competition evaluates this setting using 86 identities captured by four synchronized Intel RealSense D455 cameras with RGB and Depth streams across flat, ascent, descent, and oblique viewpoints. Performance is measured using mAP and CMC@1 across within-camera in/out, ascent-descent, and flat-oblique scenarios. The competition had 147 registered participants and 33 final submissions. Three teams from IIT Patna participated: 'abhinavkumar03' and 'sjkdgfhl_' ranked 5th and 7th among 31 ranked participants, and 'vivek katuri' reached 77.67% CMC@1 / 85.20% mAP on the RGB track. This paper presents the methods of the two top-10 teams. Methodology 1 (abhinavkumar03) achieves 93.51% mAP and 90.42% CMC@1 on Depth, ranking 4th of 28, while Methodology 2 (sjkdgfhl_) reaches 99.63%/99.35% on RGB and 86.26%/80.34% on Depth. Both substantially outperform the 57.19% track-average Depth mAP, with Methodology 1 yielding a 7.3-point gain over Methodology 2. These results demonstrate the importance of depth representation and supervision for privacy-preserving Re-ID.

</details>


### [209] [RBLA+: Efficient Rank-Specific LoRA Adapter Extraction for Federated Fine-Tuning with Heterogeneous Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12961)
*Shuaijun Chen,Omid Tavallaie,Xin Chen,Wentao Gao,Nguyen Tran,Albert Y. Zomaya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了RBLA+方法，通过使用紧凑的QR/core-SVD分解来减少提取的LoRA更新的弗罗贝尼乌斯误差，同时考虑客户端和服务器的缩放约定。这种方法在多个异构设置中提高了视觉和语言任务的准确性，并且在CPU基准测试中，与密集的截断SVD基线相比，具有较低的提取时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Federated Fine-tuning中不同的客户端可能具有不同的LoRA秩数，需要提出一种在聚合后能够高效处理不同秩数并向每个客户端生成适配器的方法。

Method: RBLA+ 使用紧凑的 QR/core-SVD 分解方法来减少从聚合的 LoRA 因子中提取的更新的弗罗贝尼乌斯误差，而无需形成密集的更新矩阵。

Result: 在视觉和语言任务中，RBLA+ 组装的准确率优于 RBLA，并且在 CPU 基准测试中的提取时间比密集的截断 SVD 基线更低。它还能够保持每轮通信量恒定，符合每个客户端指定的适配器大小。

Conclusion: RBLA+ 提供了一种有效的方法来处理不同客户端的适应需求，并在多种情况下展示了其在提高模型性能方面的优势。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning with low-rank adaptation (LoRA) reduces communication and computational overhead by training and exchanging compact trainable LoRA factors. Clients may use different LoRA ranks to meet adapter communication budgets and accommodate different local adaptation needs. The server must process these differences during aggregation and construct adapters at each client's rank for local training. We distinguish pre-aggregation alignment, which handles factor dimensions and scaling conventions before aggregation and rank-specific LoRA adapter extraction, which constructs client-sized pairs from the aggregated global model. Two common approaches to rank-specific extraction are direct rank truncation and Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) of the dense update. Direct prefix truncation keeps the leading columns of one aggregated factor and the corresponding rows of the other. It is cheap but can increase reconstruction error. The dense SVD approach first forms the product of aggregated LoRA factors. It then decomposes this matrix and retains the leading singular components to construct adapters at the target rank. This gives the optimal rank-constrained approximation in Frobenius norm but incurs significant computational overhead. To address this trade-off, we propose RBLA+ as an extension of RBLA. It uses established compact QR/core-SVD decomposition to minimize Frobenius error for the extracted LoRA update without forming the dense update. It also accounts for client and server scaling conventions during upload and broadcast. Across vision and language tasks, RBLA+ improves accuracy over RBLA in multiple heterogeneous settings. In CPU benchmarks with synthetic adapters, RBLA+ has lower extraction time than the dense partial-SVD baseline. RBLA+ preserves each client's prescribed adapter size and per-round communication payload.

</details>


### [210] [BuySideFlow: A Point-in-Time Benchmark for LLM Agents in Investment Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12966)
*Dong Bo,Fanjin Zhang,Ke Cheng,Xinxin Zhao,Xiaowei Su,Yang Wang,Jinghang Xu,Cuiping Li,Hong Chen,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究引入了BuySideFlow基准，包含408个手工设计的中文任务，涵盖股票、基金、固定收益和宏观经济等领域。评估结果显示九种智能代理配置的准确性范围从16.2%到36.8%不等，表明输出一致性无法保证时间准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型（LLMs）能够生成SQL和SQL-Python工作流以支持投资研究，但在保持点在时间（PIT）有效性方面仍面临挑战。

Method: 研究设计了BuySideFlow基准，并包括可执行参考和时间错误分类，同时执行工作流并进行专家审查。

Result: 九种智能代理配置在所有任务上的结果准确性从16.2%到36.8%不等。审查900次运行中100个共享任务的执行情况，在380次通过结果检查的运行中有193次（50.8%）发现了PIT违规情况。

Conclusion: 研究成果表明，输出一致性不能确保时间准确性，进一步强调了在投资研究中PIT一致性的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate SQL and SQL--Python workflows for investment research, but ensuring point-in-time (PIT) validity remains challenging. Agents must enforce disclosure cutoffs and select the appropriate historical states and record versions throughout data retrieval and financial analysis. We introduce \textsc{BuySideFlow}, a benchmark of 408 practitioner-derived Chinese-language tasks spanning equities, funds, fixed income, and macroeconomics over a fixed snapshot from a licensed production financial database. We provide executable references and a taxonomy of temporal errors, pairing result checks with expert audits of executed workflows. We evaluate nine agent configurations on all tasks, with result accuracy ranging from 16.2\% to 36.8\%. An audit of 900 runs on 100 shared tasks confirms PIT violations in 193 of the 380 runs that pass result checks (50.8\%), showing that output agreement alone does not establish temporal validity.

</details>


### [211] [TrustMod-SM: A Multi-Axis Benchmark for Evaluating Trustworthiness of LLMs in Social Media Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12970)
*Aizan Zafar,Sreekantam Sai Venkata,Gathik Jindal,Ritu Raj Sharma,Zishan Ahmad,Tulika Saha,Srinath Srinivasa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为TrustMod-SM的基准，涵盖了八个多数据集的约29,000个实例，评估了16个开放重量模型和一个封闭前沿模型在信任度、公平性、安全性、稳健性和上下文完整性这五个维度上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为大型语言模型（LLMs）在社交媒体内容审核方面具有巨大的潜力，但对其除了整体准确性之外的信任度仍缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 作者构建了一个名为TrustMod-SM的基准，评估了16个不同参数规模（0.5B-284B）的模型在不同的维度上的表现。

Result: 研究发现，尽管检测准确性较高，但模型在信任度、公平性、安全性、稳健性和上下文完整性五个维度上仍存在差距。大模型在处理某些特定任务时存在偏见和局限性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，没有任何一个模型能够在所有五个维度上表现卓越，模型的规模并不能均匀解决这些问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show strong potential for social media content moderation, but their trustworthiness beyond overall accuracy remains underexplored. We introduce TrustMod-SM, a benchmark of roughly 29,000 instances across eight datasets, to evaluate LLM moderators on five dimensions: trustfulness, fairness, safety, robustness, and context integrity. Evaluating 16 open-weight models (0.5B-284B parameters, spanning text-only and vision-language architectures) and one closed frontier model, we find persistent trustworthiness gaps. Higher detection accuracy often coincides with greater demographic disparity, and models frequently comply with concealment or exaggeration prompts, assign high confidence to wrong predictions, rarely escalate ambiguous cases, and struggle to separate hate speech from counter-speech or reclaimed language (especially below 8B parameters). Scaling does not uniformly resolve these issues, and no single model excels across all five dimensions.

</details>


### [212] [ZeroPoint: Lightweight Energy-Based Decision Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12992)
*Javier Marín*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZeroPoint 是一种增强版的基于能量的模型（EBM），它引入了一个固定的能量为零的状态来表示“空答案”，这为不同选项提供了统一的参考标准，使得模型不仅能够选择最佳选项，还能在没有合适选项时选择“空答案”。模型通过普通的负对数似然损失进行训练，无需额外的拒绝准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 EBMs 只有相对的兼容性定义，但缺乏一个绝对的参考点，这在决定是否选择任一选项时存在局限性。ZeroPoint 通过引入一个固定能耗为零的“空答案”状态，解决了这一问题，使得在没有合适选项时能够更好地进行决策。

Method: ZeroPoint 使用了一个小型的多语言编码器，该编码器可以读取输入和所有选项，并且对选项顺序不敏感。通过这种方式，模型实现了在没有合适选项时选择“空答案”的可能性。

Result: 实验结果表明，相比于其他拒绝策略，ZeroPoint 在区分具有和不具有合适选项的输入方面表现更佳，特别是在没有合适选项时选择“空答案”的情况下。

Conclusion: ZeroPoint 提供了一种新的方法，通过引入固定能耗为零的“空答案”状态，解决传统 EBMs 的相对兼容性问题，使模型能够更加灵活地处理没有最佳选项的情况。

Abstract: Energy-based models (EBMs) assign a scalar energy to every joint value of the observed and predicted variables. In decision models, the prediction is chosen from a finite list of options provided with each input. Inference then reduces to comparing the energies of the options, and training lowers the energy of the correct option relative to the others. A complete decision must also account for the possibility that no option is acceptable. However, the energies learned by a standard EBM define only relative compatibility: adding the same input-dependent quantity to all energies leaves the predictions, the Gibbs distribution, and the usual training losses unchanged. The model therefore has no learned reference level against which to decide whether any option fits. We propose ZeroPoint, an EBM that makes the empty answer a state of the model with energy zero for every input. This fixed state provides an explicit reference for the energies of all listed options, whose non-negative depths measure their compatibility with the input relative to the empty answer. The options and the empty answer share one distribution, in which an option at zero depth receives no probability. The decision to select an option or to abstain is thus part of ordinary inference, trained with the negative log-likelihood loss, rather than a separate rejection rule. The energy function is a small multilingual encoder that reads the input together with all the options and is invariant to their order. Ablations show that most of the benefit comes from training with examples in which no option fits. With the same encoder and data, the fixed zero separates inputs with and without an acceptable option slightly better than a learned rejection class and clearly better than a thresholded softmax, while leaving the choice among options unchanged.

</details>


### [213] [Seeing Is Not Believing: Counterfactual Lesion Erasure Teaches a Small Medical Vision-Language Agent to Report Only What the Image Shows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13006)
*Bhaskar Gurram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于医疗影像分析的视觉-语言模型（VLMs），并通过构建无手动注释的反事实视图，评估了模型对病变的感知能力。在507个内部病例中，MedScope-4B在反事实视图处理后的证据敏感性差距（ESG）为0.758，显著高于其他模型，且在新的外部测试中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的分割数据集可能不足以检测视觉-语言模型在病变感知上的差异性表现，因此研究团队设计了一种新的方法，通过反事实视图来评估和对比模型的效果。

Method: 研究团队使用公开的分割数据集构建了反事实视图，包括擦除病灶后的图像、相同形状但位置不同的控制编辑图像，以及未经编辑的病灶自由图像。通过计算模型对这些视图的响应差异来评估其在病变感知上的能力。

Result: 实验结果显示，MedScope-4B在反事实视图处理后的证据敏感性差距（ESG）为0.758，远高于其他模型；同时在具体的测试中，对于病灶的识别更加谨慎准确，且在新的外部测试中心也表现优于其他模型。

Conclusion: 研究发现，通过训练模型处理擦除和编辑后的图像，可以使模型具备更强的证据敏感性，但这种方法不能显著提高模型的感知能力。这表明仅通过增强训练数据并不能完全弥补模型在复杂临床场景中的不足，尤其是在稀少的关键发现领域。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) often report a finding because the question names it; standard benchmarks miss this. From public segmentation datasets we build counterfactual views without manual annotation: the lesion erased by inpainting, a same-shape control edit elsewhere, and unedited lesion-free images. The evidence sensitivity gap (ESG) is the rate of absent-or-refer answers after erasure minus the rate after the control edit; it is zero for a model that ignores the image or reacts to both edits alike. MedScope-4B (Qwen3.5-4B, LoRA, image tools) was trained on label-derived trajectories, selected by a pre-registered rule and tested once against nine frontier and six open VLMs; four pre-registered checks then probed the mechanism. On 507 internal cases across five imaging types, MedScope-4B reached ESG 0.758 and claimed an erased lesion in 9.6% of cases, against 0.27-0.47 and 41-62% for the frontier models (all p<0.001), without significant loss of accuracy on real lesions. Without counterfactual views ESG fell to 0.279+-0.107 (three seeds); the effect replicated on MedGemma 1.5 4B and two unseen inpainting tools. At four external centres the advantage held but frontier models detected more real lesions; on a fresh external test MedScope-4B detected 0 of 48 pneumothorax films, with net utility below gemini-4-argon's (-0.36 [-0.50, -0.23]). Matching the control edit was not shown to matter; over three seeds no fresh-external ESG advantage over removal-based DPO was established; a paired-view consistency objective did not beat a matched-compute control, while that continuation alone cut erased-lesion claims on unseen tools at every seed, at a cost in sensitivity at one. Training on erased and edited images gives a small model an evidence-gated disposition that prompting does not give frontier models; it does not improve perception, and rare critical findings need coverage.

</details>


### [214] [From Questions to Decisions: Benchmarking How LLM Agents Acquire Decision-Relevant Information from Human Beings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13015)
*Yuhan Tang,Ke Fan,Xinkai Zou,Baichuan Mo,Kangxin Cui,Ao Qu,Ou Zheng,Jiangbo Yu,Haris N. Koutsopoulos,Jinhua Zhao,Yuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入Ask2Decide基准测试，评估了LLM在决策过程中获取关键缺失信息的能力，发现即使提供部分信息，许多前沿的LLM在规划任务上仍存在严重问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常提供完整规划问题或依赖于未校准的语言模型模拟用户，未能充分测试LLM请求关键信息的能力。因此，研究人员创建了Ask2Decide基准测试来弥补这一空白。

Method: 研究人员设计了一个涵盖43项规划任务的基准测试，其中包含关键缺失信息的操作员记录和优化求解器，以确定这些信息是否改变了最优化计划。此外，还收集了79次互动会话来自19名参与者，并根据真实用户反馈调整了模拟操作员。

Result: 在缺少关键信息的情况下，四款领先语言模型中只有5-28%的规划任务可行，而当所有相关信息提供时，这一比例上升至58-98%。然而，模型发现可能改变最优化计划的关键信息的能力仅略高于忽略这些信息的可能性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在规划过程中获得相关决策信息是使用LLM进行规划的主要瓶颈，需要进一步强化和改进。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly asked to make plans for people, such as scheduling drivers or assigning nurses. However, people's requests are often incomplete, and the supporting information for decision-making may be outdated, requiring agents to ask for clarification. Existing benchmarks typically provide fully specified problems or rely on uncalibrated LLM-simulated users, leaving this ability insufficiently tested. We introduce Ask2Decide, a benchmark of 43 planning tasks that evaluates whether agents ask for missing information that matters for their decisions. An operator holds facts absent from the agent's request and records, and optimization solvers determine which facts change the optimal plan. We also include similarly worded facts that leave the optimal plan unchanged to test whether agents distinguish useful information from irrelevant details. We collect 79 interaction sessions from 19 participants and find that, after an incorrect plan, human replies omit the correcting fact far more often than replies from an uncalibrated simulated user (43% versus 7%). Using these interactions, we calibrate a simulated operator that more closely matches human reply patterns. Evaluating four frontier LLMs, we find that their initial plans are infeasible on 58-98% of tasks when information is missing, compared with 5-28% when all facts are provided upfront. Agents uncover facts that change the optimal plan only slightly more often than facts that leave it unchanged (39-52% versus 32-41%). These results show that obtaining decision-relevant information through interaction remains a major bottleneck for LLM planning. This is ongoing work and will be updated. The date of this version is shown on the first page.

</details>


### [215] [Multi-Future Drafting: Mixture Block Drafters for Tree-Verified Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13017)
*Haoyu Huang,Jialiang Kang,Linlin Yang,Xiao Yang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了 Multi-Future Drafting (MFD) 方法，通过引入一个潜在变量替换链式法则，能够在一次前向传递中生成 K 个连贯的块，并且使用混合 Draft Tree 允许验证器选择目标实际采用的未来路径，从而可以达到最高 7.8 倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如 DFlash 在一次前向传递中生成未来的 token，而 DDTree 等验证方法则会导致预算分配不均，形成无法实现的 chimera 前缀。因此，该研究旨在开发一种新的方法以改善这一问题。

Method: MFD 方法通过在 drafter 的输入中学习 K 个模式嵌入来生成 K 个连贯的块，并让混合 Draft Tree 根据实际目标的路径进行选择。这种方法不依赖于传统的链式法则，而是用一个潜在变量进行耦合。

Result: 实验结果表明，MFD 方法在四个从 4B 到 27B 参数的 Qwen 目标上，于 batch size 为 1 的情况下，可以达到最高 7.8 倍的速度提升。在 Qwen3-4B 和 Qwen3-8B 上，MFD 方法比同一训练提示下发布的 DARTree 快 9-16%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法 MFD 在提高速度的同时，还能更准确地匹配目标，从而实现更为优秀的性能。

Abstract: Block drafters such as DFlash draft a block of future tokens in one forward pass, and draft trees such as DDTree verify the prefixes ranked highest by the product of the drafter's per-position distributions. Under tree verification, the expected number of accepted tokens is the probability mass the tree covers. We show that a product misallocates it: when the continuation can follow several futures, it splices them into chimeras, prefixes stitched together from different futures that the target never produces, and gives them as much budget as the futures themselves. Barring ties, no product-ranked tree can hold two futures that disagree in $m$ positions without $2^{m-1}-1$ chimeras, however well the drafter is trained, and in practice chimeras make up 27-33% of a 64-node tree. We propose Multi-Future Drafting (MFD), which restores the coupling with a latent variable instead of the chain rule: $K$ learned mode embeddings at the drafter's input draft $K$ coherent blocks in the same pass, and a mixture DDTree lets the verifier pick the future the target takes, losslessly and with $K+1$ extra vectors. On four Qwen targets from 4B to 27B parameters, at batch size 1, MFD reaches up to 7.8$\times$ speedup, 5-8% faster than the best single-mode tree trained identically and, on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, 9-16% faster than the released DARTree trained on the same prompts.

</details>


### [216] [ChipOmni: A General-Purpose AI Agent for Electronic Design Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13020)
*Pengkun Wang,Rui Mao,Zhe Zhao,Dongting Xie,Haibin Wen,Song Xu,Di Wu,Xu Wang,Zhengyang Zhou,Binwu Wang,Xiaofeng Cao,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChipOmni是一个多模态理解和动态规划的AI框架，能够跨异构任务集成多平台协作和验证驱动的工具互动，从而自主优化设计，验证并修复设计。该研究在多个基准测试中的表现优于现有方法，并实现了一个自然语言到微片级SoC设计的长周期自动化案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EDA流程高度分散，工程师需要协调不同的软件工具和设计阶段。虽然大规模语言模型在EDA中带来了更普遍的推理能力和代码生成功能，但现有方法仍聚焦于孤立任务，难以实现自主设计、验证和修复。因此，引入了ChipOmni框架，旨在开发一个能够支持自动生成设计文件、多角色协作和集成反馈的AI代理系统。

Method: ChipOmni框架融合了多模态理解、动态规划、多代理协作和验证驱动的工具交互。通过不断从外部工程工具中获取反馈，框架实现了一个不断演变的任务上下文，整合了领域知识，并逐步优化设计。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，ChipOmni在多个具体的任务指标上优于现有基准，并成功解决了30项AnalogCoder-Pro任务。在一个长周期案例中，ChipOmni从自然语言RISC-V规范自动化推进到微片级SoC的GDSII布局，无需人工干预。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了ChipOmni框架能够协调异构任务的设计验证，并持续优化长周期的工程流程，从工具中心流程向代理中心闭环系统转变。
