### [288] [Don't Just Trust Your Memory: Evidence-Linked Memory Reduces Memory-Induced Degradation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14320)
*Yukyeong Lee,Yumin Choi,Jinheon Baek,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WikiTrace 通过将教训与原始轨迹链接起来，允许代理检查教训的有效性，从而减少记忆引起的退化并提高任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的记忆系统使代理在任务中可能会错误地遵循教训，导致性能下降，WikiTrace 旨在通过将教训链接到其原始轨迹来解决此问题。

Method: WikiTrace 通过存储每个教训的简短见解，并链接到其原始轨迹的原因总结和原始轨迹本身来实现。在处理新任务时，代理会从所有见解开始，如果某个见解缺乏执行所需的信息、与另一个见解冲突或来自失败的任务，则将通过链接追溯到终端工具以补充缺失的信息或纠正见解。

Result: WikiTrace 在五个代理性基准测试中表现出色，不仅超越了现有的记忆方法，还在相同见解下减少了记忆引起的退化。

Conclusion: WikiTrace 通过保持教训与原始轨迹的联系，能够显著提升代理在新任务中的表现，减少了记忆引起的退化风险。

Abstract: External memory enables LLM agents to improve from experience by distilling trajectories of past tasks into concise lessons for new tasks. However, memory can also lead to memory-induced degradation, where an agent fails on tasks it would solve without memory, often because it follows a lesson that is incorrect, or misapplies or misreads a valid one. This is because lessons are inferred from trajectories labeled only with task success or failure, yet are typically provided without the trajectories they came from, leaving the agent no way to check them. To address this, we introduce WikiTrace, an evidence-linked memory system designed to let agents check each lesson against its original trajectory. Specifically, motivated by the finding that a trajectory often still holds the evidence needed to catch an erroneous lesson, WikiTrace stores each lesson as a concise insight linked to a summary of why its trajectory succeeded or failed and to the raw trajectory itself. Then, on a new task, the agent starts from all insights and, whenever one lacks details needed to act, conflicts with another, or comes from a failed task, descends through its links with terminal tools to fill in missing details or correct the insight before acting. We validate WikiTrace on five agentic benchmarks, showing that it consistently outperforms existing memory approaches and a baseline given the same insights alone, while also reducing memory-induced degradation. Our code is available at https://github.com/yukyeongleee/wikitrace

</details>


### [289] [When Should Agents Remember? Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14326)
*Runxuan Tang,Haoyu Gao,Yuyan Ding,Junyi Yao,Zihao Zheng,Zecheng Sheng,Liwei Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FALSIFICATION-GATED SELF-EVOLUTION (FGSE)的方法，该方法将经验推导出的行为更新作为受验证器支持的结构化假设进行处理，经过验证后，能够显著提高任务性能并减少错误更新，尤其是在长时间应用环境中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的自改进机制直接将经验教训写入记忆中，导致局部成功无法可靠地成为可重复和安全的知识信号。为了解决这个问题，本文提出了一种验证器驱动的框架FGSE，用于决定何时将经验教训接受为持久代理状态。

Method: FGSE将候选教训表示为具有预条件、行为改变、预期效果和验证器的结构化假设。它仅在临时状态中应用这些假设，并在目标移植、反驳和归档回归探针上进行测试。基于测量的优势和风险，它将更新提交、优化或拒绝。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，FGSE取得了很强的任务性能，包括39.8的WebArena成功率、78.8的AppWorld平均完成率，以及0.724/0.487的Pass1在τ-Retail/τ-Airline上的成绩。此外，FGSE将反驳失败减少到5.0%，回归损害减少到1.3%，有害的提交更新减少到3.6%。

Conclusion: 本文表明，将记忆更新作为假设进行测试有助于更可靠地进行长期自适应，通过FGSE框架的应用，这种策略能够提升LLM代理在丰富验证者环境中的长期适应性和可靠性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate in long-horizon, verifier-rich environments, but current self-improvement mechanisms often write episode-derived lessons directly into memory, making local success an unreliable signal for reusable and safe knowledge. This paper addresses the core question of when an experience-derived behavioral update should be admitted into persistent agent state. We propose Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution (FGSE), a verifier-grounded framework that represents each candidate lesson as a structured hypothesis with an explicit precondition, behavior change, expected effect, and verifier. FGSE applies the hypothesis only in a temporary state, tests it on target-transfer, falsification, and archived-regression probes, and then commits, refines, or rejects it according to measured gain and risk. Across web, app, tool-use, and long-term memory benchmarks, FGSE achieves strong task performance, including 39.8 WebArena success rate, 78.8 AppWorld average completion, and 0.724/0.487 Pass$^1$ on $τ$-Retail/$τ$-Airline, while reducing falsification failures to 5.0%, regression damage to 1.3%, and harmful committed updates to 3.6%. These results suggest that testing memory updates as hypotheses supports more reliable long-term adaptation.

</details>


### [290] [CLADE: Category-Level Answering from Deficient Evidence in Retrieval-Augmented Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14355)
*S M Rayeed,Charles V. Stewart,Yao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了检索增强系统在回答精细粒度问题时遇到的问题，并提出了一种名为‘可解析不足’的新概念，即只有更广泛的类别信息而缺乏具体细节的段落。研究通过语言模型实验和生态学物种互动的例子，展示了使用领域本体解决此类问题的有效性，同时指出模型选择类别的方式仍需改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前检索增强系统在回答问题时虽然能够找到相关的段落，但这些段落通常提供的信息较为粗略，不足以直接回答问题中的具体细节。研究旨在探索具体解决这类问题的方法。

Method: 通过对照实验的方法，研究使用语言模型对具有广义疾病名称和不存在信息的段落进行处理，并通过引入领域本体来判断准确性。

Result: 与空白段落相比，使用具有广义疾病名称的段落降低了13.8%的犹豫率，但模型的回答错误率达到了50%。引入领域本体后，模型能够包括正确答案的概率提高了98.5%，然而直接通过字符串匹配找到正确类别的比例仅19.8%。当允许模型选择类别时，正确识别率提高到30.0%，最终整体恢复率从10.0%提升到26.8%。

Conclusion: 研究认为，当面对这类粗略信息时，模型应该停止猜测并咨询领域本体，但是当前技术仍然存在局限性，如何有效选择类别仍是待解决的问题之一。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented systems often retrieve passages that are accurate and relevant but state the answer at a coarser level of granularity than the question requires. For example, a question may ask which specific disease a drug treats (familial hyperlipidemia), while the retrieved passage mentions the broader disease family (hyperlipidemia). Prior work on context sufficiency treats such coarse passages simply as insufficient. We argue that they form a distinct case, which we call resolvable insufficiency: text alone cannot settle the fine-grained answer, but the broader category it mentions maps to a short list of candidate answers in a domain ontology. When language models read these coarse, family-only passages, they exhibit a phenomenon we call Clade (Category-Level Answering from Deficient Evidence): they answer instead of abstaining or searching further. On biomedical questions from PrimeKG, family-only passages reduce abstention by 13.8 points compared to uninformative passages, and the resulting guesses are wrong about half the time. A counterfactual test confirms the cause: replacing an exact disease name with its broader family name lowers abstention by 17.4 points compared to deleting the sentence. Simple fixes like confidence filtering, scaling to a larger model, and explicitly instructing the model to abstain cannot resolve Clade, and this replicates on ecological species interactions in GloBI. Finally, we test whether an ontology can turn coarse passages into exact answers. Given the correct family, an ontology list includes the true answer in 98.5% of cases, but string matching finds that family on the page for only 19.8%. Letting the model choose the family raises this to 30.0% and end-to-end recovery from 10.0% to 26.8%. Overall, our results show when language models should stop guessing and consult an ontology, and highlight which steps of that process remain unsolved.

</details>


### [291] [Cognitive Mapping of Learning by Thinking Mechanisms in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14382)
*Dong Han,Jin Zhang,Kunyue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种认知LbT框架，将人类学习思考中的四种机制映射到大型语言模型的计算过程，探讨了这些机制的功能近似和潜在失败，并提出了关于LbT在大模型中何时成功或失败可测试的命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大型语言模型在某些任务上表现出类似人类学习思考（LbT）的能力，但缺乏对认知机制的理解和界限的研究，提出了此框架来探讨这一现象。

Method: 该论文通过结合代表性方法（如链式思考提示、自一致性、自我反思、树状思考搜索、自我指令和自我教导推理），分析大型语言模型的内部工作机制。

Result: 研究证明，特定方法可以功能上近似这些机制，并在某些任务上改善了性能，没有额外的标记示例。例如，自我指令从175个种子任务生成了约52,000个指令，相对提高了33%的Super-NaturalInstructions。然而，该方法不能保证模型参数中的持久学习。此外，这些机制还导致了诸如幻觉扩散、模型崩溃、概念漂移和高计算成本等失败。

Conclusion: 该框架为测试Llm中的LbT提供了可操作的假设，并为设计更可靠的自主学习系统提供了结构化的基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly exhibit capabilities that resemble human Learning by Thinking (LbT), the acquisition of new insight through internal processes such as explanation and reasoning, without new external input. A systematic account of the cognitive mechanisms behind these capabilities, and of their limits, remains underdeveloped. This paper proposes a Cognitive LbT Framework that maps four mechanisms (Reflective Reasoning, Synthetic Memory Formation, Internal Simulation, and Conceptual Self-Organization) onto computational processes in LLMs. Drawing on representative methods, including chain-of-thought prompting, self-consistency, self-reflection, Tree-of-Thoughts search, Self-Instruct, and self-taught reasoning, the analysis argues that selected methods can functionally approximate aspects of these mechanisms and can improve performance on some tasks without additional labeled examples, although this does not always constitute durable learning in model parameters. For example, Self-Instruct generated approximately 52,000 instructions from 175 seed tasks and reported a 33% absolute improvement on Super-NaturalInstructions (Y. Wang et al., 2023). The same mechanisms also produce characteristic failures, including hallucination diffusion, model collapse, concept drift, and high computational cost. These failures are not isolated technical defects but follow from four structural asymmetries between LLMs and human cognition: the identity of generator and monitor, closed learning loops without external grounding, symbolic rather than sensorimotor simulation, and the cost of inference-time computation. The framework yields testable propositions about when LbT in LLMs should succeed or fail and offers a structured basis for designing more reliable autonomous learning systems.

</details>


### [292] [An Energy-Based Perspective on Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14383)
*Bo Dai,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过强调能量模型在机器学习中的统一视角，讨论了能量模型的三个核心组成部分：能量函数、推理方法和学习方法，展示了多种模型和技术可以归类为能量模型的不同实现方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文章旨在通过能量模型提供一个统一的视角，连接机器学习的不同方面，并展示其广泛的应用和潜在的创新可能性。

Method: 文章首先介绍了能量模型的三个核心组成部分，即能量函数、推理方法（如采样和推断）和学习方法（如最大似然、噪声对比估计、伪似然、分位数匹配和对抗训练），并且展示了这些学习方法共享一种对比结构。然后，文章探讨了不同模型和方法在这些方面的主要差异，尤其是如何处理分区函数和采样。

Result: 通过这种统一的视角，文章不仅解释了现有的机器学习方法，还组织它们进入一个统一的框架中，提出了新的算法和组合能量函数的方法，以便在推理期间结合学习模型。

Conclusion: 文章总结称，通过在推理和学习方法中保持一致性，同时改变能源函数来创造新的模型和组合现有模型，可以充分利用能量模型带来的统一框架，并探索更多创新的机会。

Abstract: Energy-based models (EBMs) define a distribution through a scalar energy, $p_θ(x)\propto\exp(-E_θ(x))$, and are usually presented as one model family among many. This tutorial has a broader goal: beyond introducing EBMs, it uses them as a single lens that connects the different aspects of machine learning. Any EBM is specified by three choices, the energy, which determines what the model represents; the inference procedure, which samples from or optimizes the energy; and the learning method, which fits the energy to data. We first develop these components, including sampling, variational inference, and learning by maximum likelihood, noise contrastive estimation, pseudolikelihood, score matching, and adversarial training, and show that the learning methods share one contrastive structure. We then show that discriminative models, generative models such as GANs, normalizing flows, diffusion and autoregressive models, representation learning methods such as contrastive learning, word2vec, BERT, denoising auto-encoders, and diffusion representations, reinforcement learning with EBM policies and world models, inverse reinforcement learning, and planning, as well as inference-time computation with verifiers, are all EBMs that differ in these three choices, mostly in how they handle the partition function and sampling. This view explains existing methods, organizes them into a common framework, and suggests new algorithms by replacing one component while keeping the others, with composition of energies as a principled way to combine learned models at inference time.

</details>


### [293] [Precision on Demand: Escalating Weight Bits at Uncertain Decoding Steps in a Small Local Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14418)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Logesh L,Aliyas Ahmad Misri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了一种局部解码方法，通过存储8位权重并只在低精度输出分布不确定时才进行8位级别的重计算，来降低量化成本。验证推测在相似成本模型下能达到零KL散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的局部解码方法通常会固定一个权重精度，而该研究探索了一个新的策略，能够在保持模型性能的同时减少存储成本。

Method: 该研究通过存储8位权重并在必要时进行8位级别的重计算来降低量化成本。该方法在验证集上通过设定熵阈值或顶级概率差来判断是否需要重计算，从而在确保模型准确性的情况下显著降低存储要求。

Result: 实验结果显示，在GSM8K基准测试的150个真实问题中，该方法能达到62.7%的准确率，略低于将所有权重以8位表示的方案。同时，该方法的运行成本仅为后者的87%，表明能够在保持性能的同时显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 研究发现，通过合理的权重分配和仅在必要的时候进行高精度计算，可以在保证模型性能的同时大幅降低量化成本。

Abstract: Local decoding usually fixes one weight precision for the whole generation. We study a different allocation: store 8-bit weights, decode with their top 4 bits, and redo a step at 8 bits only when the low-precision output distribution looks uncertain (entropy above a threshold or a small top-two margin). Quantization is simulated, the model is Qwen3-0.6B, and the benchmark is GSM8K. Cost is counted in weight bytes read per token. On 36724 replayed steps the entropy of the low pass ranks argmax flips with AUC 0.93. At a 15% escalation budget it catches 78% of flips and the margin gate 94%, against the 15% expected for a random gate of equal rate (a computed expectation, not a run). In 150 live problems all-high scores 64.0% and all-low 55.3% (McNemar p=0.053). An entropy gate with a 16% escalation rate scores 62.7% at 0.87 of the all-high weight bytes. Its paired difference is -1.3 (-8.0 to +5.3) points to all-high and +7.3 (+0.0 to +14.7) points to all-low (p=0.080), so the live data do not separate the gate from either end. Verified speculation reaches zero KL at similar bytes in the same cost model. A live random control, a thinking-mode phase rule, a second model and live KL were not run.

</details>


### [294] [Evaluating LLM-Generated World Models for Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14419)
*Souhaila Djaffal,Amna Jamal,Gagana Madhugiri Devaraja,Anidipta Pal,Jobst Heitzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了五种LLM生成部分可观测随机博弈形式世界模型的能力，并在机械有效性、参考结构一致性和语义标准三个维度上进行了评估。结果表明，机械有效性虽是必要条件，但不足以保证模型准确反映决策问题的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自然语言生成技术的发展，研究如何从开放语言环境中构建世界模型成为了一个关键问题，尤其是在多智能体系统中，多种可能的形式化方案都可能是合理的。因此，本文旨在探索大型语言模型(Large Language Models，LLM)生成能够支持规划、验证和安全推理的形式世界模型的能力。

Method: 本文通过建立一个包含44个场景的基准测试集来评估LLM生成的模型。这些场景涵盖了游戏、情境以及文化三个方面的设置，并从机械有效性、参考结构一致性和语义标准三个方面来评估生成的模型。

Result: 研究结果显示，在机械审计严格的‘参考支持游戏’轨道中，134个生成的世界模型通过了机械审计，但是平均不变性结构F1为0.366，仅有28个模型完全匹配了撰写的参考。在‘文化’轨道中，明确命名国家显著改变了大多数LLM生成的结构表示，而自动化语义评分的变化却微乎其微。

Conclusion: 本文的研究证明，机械有效性虽然是生成世界模型的必要条件，但不足以确保下游神经符号推理和规划使用相同的模型结构。

Abstract: Formal world models can support planning, verification, and safety reasoning, but constructing them from open-ended natural-language situations is difficult, particularly in multi-agent settings where several formalizations may be plausible. We study whether five LLMs can generate such world models as partially observable stochastic games and whether mechanical validity is sufficient to establish that the resulting model faithfully represents the intended decision problem. We introduce a benchmark of 44 scenarios spanning game, situational, and cultural settings and evaluate generated world models along three dimensions: mechanical validity, reference-relative structural agreement, and semantic criteria. Across three empirical tracks, we analyze 410 first-pass generations. In the reference-backed game track, 134/135 generated world models pass a strict mechanical audit, yet mean permutation-invariant structural F1 is only 0.366, with just 28/134 mechanically valid models exactly matching the authored reference. In the cultural track, explicitly naming the country changes the generated structural representation for most LLMs while producing only small changes in automated semantic scores. These results show that executability is a necessary but insufficient criterion for LLM-generated world models: models that are mechanically valid can still differ substantially in the structure used by downstream neuro-symbolic reasoning and planning.

</details>


### [295] [Re-Search: Dissecting LLM Ideation Through a Two-Player Questioning Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14487)
*Muhan Gao,Hangxiao Zhu,Xinqi Su,Yu Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究理念是迭代的，现有评估方式未能揭示这一过程，该论文提出了一种名为Re-Search的方法，通过200篇ICML 2026论文测试六种大型语言模型（LLMs），发现大部分进展发生在最初的30个问题中，瓶颈在于生成能力而非判断能力，模型难以自行产生好想法或问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的模型评估方式偏向于评估模型的执行能力，未能反映其在研究理念探索过程中的动态变化，这不利于深入理解LLMs在研究领域中的潜力和限制。

Method: Re-Search方法设计了一种类似‘Twenty Questions’的问题形式，给定一个已发布论文的研究问题，模型需要通过提问来恢复该论文的核心思想。这种方法通过给模型提问并接收二进制反馈追踪其想法演变的过程。

Result: 大多数研究进展在最初的30个问题中就已经完成，之后模型的表现趋于停滞；分析表明，生成能力而非判断能力是瓶颈。

Conclusion: 研究表明，应扩大搜索范围，引入外部想法和问题，而不只是单个模型的生成能力提升，以克服当前的瓶颈，促进研究理念的探索和发展。

Abstract: Research ideation is iterative: researchers refine their ideas step by step based on feedback. Yet existing evaluations obscure this search process, making it difficult to understand how LLMs explore the space of research ideas and what limits their progress. We introduce Re-Search, which adapts the format of Twenty Questions to research ideation: given only the research problem of a published paper, a model must rediscover the paper's core idea by asking yes-or-no questions. Built from 200 ICML 2026 papers, Re-Search tracks how a model's idea evolves as it asks questions and receives binary oracle feedback, isolating ideation from execution. Across six recent LLMs, nearly all progress happens within the first 30 of 100 questions, which largely decide how the game ends; after that, most models plateau. Through controlled interventions on the candidate questions and the hypothesis space, we find that the bottleneck is generation, not judgment: models recognize a good idea or a good question when one is offered, but often cannot produce it on their own. We further analyze the ideation traces and find that models fail by either over-exploiting one direction or over-exploring too many. These findings point to concrete design principles for autoresearch systems: since models can judge candidates they cannot produce, enriching the search with externally sourced ideas and questions may be more effective than improving any single model's generation ability.

</details>


### [296] [ReTrace: Distilling Tool-Using Agents into Planner-Free Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14492)
*Kaiyuan Hou,Yang Ping Kuo,Indranil Bhattacharya,Rohith Nama,Arushi Gupta,Carlos D. Castillo,Prathap Ramachandra,Xiaofan Jiang,Francesco Carbone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReTrace 是一种通过将标准操作程序（SOP）和执行日志转换为不依赖于 LLM 的可重用工作流的技术，从而提高自动化流程的效率和降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前通过大型语言模型（LLM）实现业务过程自动化存在延迟和成本问题，ReTrace 通过收集并编译每个步骤的条件，将其转换为一种可执行的工作流规则文档来解决这些问题。

Method: ReTrace 从 SOP 和运行日志中提取每个步骤背后的条件，将其合并成紧凑的规则文档，并生成可执行的工作流。该过程的最后一个阶段依赖于平台，因此规则文档可以被编译以适应不同的编排平台。

Result: 在我们的生产平台上的79个流程中，ReTrace 工作流达到了93.8%的任务完成率，比采用代理方法降低了24%的成本。在 τ^2-Bench 上取得 75.8% 的任务成功率（代理的方法为 80.6%），运行时间缩短至1.3秒（代理的方法为170秒）。通过三轮修复训练失误，可以将 k=2 个工作流的成功率从 66.7% 提高到 81.2%。而在 SOP-Bench 上，没有修复训练误差的 ReTrace 仅达到 80.6% 的任务完成率，通过修复训练错误，该方法提高了至 87.8%，且运行时间降低了。

Conclusion: ReTrace 通过使用规则文档而非基于大型语言模型的代理，从理论上讲，不仅减少了延迟，还降低了成本，并且在某些特定流程中表现优于传统的基于代理的方法，特别是在经过修复训练后。

Abstract: Business process automation runs the same Standard Operating Procedure (SOP) again and again on new inputs. Large Language Model (LLM) agents can follow an SOP written in natural language, but they call an LLM to choose every step of every run, which adds latency and cost at scale. We present ReTrace, which turns an SOP and a few logged agent runs into a reusable workflow in which code, not an LLM, selects the next step. The agent's reasoning, the explanation it writes with each tool call, often states why it took an action; the tool calls alone do not. ReTrace extracts the condition behind each step, merges these conditions across runs into a compact rule document, and generates an executable workflow from it. Only this last stage depends on the platform, so rule documents can be compiled for different orchestration platforms. On $79$ procedures from our production platform, ReTrace workflows reach $93.8\%$ completion, against $96.8\%$ for the agent, at $24\%$ lower cost per task. On $τ^2$-Bench, they reach $75.8\%$ task success (agent: $80.6\%$) and run in $1.3$s instead of $170$s; three rounds of repair on training failures lift $k{=}2$ workflows from $66.7\%$ to $81.2\%$. On eight SOP-Bench procedures, ReTrace without repair trails few-shot generation ($80.6\%$ vs. $83.6\%$), but repair from labeled training failures lifts it to $87.8\%$ at $0.40$s per task, compared to $85.5\%$ in $46.6$s for the agent.

</details>
